### [1] [Circuit-aware protection against burst errors in lattice surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14583)
*Jaebum Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在模拟的晶格手术CNOT中，将保护（gap engineering）放置在每次合并时被测量的校验类型对应的辅助比特上，相比深几何带布局可将突发错误导致的超额风险降低约38%，并以更少的受保护辅助比特达到浅带参考风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模量子计算需抵抗同时扰动多个超导量子比特的高能冲击。gap engineering可抑制此类冲击引起的弛豫，但只能保护部分比特，因此必须决定保护位置。晶格手术过程中测量随patch的合并/分裂而变化，不同辅助比特与时间边界会被暴露于突发错误，故需研究如何依据逻辑电路的测量结构来安排保护。

Method: 针对模拟的晶格手术CNOT，比较三种静态保护布局，并控制受保护辅助比特数量及X:Z校验类型分配相同。距离9下，在相关背景匹配、且不向解码器提供冲击参数的条件下，对比"保护每次合并所测校验类型"的typed布局与深几何带布局；分析时间边界处的report error与辅助比特故障传播至数据比特的相互作用。

Result: 距离9时，typed布局相对深几何带使突发诱导的超额风险降低38(+6/-5)%；并以估计少49(+2/-14)个受保护辅助比特达到浅带参考风险。距离13时，在oracle decoding下优势不再明显，且五轮突发上限会逆转浅带排序。

Conclusion: 逻辑电路的测量结构可指导保护布局的选择，但该结论限于所测试的模拟解码器与突发持续时间范围；结论随距离与突发长度上限而改变。

Abstract: Large-scale quantum computing will require logical operations protected against high-energy impacts that disturb many superconducting qubits at once. Gap engineering suppresses relaxation induced by these impacts, but protecting only a subset of qubits requires choosing where that protection is placed. During lattice surgery, measurements change as patches merge and split, exposing different ancillas and temporal boundaries to burst errors. Here we compare three static protection layouts for a simulated lattice-surgery CNOT, matching the numbers of protected ancillas and the X:Z check-type splits. At distance 9, protecting the check type measured during each merge reduces burst-induced excess risk by $38^{+6}_{-5}\%$ relative to a deep geometric band under correlated background matching, without giving the decoder the impact parameters. We attribute this difference to report errors at temporal boundaries and ancilla faults propagating to data, which interact to increase the excess risk of a shallow band. We also find that the typed layout reaches the shallow-band reference risk with an estimated $49^{+2}_{-14}$ fewer protected ancillas. At distance 13, the advantage is not resolved under oracle decoding, and a five-round burst cap reverses the shallow-band ordering. These results indicate how the measurements of a logical circuit can guide protection placement within the simulated decoder and burst-duration regimes tested here.

</details>


### [2] [Controlled preparation and unconditional transport on a fluctuating quantum graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14588)
*Xianbin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在指定方形候选格点上构建单探针模型，证明在给定相干图基态下可实现无条件到达概率≥5/8的下界，并在局部误差预算下保持≥9/16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lieb–Robinson界只限制量子信息传播速度，不能保证指定输入可被制备并以可观概率传输；在边涨落的量子图上，还需控制图相干性、有限边界与制备误差，才能把谱传播与无条件读出联系起来。

Method: 采用单探针模型与预设方形候选格点，组合广义Rokhsar–Kivelson母体、谱约化、绝热制备与局域动力学估计；假设已提供相干图基态，构造有限源波包与跳跃斜坡，将有布洛赫群速度严格为正的孤立支填充；并用精确算术证书及小系统数值诊断佐证。

Result: 在足够大的有限开放方形指定接收区，证明无条件到达概率至少5/8；在对局域联合态与哈密顿量误差给出显式界后，下界保持9/16，且无需全局多体态接近性或对图测量的后选择。

Conclusion: 结果为涨落量子网络中的输运与读出提供模型特定基准，把传播机制与可观测下界相连，并给出保持该下界所需的局域误差预算，区分“抑制不良影响”与“保证信号送达”；资源估计保守、假设图基态供应，且不确立无背景几何或引力动力学。

Abstract: Lieb--Robinson bounds limit the spread of quantum information, but do not establish that a prescribed input can be prepared and transmitted with appreciable probability. For quantum graphs with fluctuating edges, connecting spectral propagation to unconditional readout also requires control of graph coherence, finite boundaries, and preparation errors. We address this problem in a one-probe model on a prescribed square candidate lattice, combining a generalized Rokhsar--Kivelson parent, spectral reduction, adiabatic preparation, and local dynamical estimates. Assuming a supplied coherent graph ground state, we construct a finite-source wave packet and a hopping ramp that populate an isolated branch with strictly positive group velocity. We prove an unconditional arrival probability of at least $5/8$ in a specified receiver region of a sufficiently large finite open square. Explicit bounds on local joint-state and Hamiltonian errors retain a lower probability of $9/16$, without requiring global many-body state closeness or postselection on graph measurements. Exact arithmetic certificates and a separate small-system numerical diagnostic support the quantitative analysis. The construction provides a model-specific benchmark for transport and readout in fluctuating quantum networks, linking a propagation mechanism to an observable lower bound and identifying the local error budgets required to preserve it. It distinguishes suppression of unwanted influence from guaranteed signal delivery, a useful separation for quantum-network and emergent-geometry models. The resource estimates are conservative, graph-state supply is assumed, and the result does not establish background-free geometry or gravitational dynamics.

</details>


### [3] [Probing Krylov Dynamics in Driven Many-Body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14611)
*András Grabarits,Adolfo del Campo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出时间依赖多体Krylov问题的精确闭式解，适用于标度不变与旋转不变系统，并将Krylov复杂度与可实验测量的物理量（云团宽度、集体自旋进动）直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Krylov复杂度是刻画算符增长、信息扰乱与量子混沌的核心指标，但标准Krylov构造假设生成元（Hamiltonian）固定，而实验室量子系统通常被时变驱动，导致原有框架失效，从而阻碍了Krylov复杂度的实验研究。

Method: 对当前量子模拟与控制平台中实现的标度不变和旋转不变系统，精确且以闭式求解时间依赖的多体Krylov问题；建立将驱动动力学映射到相互正交Krylov子空间的精确关系；并将构造推广到Liouville空间以求解时间依赖的密度矩阵Krylov问题。

Result: 得到仅由初始Hamiltonian谱性质与驱动schedule决定的闭式spread complexity；在标度不变量子气体（驱动Lieb-Liniger模型、Tonks-Girardeau气体、Calogero-Sutherland模型）中复杂度由原子云团宽度及其变化率决定，可通过原位与飞行时间成像测量；在旋转不变自旋系统（Haldane-Shastry、中心自旋模型）中由集体自旋进动主导，可在囚禁离子、腔QED与电路QED平台探测读出。

Conclusion: 本文解决了时变驱动下Krylov复杂度不可定义的问题，使初始热态的Krylov动力学具备实验可达性，并为当前量子模拟平台提供了直接可测量的复杂度探针。

Abstract: Krylov complexity has become a central probe of operator growth, scrambling, and quantum chaos. Yet, a basic fact hinders its experimental study: laboratory quantum systems are routinely driven in time, whereas the Krylov construction assumes a fixed generator and breaks down once the Hamiltonian varies. Here, we solve the time-dependent many-body Krylov problem exactly and in closed form for scale- and rotation-invariant systems realized in current quantum-simulation and control platforms. We find exact relations that map the driven dynamics onto mutually orthogonal Krylov subspaces and yield a closed-form spread complexity, determined solely by the spectral properties of the initial Hamiltonian and the driving schedule. The construction extends to Liouville space and solves the time-dependent density-matrix Krylov problem, which makes the Krylov dynamics of initial thermal states experimentally accessible. In scale-invariant quantum gases, such as the driven Lieb-Liniger model, the Tonks-Girardeau gas, and the Calogero-Sutherland model, the complexity is set by the width of the atomic cloud and its rate of change, directly accessible via in situ and time-of-flight imaging. In rotation-invariant spin systems such as the Haldane-Shastry and central-spin models, it is governed by collective spin precession that can be probed and read out in trapped-ion, cavity-QED, and circuit-QED platforms.

</details>


### [4] [Squeezed Light Generation by Cooper Pair Recombination in a Superconductor-Semiconductor Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14622)
*Sima Buchbinder,Avi Koriat,Alex Hayat*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论证明在紧凑的电驱动超导体-半导体混合结构中，利用库珀对复合并发生的光-物质非线性相互作用可产生压缩光，其压缩强度正比于超导能隙平方与器件长度，并随偏压增大而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩光产生方法通常依赖体积较大的光学装置或复杂泵浦方案，难以实现紧凑集成。作者希望探索一种电泵浦、可集成化的压缩光产生机制。

Method: 基于二阶微扰理论构建有效哈密顿量，将相互作用的电子部分表示为光子耦合强度系数，从而建立描述库珀对复合对光子态正交压缩效应的模型；分析压缩强度与超导能隙、器件长度及外加电压的依赖关系。

Result: 压缩强度与超导能隙的平方及器件长度成正比，并随外加电压增大而提高；在实际器件长度、材料与偏压条件下，于临界温度以下工作时可获得与现有压缩光产生方法相当的压缩度。

Conclusion: 该理论方案表明电泵浦压缩光产生具有可行性，为在紧凑集成结构中实现光压缩铺平了道路。

Abstract: We theoretically demonstrate squeezed light generation in a compact, electrically driven device utilizing the nonlinear light-matter interaction of Cooper-pair recombination in a hybrid superconductorsemiconductor structure. On the basis of second-order perturbation theory, we developed an effective Hamiltonian which incorporates the electronic component of the interaction as a photon coupling strength coefficient, enabling a model to describe the quadrature squeezing effect of Cooper-pair recombination on the photonic state. We find the squeezing strength to be proportional to the superconducting energy gap squared and device length, as well as increasing with applied voltage. For practical device length, materials and voltage bias, we calculated squeezing values comparable to those of existing squeezed light generation methods when operating below the critical temperature. This theoretically developed concept shows viability for electrically pumped squeezed light generation and can pave the way towards realization of squeezing in compact integrated structures.

</details>


### [5] [Towards Distributed Quantum Computing: Quantum Gate Teleportation via Satellite-Distributed Entanglement under Atmospheric Depolarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14623)
*Artur Czerwinski,Jakub J. Borkowski,Maciej Wiśniewski,Saeed Haddadi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究以卫星分发纠缠光子对为资源的量子门隐形传态，用于在分布式量子计算机间实现非局域CNOT门，并量化大气信道噪声对门保真度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星可实现远距离量子处理单元（QPU）间的纠缠分发，但大气传播等物理层效应如何传导到门级性能尚缺乏系统刻画；本文旨在连接卫星光信道的物理层建模与分布式量子计算的门级性能。

Method: 构建最小四比特协议：两个网络比特提供纠缠资源、两个电路比特位于不同处理器；以隐形传态方式实现非局域CNOT门。将引力场中的大气传播效应建模为有效两比特退极化信道，其参数由大气与光学量（Fried参数、接收孔径等）决定；以理想CNOT输出态与含噪输出态间的保真度为性能指标。

Result: 保真度由分布式纠缠的质量主导，并联合依赖于大气相干性（Fried参数）、孔径尺寸与有效退极化强度。

Conclusion: 卫星使能的分布式量子计算中，门级保真度可直接追溯到大气光信道的相干性与接收光学参数，为物理层设计与门级性能评估建立关联。

Abstract: We investigate quantum gate teleportation as a primitive for satellite-enabled distributed quantum computing. In the considered architecture, a satellite distributes entangled photon pairs to two optical ground stations, which transfer the shared quantum resource to remote quantum processing units. This entanglement is then used to implement a nonlocal controlled-NOT gate between two circuit qubits located in different processors. We formulate the minimal four-qubit protocol, consisting of two network qubits that provide the entangled resource and two circuit qubits on which the distributed gate is performed. Atmospheric propagation effects in the gravitational field are modeled through an effective two-qubit depolarizing channel whose parameters depend on atmospheric and optical quantities, including the Fried parameter and receiver aperture. The performance of the protocol is quantified by the fidelity between the ideal CNOT output state and the state produced by noisy quantum gate teleportation. Our results show that the fidelity is governed by the quality of the distributed entanglement and depends jointly on atmospheric coherence, aperture size, and effective depolarization strength. These findings connect physical-layer modeling of satellite optical channels with gate-level performance in distributed quantum computing.

</details>


### [6] [The memory cost of sequential quantum state preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14652)
*Jian Li,Patryk Lipka-Bartosik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将"量子设备逐个制备系统所需内存"形式化为量子资源理论下的顺序制备与集体制备内存成本比较问题，证明二者渐近每输出内存成本相同，但顺序制备可能需要无界更大内存，并刻画该差距对物理限制（能量守恒幺正、噪声操作、固定重复交互）的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子设备在逐次（顺序）处理系统流时的内存开销是实际与理论基础问题；资源理论可形式化"内存与所处理系统流允许如何交互"的物理限制，但顺序与集体制备的内存成本关系及其对限制条件的依赖尚不清晰。

Method: 以量子资源理论为框架，在一般性假设下比较顺序制备与集体制备的渐近内存成本（每输出内存维度）；针对能量守恒幺正，求从能量本征态制备纯态的最优内存维度；针对噪声操作构造分离实例；并分析重复固定交互的顺序协议中内存维度与成功轮数的关系，与量子催化建立等价性。

Result: 一般假设下顺序与集体制备的渐近每输出内存成本相同；但顺序制备内存可无界大于集体制备。能量守恒情形下给出制备纯态的最优内存维度，顺序所需额外内存要么有界、要么随输出数对数增长。噪声操作下存在变换：集体制备任意多独立输出仅需一个辅助量子比特，而顺序制备无任何固定维度内存可完成。重复固定交互的顺序协议中，d维内存若可成功d²轮则可永久成功，且该顺序制备等价于量子催化。

Conclusion: 顺序与集体制备在渐近内存率上不可区分，但有限情形下存在由物理限制调节的本质差距；内存维度、成功轮数与催化之间存在结构性联系（d²轮饱和、"一次成功即永久成功"），为量子设备内存需求提供了资源理论刻画。

Abstract: How much memory does a quantum device need to prepare systems one at a time? We address this question through the lens of quantum resource theories, which formalize physical restrictions on how the memory may interact with the stream of systems it processes. By comparing sequential with collective preparation, we show that under general assumptions both have the same asymptotic memory cost per output. Still, sequential preparation can require an unboundedly larger memory than collective one, and we investigate how this gap depends on the physical restrictions on the allowed interactions. For energy-conserving unitaries, we determine both optimal memory dimensions needed for preparing pure states from an energy eigenstate, and find that the extra memory required sequentially either remains bounded or grows logarithmically with the number of outputs. Under noisy operations, we reveal transformations for which one auxiliary qubit suffices for preparing arbitrarily many independent outputs collectively, while no memory of fixed dimension can deliver them sequentially. Finally, for sequential protocols that repeat a fixed interaction, we show that a $d$-dimensional memory that succeeds for $d^2$ rounds will succeed forever, and that such a sequential preparation is equivalent to quantum catalysis.

</details>


### [7] [Quantum synchronization in XX chains under relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14657)
*Kübra Sümer,Ege Yiğit Aldırmaz,Steve Campbell,Barış Çakmak,Göktuğ Karpat*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究非均匀比特XX链在多局域振幅阻尼噪声下的稳定同步，给出暗模能量为被阻尼位点隔开的各无阻尼段共享本征值的谱匹配条件：当且仅当各段恰好共享一个非零本征值时出现边缘比特的通用稳定同步，并证明单暗模情形下参与同步振荡的任意比特对保持恒定非零纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Çakmak等，PRA 114, L030202 2026）仅处理均匀链的稳定同步问题，缺乏对位点依赖频率与交换耦合的非均匀链的系统理解，本文旨在将结论推广到非均匀情形并刻画同步出现的谱条件。

Method: 在含位点依赖频率与交换耦合的XX链上引入多局域振幅阻尼噪声，通过分析暗模（无衰减子空间）结构，将暗模能量刻画为被阻尼位点分隔的各无阻尼段本征值的公共部分；以谱匹配条件判定同步，结合耦合强度对振荡相对幅度的影响，并解析计算渐近两两concurrence。

Result: 证明边缘比特的通用稳定同步当且仅当各无阻尼段恰好共享一个非零本征值，此时暗模支撑上的所有比特同步且锁定常数与初态制备无关；给出解析示例表明非均匀性可产生均匀链中不存在的同步，并将依赖初态的锁定转变为通用同步；在单暗模情形下，任意参与同步振荡的比特对保持非零恒定concurrence。

Conclusion: 非均匀XX链的稳定同步由暗模的谱匹配条件完全决定，耦合到阻尼位点的强度调控振荡幅度，非均匀性可作为同步的资源；同步与纠缠在单暗模机制下共存，为噪声环境下多比特量子网络的同步与纠缠协同设计提供判据。

Abstract: We study stable synchronization in qubit XX chains with site-dependent frequencies and exchange couplings under multi-local amplitude damping noise, extending the uniform-chain results of B. Çakmak et al., [Phys. Rev. A 114, L030202 2026] in several directions. We establish that the dark-mode energies are precisely the eigenvalues shared by all undamped segments separated by the damped sites. Generic stable synchronization of the edge qubits occurs if and only if these segments share exactly one eigenvalue and this eigenvalue is nonzero, in which case every qubit on the support of the corresponding dark mode is synchronized with locking constants independent of the initial preparation. This spectral matching condition identifies the Hamiltonian parameters that support synchronization, while the couplings to the damped sites control the relative oscillation amplitudes. We present analytical examples which show how inhomogeneity can create synchronization absent in uniform chains and turn preparation-dependent locking into generic synchronization. We also calculate the asymptotic pairwise concurrence and prove that, in the single-dark-mode regime, every pair participating in synchronized oscillations remains entangled with a nonzero, constant concurrence.

</details>


### [8] [Faithful filtering of constrained quantum records: Local limits and collective advantage from loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14695)
*Qingtian Miao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 弱探测可在不揭示自旋共同取向的情况下记录其是否对齐；论文求出了读取这些探测以宣布对齐子空间投影时的最优成功概率，并证明联合读取可超越所有基于局域操作与经典通信（LOCC）与可分离测量的策略，其条件由记录间的不可约依赖关系刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱测量/弱探测能够只记录"相邻自旋是否对齐"这一相对信息而隐藏共同取向，从而在读取探测时实现对未知自旋态向对齐子空间的保持相干性的投影。核心问题是：这种投影在分别读取与联合读取探测时各自能以多大概率成功？联合读取相对所有局域策略是否有本质增益，其成立条件是什么？

Method: 在二元纯直积记录且假阳性为零的设定下，推导所有可分离测量（进而所有LOCC）上的精确最优成功概率，并给出直观表述——即单侧局域见证排除所有替代假设的概率，且同时局域测量即可达到该值。进一步分析联合读取：对连通简单图上的成对宇称记录，以及一般二元线性记录，分别给出联合读取优于任何局域策略的充要条件（图中存在自环；至少三条记录间的不可约依赖），并分析弱记录极限下效率标度的变化。

It is the probability that one-sided local witnesses exclude every alternative, and simultaneous local measurements attain it. On a triangle the efficiency becomes linear rather than quadratic in the witness probability, and a three-pulse coherent-light receiver captures this gain with a single click. Abstract: Quantum reinforcement learning (QRL) has emerged as a promising approach for controlling quantum systems and accelerating learning tasks with quantum resources. However, most existing QRL formulations still retain a classical reinforcement learning interface: states and actions are often encoded in computational bases and rewards are extracted through measurements. This interface is restrictive for genuinely quantum environments: choosing a measurement can discard entanglement information, while repeated intermediate measurements collapse the evolving state and require costly re-preparation. This motivates a fully QRL framework in which the agent, environment, rewards, and return accumulation are all quantum. In this paper, we take a step toward this goal and introduce a new QRL framework in which both the policy and the environment are modeled by conditional quantum channels. We then propose a quantum policy-gradient algorithm for parameterized conditional quantum channels with a trainable control basis. We also derive finite- and infinite-horizon gradient estimators using a new parameter-shift rule. This rule is needed because policy parameters are shared across repeated interaction blocks and the infinite-horizon objective is not a finite circuit; rather than shifting every occurrence and evaluating the whole trajectory, our estimator shifts only one sampled policy block. Sampling this block from a geometric distribution yields an unbiased gradient estimator under bounded rewards. Finally, we establish convergence of the resulting stochastic gradient-ascent procedure to stationary points under standard smoothness and step-size assumptions.

Abstract: Standard Bell inequalities rely on measurement independence, the assumption that measurement settings are perfectly decoupled from the source of correlations. Abstract: Cellular communication is done over an imperfectly predictable multimode environment, where the sender only knows about the distribution of possible intermode coupling and scattering a signal faces. It may thus seem surprising that such communication is efficient. Telatar proved that the sender's lack of exact knowledge about the channel, on average, does not significantly hinder multimode communication. As the number $N$ of modes increases at fixed signal-to-noise ratio, the average capacity of a classical multimode fading channel grows linearly in $N$, like that of $N$ decoupled single-mode channels. We study the quantum analogue in a Haar-random passive bosonic network and prove a similar scaling for unassisted quantum information transmission. Suppose that the sender drives a fixed fraction of the input modes and the receiver collects more than half of the output modes. Then the quantum capacity of such channels at zero temperature grows linearly with the number of modes at fixed photon number per mode, just like the classical capacity grows with the number of antennas. At nonzero temperature, the Haar-averaged coherent information of the isotropic thermal input bounds the capacity from below, and its value per mode converges to an integral against Wachter's law. We numerically identify positive achievable rates in models motivated by cryogenic microwave networks and room-temperature multimode fibers.

Abstract: Quantum cloud providers can read the circuits they receive and their outputs, yet methods for hiding a circuit are usually evaluated on the circuit as written, before compilation optimises it. We compare three ObfusQate schemes (inverse, composite and delayed gates), ECQCO and Q-MaskCut under one protocol that compiles every protected circuit at optimisation level 3 before measuring it; the goal is to keep the provider from learning the user's algorithm and answer. Q-MaskCut is one example combination of existing techniques: a one-time-pad key, decoy wires, circuit cutting with a random rotation pair across each cut, and Pauli twirling with dynamical-decoupling masking after compilation. Q-MaskCut hides the output (mean total variation distance without the key 0.94), and without noise, with exact fragment probabilities, it decodes exactly. Its compiled normGED of 0.88 comes mostly from the masking, which a second compilation removes, leaving 0.42 from the key, decoys and padlocks. With noise, its fidelity is comparable to the original's; it needs 7 to 91 fragment variants per circuit in the simulations (7 to 61 in a run on ibm_marrakesh) and a classical reconstruction. Protection should therefore be evaluated on the compiled circuit and again after the provider recompiles it.

Abstract: Quantum error-correcting codes trade decoding reliability against the resources needed to measure their checks. We formulate Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) code design at fixed encoding resources as a decoder-aware multiobjective problem. The objectives are failure probability under sector-wise degenerate maximum-likelihood decoding and check weight, the minimum total weight of an independent pure-type generator basis. For independent bit- and phase-flip noise with perfect syndrome extraction, deterministic truncation bounds support selection under a failure-rate constraint. At a per-sector error probability of 0.01, the bounds certify that a code encoding two logical qubits into 40 physical qubits meets a failure-rate target of 0.001 at check weight 139. The lower bound of every cheaper candidate in the corresponding (40,2) main menu exceeds this target. In paired searches sharing one annealing kernel and equal query budgets, decoder-aware guidance gives significantly higher mean final hypervolume than distance guidance in all eight tested configurations under the reported Holm correction. Against distance-multiplicity guidance, five configurations show significant gains and three do not, including one with a negative mean difference. All arms are scored in common truncated-reliability and normalized-check-weight coordinates. The framework connects reliability-check-weight trade-offs to bound-certified selection within evaluated CSS pools.

The simulator corresponds to a local hidden variable model, so we use Bell inequalities to prove the simulator can't also support Hadamard gates, CZ gates, or S gates. We note that CSS circuits appear ideal for benchmarking quantum error correction in the presence of atom loss errors.

Result: 证明（1）固定强度下局域逻辑泄漏阻碍渐近忠实恢复，沿逻辑算符支撑的累积迫使所需测量强度随码距对数增长；（2）该障碍可局域消除：在足够弱局域随机误差下，仅增强消失比例校验即可恢复可恢复性，体区保持有限测量强度；（3）严格三维去禁闭相中，增强管道使宏观分离的代表渐近等价并实现相同受控逻辑作用；二维中即使两种编码均可恢复，代表仍可不等价。

Abstract: Stabilizer measurements provide a direct route to preparing quantum error-correcting codes and long-range entangled states. In realistic implementations, however, finite measurement strength can coherently deform the encoding map away from the projective limit. Certain nonlocal signatures associated with long-range order can survive such a deformation, but this alone does not guarantee that the resulting state protects encoded quantum information. We show that, at fixed strength, local logical leakage obstructs asymptotically faithful recovery; along logical operators' supports, its accumulation forces the relevant measurement strength to grow logarithmically with code distance. We then prove that this obstruction can be removed locally: for fixed toric registers under sufficiently weak local-stochastic errors, enhancing only a vanishing fraction of checks near selected logical supports restores recovery while the bulk remains at finite measurement strength. With recoverability restored, we prove a further dimensional distinction in logical operations. In the strict three-dimensional deconfined phase, suitable enhanced tubes restore the equivalence of macroscopically separated representatives, so either representative asymptotically implements the same controlled logical action on a fixed encoding. In two dimensions, recoverability alone does not restore this equivalence: the representatives can remain inequivalent even when both associated encodings are recoverable.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 优化测量的组合方式也能反过来指导哪些项应一起测量，为在现有及近期设备上保留局部测量的前提下降低变分量子模拟的采样开销提供了途径。

Abstract: Estimating molecular energies on quantum computers requires repeated measurements of many Pauli terms, making sampling a major resource cost. Measurement design chooses which compatible terms are measured together and allocates a finite shot budget among bases to minimize the variance of an energy estimator. We introduce variance-guided measurement group construction (VGMC), which reduces variance beyond optimization on existing measurement bases by changing which Hamiltonian terms share measurement shots. After including every Hamiltonian term measurable in each existing basis, we jointly optimize estimator coefficients and shot allocations to separate inefficient use of these measurements from limitations of their measurement combinations. We compare a candidate's benefit with the cost of withdrawing shots from existing bases, and use the resulting scores to guide greedy construction of qubit-wise commuting groups followed by joint re-optimization. With exact covariances, VGMC outperformed the state-of-the-art measurement design methods across the molecular benchmarks where it introduced additional measurement bases, achieving variance reductions of 18.19%-23.38%. The gains persisted with approximate covariances, and simulations with finite shots indicated lower estimated shot requirements near chemical accuracy. These results show that optimizing how measurements are combined can also guide which terms are measured together, offering a route to reducing the sampling overhead of variational quantum simulation while retaining local measurements on current and near-term devices.

</details>


### [17] [QSim-Arena: an evolving developer for quantum simulation advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15000)
*Jue Xu,Yue Wang,Qi Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 QSim-Arena 框架：以 AI 智能体构建"闭环验证+基准测试"与"开放探索"相结合的研究体系，将量子优势评估转化为可演化、可复现的研究流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子动力学模拟中的实用优势存在争议：声称的量子优势常被更强的经典方法迅速缩小或推翻，而量子与经典双方的进展速度超出期刊发表周期，导致评估结论滞后、可比性差、难以复现。

Method: 将哈密顿量、初态及演化态/可观测期望值的精度目标作为显式规范；AI 智能体把已发表技术转化为可执行组件，复现报告结果并在多类哈密顿量族上做基准测试；同时独立实现并交叉校验竞争性经典方法，基于严格误差界与经验校准给出端到端量子资源估计的可信基线；在闭环之外，智能体还重组经典模拟技术，并利用文献知识协同设计容错量子线路。

Result: 初步应用通过整合已有技术改进了基线资源估计；框架实现了对量子与经典方法的统一复现、交叉校验与资源评估。

Conclusion: QSim-Arena 将量子优势评估视为持续演化、可复现的研究过程，能够随算法、误差分析与硬件进展而更新，从而避免静态、易过时的结论。

Abstract: Where practical advantage emerges in quantum dynamics simulation remains a central debate in quantum computing. Claimed advantages can be rapidly narrowed or overturned by stronger classical methods, while progress on both sides can outpace the journal publication cycle. We present QSim-Arena, a research framework in which AI agents combine a closed loop of verification and benchmarking with open exploration. Hamiltonians, initial states and accuracy targets for evolved states or observable expectation values provide explicit specifications for evaluating simulation methods. Agents translate published techniques into executable components, reproduce reported results and benchmark their performance across diverse Hamiltonian families. Competing classical methods are implemented and independently crosschecked, providing credible baselines for end-to-end quantum resource estimates based on rigorous error bounds and empirical calibration. Alongside this closed loop, agents recombine classical simulation techniques and co-design fault-tolerant quantum circuits using knowledge extracted from the literature. Initial applications improve baseline resource estimates through the integration of established techniques. QSim-Arena thus treats quantum advantage assessment as an evolving, reproducible research process that can respond to advances in algorithms, error analysis and hardware.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKP态高度非高斯，在存在光子损耗时难以控制；需要明确理想控制路径，并评估不同实验门集实现这些路径的能力。

Abstract: Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) states are highly non-Gaussian, making their control challenging, particularly in the presence of photon loss. Here, we use the brachistochrone, the fastest path at bounded speed, to construct ideal nonlinear Hamiltonians for state preparation and logical gates, and compare it with the photon-optimal path. We find that the two paths nearly coincide for state preparation but differ markedly for logical operations, namely the brachistochrone preserves the encoded qubit during a bit flip, whereas the photon-optimal path flips a known state through the vacuum. We then introduce the tangential capacity to quantify how effectively gate sets track these paths. Using this analysis, we find that conditional squeezing achieves the highest capacity, preparing GKP states in 824 ns with a fidelity of 0.99 and a minimum closeness to the path of 0.90, compared with 0.98 and 0.51 for conditional displacements and 0.83 and 0.19 for SNAP gates.

</details>

<details>

Abstract: Two level systems (TLSs) are a major source of loss in superconducting devices, limiting the performance of qubits, detectors, and resonators. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Allen等人此前建立了弱振荡场探测的传感时间下界（"Grover-Heisenberg极限"）并给出达到该极限的协议，但该协议依赖辅助量子比特来运行Grover算法子程序，因此单传感量子比特能否实现相同标度的问题悬而未决。

Abstract: We consider a fundamental quantum sensing task: detecting weak oscillating fields with unknown strength and frequency. Recent work by Allen et al. established a fundamental lower bound on the required sensing time, which they named the "Grover-Heisenberg limit", and gave a protocol attaining this limit. The protocol relies on auxiliary qubits, which are used to run Grover's algorithm as a subroutine, leaving open the question of whether a single sensing qubit can achieve the same scaling.

Our protocol's sensing time grows only as the square root of the number of candidate frequencies. We prove that this square-root dependence, which we refer to as the "discrete Grover-Heisenberg limit", is optimal in the worst case.

Abstract: Quantum phase estimation is a key component of many quantum algorithms. Recently, high-confidence, tapered QPE (tQPE) has been introduced, significantly reducing the number of ancilla qubits necessary for a given failure probability, $ε$. We consider tQPE with $p = l+m$ ancillas, where $l$ represents the number of ancillas necessary to estimate a phase to a given resolution and $m$ represents the number of ''confidence'' qubits added to ensure a desired success probability. With tQPE the failure probability decays double exponentially in $m$, implying that only a few confidence qubits suffice for virtually any problem of interest. A key challenge for tQPE is the construction of the quantum circuit for preparing the taper state on the ancilla register. Here, we develop scalable tensor network methods (previous work was limited to ($l + m = 24$)) giving highly optimized state preparation circuits for optimally concentrated DPSS (Slepian) tapers. We provide an accompanying database of resource-efficient state preparation circuits for $l = 1, \dots, 50$ and $m = 1, \dots, 4$. Our circuits are shallow enough for a demonstration on near-term devices for small values of $l$. We run tQPE circuits for a 1-qubit system initialized in the ground state on Quantinuum's H2-2 quantum computer, demonstrating a $3\times$ to $18\times$ reduction in failure probability for $l=1,\ldots,10$ on a noisy device.

We also show that SMILES writing order can change the output, that a run succeeds with probability at most $2^{-n}$ for $n$ bonds, and that a cup-free design avoids this cost.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出 $g$-分支实现（最多 $g$ 个神谕并行），将 $T$ 项分到 $g$ 个寄存器；在 $k$-local 原子神谕模型下分析 select 深度，给出紧界 $Θ(C+\log(g\max\{n,m\}))$，其中 $C$ 为分箱 makespan、$m$ 为最大箱。通过双向归约把工作量下界对应到经典多处理器调度（每个实现诱导一个 $g$ 机调度，构造可实现任意分箱），并允许按 McNaughton 规则拆分神谕以达工作量下界 $\sum_j d_j/g$。比较两种选择器实现：稠密（Möttönen）方案与 one-hot 方案，在空间与深度之间权衡。

Result: 对 $g\ge2$，select 深度紧界为 $Θ(C+\log(g\max\{n,m\}))$；附加的对数路由项是光锥代价，独立于选择器编码，且对寄存器复用、测量、重置和隐形传态稳健；宽度至少为 $gn$。工作量下界可达 $\sum_j d_j/g$（允许拆分神谕时）。稠密 Möttönen 选择器内存紧致、保持标准 LCU 占用，但复用控制带来 $\log\log(m+1)$ 的 makespan 因子；one-hot 选择器在 $T$ 个选择比特下深度紧致。二者无法同时达到两个目标。标准的 $1/α$ 次归一化与成功概率不变，端点为顺序 LCU（$g=1$）与全并行实现（$g=T$）。

Abstract: An LCU block-encoding of $A=\sum_jα_j U_j$ runs the $U_j$ in series on one register, so its select depth is the total work $\sum_j d_j$. Depth was the one block-encoding constraint with no knob: grouping the $T$ terms into $g$ registers that run in parallel gives it one, and the binning is the knob. For a $g$-branched realisation (up to $g$ oracles in parallel) the select depth is tight at $Θ(C+\log(g\,\max\{n,m\}))$ for $g\ge2$, with $C$ the binning makespan and $m$ the largest bin, in the $k$-local atomic-oracle model. The work floor is a two-way reduction to classical multiprocessor scheduling: every realisation \emph{induces} a $g$-machine schedule, and the construction realises any binning, so an optimal binning attains it. The additive logarithmic routing term is a light-cone cost, independent of the selector encoding and robust to register reuse, measurement, reset, and teleportation; width is at least $gn$. The standard $1/α$ subnormalisation and success probability are unchanged; the endpoints are sequential LCU ($g{=}1$) and the fully parallel realisation ($g{=}T$). Allowing the oracles to be split (McNaughton's rule) reaches the work floor $\sum_j d_j/g$. Two selector realisations trade space for depth: a dense (Möttönen) one is memory-tight at the standard LCU footprint but its multiplexed controls cost a $\log\log(m{+}1)$ factor on the makespan; a one-hot one is depth-tight at $T$ selector qubits. Neither reaches both at once.

Abstract: We introduce the multi-head quantum neural network (QNN), a quantum module compatible with noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices that can replace transformer blocks inside pre-trained large language models (LLMs) with little loss in accuracy and with far fewer parameters than the blocks it replaces. Abstract: We derive and compare device-independent witnesses of genuine multipartite entanglement (GME) and genuine multipartite nonlocality (GMNL) for GHZ states and trees of Bell pairs. A binary-outcome family using four global measurement contexts has the tight biseparable bound independent of the number of parties. We distinguish these GME bounds from Svetlichny-hybrid bounds, which permit arbitrary communication within either side of a bipartition. For GHZ states, we also give a $2N$-context GMNL witness and compare it with MABK and Svetlichny tests. For Bell-pair trees, sums of CHSH and chained inequalities certify GME and GMNL, respectively, with explicitly specified input protocols and multi-bit local outputs. Separately, we prove the sharp four-context network bound $I_λ\leq2$ for every $N\geq3$ and $λ\geq2$, and bound the MABK scores of quantum networks assembled from bipartite sources. Abstract: This study evaluates quantum classical hybrid recurrent architectures for financial time-series forecasting: a Quantum Long Short-Term Memory (QLSTM) network and Quantum Reservoir Computing (QRC) with lag-based inputs. Classical lag vectors are encoded into quantum states via amplitude encoding, which compactly represents normalized observations but requires nontrivial state preparation; the QLSTM gates and the QRC reservoir are implemented with parameterized quantum circuits, while classical optimizers train the interface and readout layers. On a monthly revenue series extended to 1056 months by a Gaussian-process--hidden-Markov generator, with a fixed chronological 70/15/15 split and one-step-ahead forecasts, we compare both models against a full-size LSTM, a classical control matched in parameter budget and interface architecture, a small plain LSTM, and classical reservoir computing, over ten seeds and against persistence, seasonal-naive and SARIMA benchmark forecasts. The QLSTM, with 2,105 parameters, is statistically indistinguishable from an LSTM 126 times larger, but equally indistinguishable from the matched classical control and from a plain LSTM of the same budget, so its parameter efficiency cannot be attributed to the quantum circuits. The quantum reservoir is worse than a classical reservoir of equal feature dimension in all ten seeds and worse than SARIMA, whereas all recurrent models and both classical reservoirs improve on SARIMA by about 2\% in RMSE. In a 24-month rolling-origin backtest the classical LSTMs degrade more slowly than the QLSTM. We find no evidence of a quantum accuracy or parameter-efficiency advantage in this low-dimensional setting.

Method: 在有限模玻色Fock空间中，取G_0与H为湮灭与产生算子的自伴二次多项式，分析其定义域结构；分别在G_0≤0以及G_0与H的对易子满足某种半有界（half bound）假设两种条件下证明定义域等式。

Abstract: Solving large-scale Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problems with Time Windows (CVRPTW) remains challenging because strict operational constraints and scale limit both exact and heuristic methods. QAOA experiments establish a current hardware boundary for reliable constraint satisfaction at subproblems of up to 4 customers (16 qubits). Diagnostic results identify assignment-level time-window tightness, rather than local route optimization, as the dominant scalability bottleneck. The results demonstrate a practical near-term role for hybrid classical-quantum optimization while establishing empirical limits for current quantum hardware.

Abstract: The Mpemba effect, in which an initially hotter system relaxes faster than a colder one, has been widely studied in both classical and quantum systems, yet identifying the specific effects of quantization requires a consistent comparison with the corresponding classical dynamics. Starting from the Caldeira-Leggett model, we establish a quantum-classical correspondence for thermal relaxation. We find that the locality of classical energy diffusion forbids the Mpemba effect in single-well and symmetric double-well potentials. Conversely, quantum dynamics allows for direct transitions between nonadjacent energy levels, overcoming the constraints of classical local energy diffusion and enabling the Mpemba effect in these potentials. In the semiclassical limit $\hbar\to0$, the nonlocal transitions become increasingly suppressed, restoring the classical constraints. Our findings reveal that quantization fundamentally unlocks anomalous relaxation pathways, rather than merely modifying relaxation rates.

</details>

Abstract: Whether quantum correlations give non-adiabatic annealing a scaling advantage remains an open question. This fundamental separation reveals an intrinsic Stokes--Anti-Stokes asymmetry, yielding distinct transverse diffraction patterns for energy loss and thermal energy gain, and establishing a generalized detailed-balance relation that governs the radiative reduction of the apparent temperature in nanoscale electron thermometry. Abstract: Electronic structure calculations become computationally expensive on classical computers as electron correlation increases. Quantum computing offers a promising alternative through hybrid quantum-classical Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) on Noisy-Intermediate Scale Quantum (NISQ) devices. The choice of ansatz plays a pivotal role in the performance and scalability of the VQE, yet selecting an optimal ansatz remains challenging. Existing approaches including chemically inspired ansatz, adaptive schemes and Hardware Efficient Ansätze (HEA) lack a unified comparison. This gap makes the scalability and trade-offs of each method unclear for varying molecular sizes.
  This work presents a systematic benchmarking study comparing Unitary Couples Cluster Singles and Doubles Ansatz (UCCSD), fermionic ADAPT-VQE, BasicPCA HEA, and Excitation Preserving HEA across molecules of varying size like $H_2$, $LiH$, $N_2$. ADAPT-VQE achieves accuracy comparable to UCCSD with lower resource overhead, but becomes impractical for modest system sizes ($\sim$10 electrons) due to increasing circuit complexity. In contrast, HEA reduces hardware costs but requires more parameters and sacrifices accuracy, with performance strongly dependent on molecular size. The Excitation Preserving ansatz remains below chemical accuracy for $H_2$ and $LiH$, and fails for $N_2$, underscoring the need to balance expressibility and trainability. Overall, these results highlight key trade-offs between accuracy and resource efficiency across VQE ansätze, emphasizing the need to improve ADAPT-VQE scalability and HEA accuracy while addressing optimization challenges for near-term quantum hardware.

Abstract: Vibrational strong coupling within a few molecules has recently been demonstrated in infrared nanocavities at room temperature, opening new alternatives to explore chemical reactions assisted by the confined electromagnetic vacuum. The coupled system is driven by a monochromatic infrared laser that vibrationally excites the molecule directly or injects photons into the nanocavity field. Abstract: Noise degrades quantum coherence and limits the precision of quantum sensors. Quantum control can counteract these effects, but existing strategies typically assume that the noise is known. In practice, noise is often only partially characterized, so its parameters must be estimated jointly with the Hamiltonian parameters of interest. Here we show that uncertainty in the noise parameters can destroy otherwise attainable Heisenberg scaling, even when coherence is preserved. For general Markovian dynamics under arbitrary quantum control, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for each Hamiltonian parameter to retain this scaling and determine its optimal asymptotic precision when the condition holds. When the condition fails, we propose a protocol that uses a suitable noisy reference system to restore this scaling, and prove that a reference insensitive to the Hamiltonian parameters but subject to an independent copy of the sensor's noise recovers the optimal precision attainable with known noise. We design the algorithm for the mature fault-tolerant era and estimate its cost in logical qubits and Toffoli depth from a classically routine benchmark to targets where established methods are unreliable. Result: 在真实观测数据导出的博弈中，在所测交互尺度上未发现已分辨的量子—经典差距；通过控制流动性竞争与跨股票重新定价等变量，可识别支持量子优势的机制区间，并构造显式策略。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Long-range entangling gates shorten variational state preparation, but they also accelerate operator spreading across the register, linking nonlocality to a presumed noise fragility. For Heisenberg chains we prove that two faults with identical extensive support can incur either bounded or extensive energy damage, in the exact ratio $N/2$. A phase-matched longitudinal halo remains energetically dark until ignited by transverse catalysts that break a symmetry of the remote register, namely spatially nonuniform single-qubit rotations or the pair-flip component of two-qubit $XX/YY$ gates, whereas uniform rotations and magnetization-conserving exchange gates leave it strictly dark. In noisy training with full-width long-range $ZZ$ gates, suppressing either catalyst alone leaves circuits trapped on an ignited plateau sustained by remote $XY$ couplings, whereas suppressing both eliminates the plateau, achieves minimal fragility despite larger fault operators, and halves the dephasing error. Training is driven by a race between catalyst pruning and gradient force balance, as annealing the transverse catalysts out of trapped circuits rescues every trajectory while annealing the entire longitudinal sector does not. Combined with the gate-count exposure $λ=pK$, a single fragility measurement accurately predicts the noise-induced energy shift across circuit architectures and noise strengths, reducing ansatz design to a trade-off between coherent expressivity and gate count.

Abstract: Quantum Control is the art of steering the fragile dynamics of a quantum system in a precise way toward a desired final state or transformation. It is a cornerstone of many quantum technology applications and at the same time allows us to shed light on the fundamental connection between the energy structure and the time dynamics of quantum systems at the heart of some of the most interesting phenomena of quantum mechanics. In this chapter on quantum control, we introduce the control problem, go through basic error sources and methods to suppress these errors and then discuss state-of-the-art techniques to solve the control problem, like adiabatic methods, quantum optimal control and methods of machine learning.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子开关是不定因果序的典型实例，光子实验已实现其物理实现，但任何"每个操作只按确定顺序调用一次"的量子电路都无法描述它。作者希望量化：标准电路模型需要多少次黑盒调用才能重现量子开关的行为，从而判定这种因果结构是否带来可证明的查询复杂度优势。

Method: 将黑盒操作视为作用于 d 维系统的任意信道，允许电路使用经典前馈与无限制辅助量子存储；证明三个下界结果：(1) 最坏情况下无任何有限调用次数可精确模拟；(2) 以半钻石范数度量，误差低于 1/2 的近似需 Ω(d^{1/4}) 次调用（随量子比特数指数增长）；(3) 存在某单个比特，量子开关几次使用即可提取，而普通电路需指数多次调用。另外构造噪声刻画对比任务：实际噪声为固定未知基下的退相位，对照理论退极化模型，两者纠缠保真度与平均门保真度相同。

Result: 证明最坏情况下精确模拟不可能；近似模拟查询下界为 Ω(d^{1/4})；存在指数分离的单比特提取任务；在噪声刻画中，固定噪声强度下量子开关用与 d 无关的设备使用次数即可区分退相位与退极化信道，而电路模型需 Ω(d^{1/16}) 次调用。

Conclusion: 量子开关在噪声刻画这一与实际量子纠错相关的任务上具有可证明的查询优势，且纠缠/平均门保真度等指标本身无法区分两类噪声，说明不定因果序带来的优势具有实际意义；作者呼吁未来将该类优势推广到更实用的计算任务。

Abstract: The quantum switch applies two operations in a coherent superposition of their causal orders and is a simple example of indefinite causal order. No quantum circuit that uses each operation once in a definite order can even describe it, yet photonic experiments have demonstrated its physical realization. We ask how many calls to the black-box operations a quantum computer in the circuit model needs to reproduce the switch. If one of the black boxes is known to be unitary, three calls suffice. For general channels on a $d$-dimensional system, we prove three results. In all three, circuits may use classical feed-forward and unlimited ancillary quantum memory. First, no finite number of calls suffices for exact simulation in the worst case. Second, approximation to any fixed error below $1/2$, measured in half the diamond norm, requires $Ω(d^{1/4})$ calls, exponential in the number of qubits. Third, an ordinary circuit can require exponentially many calls to obtain a single bit that the switch extracts with a few uses. We consider a device whose actual noise is dephasing in a fixed unknown basis and test it against a theoretical depolarizing model. The two channels have the same entanglement and average gate fidelities, so these figures alone do not distinguish them. At fixed noise strength, the quantum switch tells them apart with a number of device uses independent of $d$, whereas the circuit model requires $Ω(d^{1/16})$ calls. Noise characterization is needed for quantum error correction, and this gives a provable query advantage of the switch in a task relevant to practical quantum computing. We invite future work that extends such advantages to more practical computing tasks.

</details>

Aaron Lott,Leonhard Neuhaus,Lee J. O'Riordan,Muhammad Osama,Masoud Mohseni,Blake R. Johnson,Yasuko Eckert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于容错量子计算中经典解码器的综述，以统一的分类体系（解码器级层次 vs. 码级层次、级联预解码、窗口化/流式/分布式实现、软硬信息协调、硬件平台）梳理解码算法、执行策略与码构造的性能与权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算要求经典解码器跟上综合征数据流，并向量子控制系统提供纠正信息与逻辑测量结果；在规模化条件下需兼顾高吞吐与低端到端延迟。现有文献在术语、评估条件和系统集成上较为分散，缺乏共享的设计与比较基础。

Method: 采用综述与分类学方法：区分解码器级层次（多解码阶段或多资源协作解码单一码）与码级层次（码构造本身引入多解码层级）；先回顾单码分层解码器及其级联预解码与窗口化、流式、分布式实现；再分析分层码与级联码，重点关注通过硬判决、软可靠度信息或综合征信息实现跨码级解码协调；最后考察量子比特模态对解码工作负载与时间约束的影响，以及CPU、GPU、FPGA、ASIC和低温平台等解码平台的满足情况。

Result: 在已报道的评估条件下，系统比较了各类解码方案与实现策略的性能与权衡，厘清了文献间的关联；同时指出现有工作在性能报告规范性和系统集成方面的空白。

Conclusion: 本文通过评估设计权衡，为量子纠错设计探索提供共同基础，并为面向实用化量子纠错的后续研究指明方向（改进性能报告与系统集成）。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires classical decoders that can keep up with syndrome data streams and supply correction information and logical measurement outcomes to the quantum control system. Meeting these requirements at scale requires high throughput while controlling the end-to-end latency from readout to usable correction. In this paper, we organize decoding algorithms, execution strategies, and code constructions using common classifications and examine their performance and trade-offs under the reported evaluation conditions. We distinguish decoder-level hierarchy, where multiple decoding stages or resources cooperate to decode a single code, from code-level hierarchy, where the code construction itself introduces multiple decoding levels. We first review single-code hierarchical decoders and their cascaded predecoding and windowed, streaming, and distributed implementations. We then study hierarchical and concatenated codes, focusing on how decoding is coordinated across code levels through hard decisions, soft reliability information, or syndrome information. We also examine how qubit modalities shape decoding workloads and timing requirements, and review how different decoding platforms meet these requirements, including central processing units (CPUs), graphics processing units (GPUs), field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs), application-specific integrated circuits (ASICs), and cryogenic platforms. By assessing design trade-offs, this paper clarifies connections across the literature and provides a shared foundation for QEC design exploration. It also identifies gaps in performance reporting and system integration to guide future research toward practical quantum error correction.

</details>

*Xulong Tang,Dionysios Diamantopoulos,Tristan Müller,Apo Kayi,Nelson Mimura Gonzalez,Muhammad Osama,Seetharami Seelam,Haralampos Pozidis,Blake R. Johnson,Yasuko Eckert*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要低逻辑错误率的 QEC 解码器，且受实时运行约束，因此依赖启发式解码与大规模仿真评估；多 GPU 是自然扩展手段，但朴素地按 syndrome 批次分发会带来主机端预处理与结果收集开销，限制扩展性。

Method: 在 Pêlagos 的 Relay-BP 解码器基础上引入三项优化：进程隔离式编排、并行主机端预处理、共享内存输出聚合；在单 gross（[[144,12,12]]）与双 gross（[[288,12,18]]）码路线上，于多 GPU 环境（8× AMD Instinct MI300X、8× MI355X）测量端到端与纯 GPU 解码吞吐。

Result: 单 gross 电路在 R=50 下，MI300X 与 MI355X 分别取得 5.68× 与 6.23× 端到端加速；双 gross 电路分别为 6.92× 与 7.01×。按纯 GPU 解码时间计，双 gross 加速比为 7.58× 与 7.45×，接近线性扩展。该实现已部署于 ORNL 基于 MI355X 的 Lux 超级计算机。

Conclusion: 通过高效多 GPU 解码可显著加速大规模 QEC 仿真，端到端测量验证了实用性，并为 AI+HPC 基础设施提供了具体量子计算用例。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires quantum error-correction (QEC) decoders that achieve low logical error rates under real-time operating constraints. These constraints motivate heuristic decoding algorithms whose error-correction performance must be evaluated through large-scale simulation. High-throughput decoding accelerates this evaluation and the exploration of decoder parameters. Using multiple graphics processing units (GPUs) is a natural way to scale these simulations, but naively distributing syndrome batches introduces host-side preprocessing and output-collection overheads that limit scaling. To address these limitations, we extend the Pêlagos relay belief-propagation (Relay-BP) decoder with process-isolated orchestration, parallel host preprocessing, and shared-memory output. We evaluate both one-gross ($[[144,12,12]]$) and two-gross ($[[288,12,18]]$) codes in a multi-GPU environment. Specifically, for the one-gross circuit at $R=50$, eight AMD Instinct MI300X GPUs and eight AMD Instinct MI355X GPUs achieve optimized end-to-end speedups of $5.68\times$ and $6.23\times$, respectively. For the two-gross circuit, the corresponding speedups are $6.92\times$ and $7.01\times$. When throughput is calculated using GPU decoding time, the corresponding two-gross speedups are $7.58\times$ and $7.45\times$, indicating near-linear GPU scaling. We deploy Pêlagos on Lux, Oak Ridge National Laboratory's AMD Instinct MI355X-based supercomputer, as a pathfinding application for high-throughput QEC simulation. This deployment provides a concrete quantum-computing use case for the AI + HPC infrastructure supporting the Genesis Mission's scientific-discovery goals. These end-to-end measurements demonstrate a practical path to accelerating large-scale QEC simulation through efficient multi-GPU decoding.

</details>


### [61] [Collective information backflow from a local population record: Identifiability and learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16334)
*Yifan Shi,Pengju Chen,Duan Wang,Quanzhen Ding,Ting Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究复合量子系统中"信息回流"这一由成对演化态定义的诊断量，与实验可得的稀疏布居记录之间的失配问题，并区分"已知热浴族内的充分性"与"短噪声记录下的均匀推断不可能性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息回流（trace-distance backflow）需要通过态对演化来定义，但实验往往只提供有限的布居数记录，二者存在结构性错配；作者希望厘清在何种条件下稀疏布居数据足以（或不足以）推断回流诊断量。

Method: 考虑两个全同量子比特集体耦合到具有指数关联函数的真空库；用理想亮态布居轨迹确定与有限时间迹距离回流诊断相关的热浴参数组合（在固定时间栅格与态对库上评估），并利用失谐符号未分辨不改变该诊断的性质，采用支持向量学习或直接物理拟合由稀疏布居预测诊断量；进一步用双假设界分析有限测量统计下的限制。

Result: 失谐符号未分辨不改变诊断量；稀疏布居记录可在已知热浴族内较准确地预测回流诊断。但有限测量统计带来不同约束：存在两个热浴，其短布居记录相似而回流显著不同；在某六时刻协议、总计600次测量的条件下，任何基于这些观测的估计器在其中一个指定热浴处的期望绝对误差至少约为0.13。

Conclusion: 布居记录在已知热浴族内可以是充分的，但不允许从短时含噪记录中进行一致精确的推断；信息回流诊断的可推断性依赖于热浴先验知识与测量统计量，二者需分开讨论。

Abstract: Information backflow in a composite quantum system is defined through pairs of evolving states, whereas an experiment may provide only a restricted population record. We study this mismatch for two identical qubits coupled collectively to a vacuum reservoir with an exponential correlation function. An ideal bright-state population trajectory determines the bath-parameter combinations relevant to a finite-time trace-distance backflow diagnostic of the full two-qubit channel, evaluated on a fixed time grid and state-pair bank. The unresolved detuning sign leaves this diagnostic unchanged. This relation allows sparse population records to predict the diagnostic using support-vector learning or direct physical fitting. Finite measurement statistics impose a different restriction: a two-hypothesis bound identifies reservoirs with similar short population records but substantially different backflow. For one six-time protocol with 600 total shots, every estimator using those observations has expected absolute error at least approximately 0.13 at one of two specified reservoirs. Thus a population can be sufficient within a known bath family without permitting uniformly accurate inference from a short noisy record.

</details>


### [62] [Training quantum neural networks one job at a time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16365)
*Muhammad Faryad,Rabab Alomairy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将变分量子算法通过云队列访问量子处理器的训练视为"执行效率"问题：在相同作业数(job)与每作业采样数(shots)下比较优化器填满每个作业的方式。提出以参数移位模板(parameter-shift stencil)作为天然批次，证明其梯度误差随每作业shots反比下降，而SPSA存在无法通过增加shots消除的误差下限。仿真中24–48参数时批量参数移位所需作业数为SPSA的1/5–1/2；在ibm_fez上8量子比特测试精度明显更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子处理器需通过云队列访问，每次往返成本高昂，而单次往返可携带多个电路；因此优化器不仅决定"每shot学到多少"，还决定"每作业学到多少"。作者希望把混合量子-经典训练重新表述为执行效率问题，即如何从每次昂贵的量子处理器交互中获取最大优化进展，并把优化器设计与开销摊销、加速器资源利用效率联系起来。

Method: 在量子神经网络分类器上，固定作业数与每作业shots，仅改变每个作业的填充方式。先直接移植在QAOA上有效的批量模式搜索，模型参数增至数十个后失效；转而保留其核心原则——在作业发送前固定一批参数向量。对由Pauli旋转构成的电路，自然批次为参数移位模板(parameter-shift stencil)；并进一步提出结构感知的模板搜索(structure-aware stencil search)，将同类行视为正弦函数读取。与SPSA对比梯度误差随shots的标度行为，并在仿真(两噪声水平)与硬件(ibm_fez，6/8量子比特，三次交错运行)上验证。

Result: 理论：参数移位梯度误差随每作业shots反比下降；SPSA(同时扰动法)误差存在无法通过增加shots消除的下限。仿真：24–48参数时批量参数移位训练所需作业数为SPSA的1/5至1/2，该排序在两种噪声水平下均成立；结构感知模板搜索同样优于SPSA。硬件：在ibm_fez上6、8量子比特、三次交错运行中，批量优化器在50个作业的第4至第20个作业内即达到SPSA的最终训练损失；8量子比特时测试精度明显更高，6量子比特差异不显著。所有硬件数据可由原始shots重新推得。

Conclusion: 优化器的"每作业填充策略"是云访问量子处理器场景下的关键设计维度：固定批次的参数移位模板能随shots无下限地降低梯度误差，从而显著摊销通信开销、减少所需作业数。批量模板类方法相较SPSA在仿真与真实硬件上均展现更优的执行效率，但测试精度优势在电路规模较小时不显著。

Abstract: When a quantum processor is reached through a cloud queue, a variational algorithm pays for every round trip, and a single round trip can carry many circuits. The classical optimizer therefore decides how much is learned per job, not only per shot. We study this for a quantum neural network classifier. We compare optimizers that are given the same number of jobs and the same shots per job, and that differ only in how they fill each job. This comparison treats hybrid quantum-classical training as an execution-efficiency problem: how to obtain useful optimization progress from each costly interaction with a quantum processor. The resulting perspective connects optimizer design to overhead amortization and efficient use of accelerator resources. A direct port of a batched pattern search that worked for the quantum approximate optimization algorithm stalls once the model has a few dozen parameters. What does transfer is the principle behind it: fill each job with a batch of parameter vectors that is fixed before the job is sent. For circuits built from Pauli rotations the natural batch is the parameter-shift stencil. We show that its gradient error falls as the inverse of the shots per job, while the error of simultaneous-perturbation methods (SPSA) has a floor that no number of shots removes. In simulation, batched parameter-shift training needs between a fifth and a half of the jobs SPSA needs, for 24 to 48 parameters, and the ordering holds at two noise levels. A new structure-aware stencil search reads the same kind of rows as sinusoids and also beats SPSA. On IBM's ibm_fez processor, in three interleaved runs at six and eight qubits, the batched optimizer reached SPSA's final training loss within 4 to 20 of its 50 jobs. Its test accuracy was clearly higher at eight qubits; at six qubits the difference was not significant. All hardware numbers can be re-derived from the raw shots.

</details>

Abstract: We propose a theoretical scheme for generating and converting optical vortices in a coherently prepared three-level Λ-type atomic medium. An azimuthally varying relative phase between the two lower states imparts additional phase winding to the generated field, yielding l2=l1+m in the ideal model, where l1 is the input topological charge and m the coherence winding number. Thus, a Gaussian input can generate a vortex, while an incident vortex can be converted to a different orbital angular momentum (OAM) mode. Analytical results and numerical examples using hydrogen transition parameters illustrate both processes. Under resonant conditions, the model predicts strongly suppressed absorption during subsequent propagation as the bright-state component is attenuated and electromagnetically induced transparency (EIT) emerges. Spatial coherence is assumed as an initial condition; its detailed experimental preparation remains open. This scheme offers a potential framework for controlling vortex light through spatial atomic coherence.

</details>

*Ramon Jose C. Bagunu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将BB84窃听与检测的对抗建模为表格型Q学习窃听者与阈值自适应检测器之间的交替最佳响应博弈，发现双方动态在20轮内不收敛、可利用性波动与最佳响应方差同量级，隐蔽窗口在低噪声下始终开放；据此指出单轮检测结果无法认证学习型检测器，须从信息结构入手而非"学得更快"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作已证明强化学习窃听者可在QBER低于中止阈值且保留可用可蒸馏密钥的条件下截获BB84，形成"隐蔽窗口"。本文追问：同样具备自适应学习能力的检测器能否关闭该窗口，以及这种攻防学习动态是否收敛到可用策略。

Result: 20轮内动态未趋于稳定：可利用性不接近不动点，轮间波动幅度不衰减；两对照组显示双方波动与最佳响应过程的方差相当，即波动尺度由最佳响应方差决定而非收敛过程。行为具噪声依赖性：低噪声（实用QKD工况）下隐蔽窗口保持开放；中等噪声下检测器在平均意义上压制攻击者但博弈不收敛；高噪声下抑制主要来自信道而非学习策略。机制上，中等噪声出现显著反持续性，低噪声下与最佳响应噪声一致，支持"后手信息不对称"解释。

Conclusion: 学习型检测器无法凭任一单轮结果获得认证，其安全性评估需考虑对手持续自适应的动态性质；关闭隐蔽窗口的关键在于打破攻防双方的信息结构（如消除后手信息优势），而非单纯让检测器在适应速度上超过攻击者。

Abstract: In prior work we showed that a reinforcement-learning eavesdropper can intercept BB84 quantum key distribution while holding the quantum bit error rate below the abort threshold and keeping a usable distillable key. In this paper we ask whether an equally adaptive learning detector can close this stealth window. We cast the interaction as an alternating best-response game between a tabular eavesdropper and a tabular threshold-adapting detector. We measure exploitability, which is the detection rate and distillable key fraction achieved by a fresh attacker best-responding to the final detector. Across three learning rules (Q-Learning, SARSA, Double Q-Learning), three channel-noise levels, and a uniform false-alarm discipline, the dynamics do not settle out to twenty rounds. The exploitability does not approach a fixed point, and its round-to-round swings do not decay. Two controls, one retraining the attacker against a frozen defender and one retraining the defender against a frozen attacker, show that the swings on each side match the variance of the best-response procedure itself. The dynamics fluctuate at the scale of best-response variance rather than converging, with neither player settling to a fixed response. The behavior is noise-dependent, and the stealth window stays open at the low noise where practical QKD operates. The defender contains the attacker on average at intermediate noise without the contest settling, and at high noise the channel rather than the learned policy suppresses the attack. As a candidate mechanism we examine a last-mover information asymmetry, supported by significant anti-persistence at intermediate noise and consistent with best-response noise at low noise. The results suggest that a learned detector cannot be certified from any single round, and that closing the window calls for breaking the information structure rather than out-adapting the attacker.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grover算法的平方根加速优势会被噪声侵蚀，作者希望从解析角度定量回答"优势退化得多快"，并统一解释不同噪声模型下已有标度结果的来源。

Method: 1) 对每层门后作用于全寄存器的去极化信道，利用其与门对易性，将有噪态表示为无噪态乘几何衰减的相干权重；2) 对独立单比特误差，证明理想Grover算子使被推出二维搜索平面的振幅冻结，从而精确刻画单次误差效应；3) 大数据库极限下将平面内分量约化为含泄漏与退相干的Bloch型方程，推导比特翻转、相位翻转、去极化、振幅阻尼噪声的速率并求解；4) 对旋转角相干误差给出随深度线性展宽的成功概率带界。

Result: 全局去极化下Grover旋转速度不变、权重几何衰减、最佳停止点前移，平方根标度仅在累积噪声小时成立；单次误差中相位翻转在搜索早期危害最大、比特翻转在后期危害最大；局域噪声在主导阶等价于被比特数增强的全局信道，故每比特可容忍误差比数据库规模的逆平方根还多下降一个寄存器尺寸因子；相干角误差的成功概率带随深度线性变宽。

Conclusion: 论文以闭式结果统一复现了此前特定噪声模型的标度，并给出误差危害差异的机理解释：搜索平面外振幅被冻结、噪声表现为泄漏与退相干，因此不同噪声的破坏力取决于其驱动泄漏与退相干的速率及发生时机。

Abstract: Grover's algorithm searches an unstructured database with a number of oracle queries that grows only as the square root of its size. Noise erodes this advantage, and we ask, analytically, how quickly. When a depolarizing channel acts on the whole register after every gate layer, it commutes with the gates, and the noisy state is the noiseless one with a coherent weight that decays geometrically with depth. The Grover rotation is not slowed. Its weight decays, the best stopping point moves earlier, and the expected query cost keeps its square-root scaling only while the accumulated noise over the circuit stays small. For independent errors on the qubits we show that the ideal Grover operator freezes any amplitude pushed out of the two-dimensional search plane. This gives the exact effect of a single error: phase flips do most damage early in the search, bit flips late, and combined errors at any time. For large databases it also reduces the noisy search to Bloch-type equations for the part of the state that stays in the plane, in which the algorithm is a rotation and the noise appears as leakage and dephasing. We derive the rates for bit-flip, phase-flip, depolarizing, and amplitude-damping noise and solve the equations. Local noise acts, to leading order, like a global channel strengthened by the number of qubits, so the tolerable error per qubit falls faster than the inverse square root of the database size by one power of the register size. For coherent errors in the rotation angle we bound the success probability by a band that widens linearly with depth. Our results recover, in closed form, scalings found earlier for particular noise models, and they explain why some errors are more harmful than others.

</details>


### [66] [Probing the extreme quantum violation of the Leggett-Garg inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16401)
*Akarshit Baranwal,Vishnu Purushothaman,A. K. Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文批判性检讨了文献中声称量子理论可超越"时间Tsirelson界"(TTB)的论证，指出这些论证存在概念缺陷，更正后LG不等式的宏观实在论界达到代数最大值，故TTB并无违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LG不等式用于检验宏观实在论与量子理论的相容性，其最大量子违反通常被称为时间Tsirelson界(TTB)。已有研究（如采用破简并测量、幺正叠加的方案）声称TTB可被超越，这与Bell不等式Tsirelson界的性质形成反差，因此有必要澄清这些主张是否成立。

Method: 对Phys. Rev. Lett. 113, 050401 (2014)中破简并测量方案与Phys. Rev. Lett. 135, 220202 (2025)中幺正叠加方案的推理进行批判性分析，指出其概念缺陷，并在恰当处理这些概念问题后重新推导LG不等式的宏观实在论界。

Result: 这些声称的TTB违反论证存在概念缺陷；恰当修正后，LG不等式的宏观实在论界可达代数最大值，因此并不存在对TTB的违反。

Conclusion: 所提出的TTB违反依赖于超出标准LG框架的方案，因而与检验宏观实在论和量子理论不相容性无关；TTB作为量子上限的地位并未被推翻。

Abstract: The Leggett-Garg (LG) inequalities were formulated for testing the incompatibility between the classical worldview of macrorealism and quantum theory. By analogy with the Tsirelson bound for Bell inequalities, the maximal quantum violation of LG inequalities is commonly referred to as the temporal Tsirelson bound (TTB). Nevertheless, several studies have claimed that, unlike the Tsirelson bound, the TTB can be exceeded within quantum theory. In particular, by employing degeneracy-breaking measurement in [\href{ https://doi.org/10.1103/PhysRevLett.113.050401}{Phys. Rev. Lett. \textbf{113}, 050401 (2014)}] and by using the superposition of unitaries in [\href{https://doi.org/10.1103/vydp-9qqq}{Phys. Rev. Lett. \textbf{135}, 220202 (2025)}], it is argued that the TTB can be violated. In this work, we critically examine the reasoning underlying these purported TTB violations and show that the corresponding arguments suffer from conceptual shortcomings. We further show that once these conceptual issues are addressed appropriately, the macrorealist bound of LG inequality reaches the algebraic maximum, and hence no violation of the TTB is obtained. In particular, we demonstrate that the proposed TTB violations rely on frameworks that go beyond the standard LG framework, and therefore are of no relevance for testing the incompatibility between macrorealism and quantum theory.

</details>


### [67] [Engineering Bosonic Steady States through Fock-Space Currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16431)
*Yan Liu,Jiahua Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出统一的Fock空间电流工程框架，通过局域电流选择性增强目标Fock态布居，实现可自主稳定化的可调Fock态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fock态是量子技术的重要资源，但内在损耗使稳定化困难；现有稳定化方案多针对特定微观过程，缺乏统一图像。

Method: 构建统一的Fock空间"电流工程"框架，推导稳态电流平衡定律，分析布居背景随Fock数反比标度，并量化局域电流对目标态布居的选择性增强；结合完整量子模拟验证。

Conclusion: 为Fock态稳定化提供统一物理图像，并给出可迁移的设计原理，用于工程化可选择的玻色稳态。

Abstract: Fock states are valuable resources for quantum technologies, but intrinsic loss makes their stabilization challenging. Whereas existing stabilization schemes are typically formulated for specific microscopic processes, here we develop a unified Fock-space current-engineering framework for the autonomous stabilization of selectable Fock states. We derive a stationary current-balance law that predicts a population background scaling inversely with Fock number and quantifies how a local current selectively enhances the target Fock-state population. This law reveals how distinct microscopic implementations obey the same steady-state design rule, allowing different target states to be selected by moving a local transition without changing the overall stabilization architecture. Full quantum simulations further support these predictions and demonstrate the autonomous buildup of target-state populations from the vacuum. Our results provide a common physical picture of Fock-state stabilization and establish a transferable design principle for engineering selectable bosonic steady states.

</details>

*Suraj Vishwanath,Jonas Hawellek,Roberto Ferrara,Christian Deppe*

TL;DR: 在电路级对含稳定子量子纠错的第二代量子中继器进行仿真，比较Steane码与五量子比特码在不同噪声下的端到端性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子中继器需结合纠错以提升保真度，但码型选择与物理噪声机制的关系尚不明确，需在电路级评估不同纠错码的实际表现。

Method: 构建包含编码、症状提取、解码与纠缠交换的两段式中继链路模型，在电路级直接注入非相干噪声（去极化、退相干）与相干噪声（系统性过旋转），对比单错纠正的Steane码与五量子比特稳定子码，并考察混合噪声交叉效应。

Result: 相干噪声下Steane码优于五量子比特码；低噪声非相干场景中当大量物理比特受影响时五量子比特码端到端保真度更高；高噪声区不编码反而最优；混合噪声存在中等交叉效应。

Conclusion: 第二代量子中继器的码型选择须依据主导物理噪声机制适配，电路复杂度与硬件导向实现选项会影响相干噪声主导区性能，为中继器纠错设计提供依据。

Abstract: We present a circuit-level simulation of second-generation quantum repeaters with stabilizer-based quantum error correction. We model a two-segment repeater link including encoding, syndrome extraction, decoding, and entanglement swapping, and compare the performance of the single-error-correcting Steane and five-qubit stabilizer codes under different noise scenarios. To that extent, we consider noise sources with incoherent (depolarizing and dephasing) and coherent (systematic over-rotation) errors and incorporate them directly at the circuit level. While the Steane code outperforms the five-qubit code at coherent errors, the five-qubit code achieves higher end-to-end fidelities under incoherent noise in the low-noise regime when a large number of physical qubits is affected by it. At higher noise regimes, employing no code turns out to be the best option. In mixed noise scenarios, we observe moderate cross-effects between the noise classes regarding the error correction performance. We conclude that code selection in second-generation quantum repeaters must be adapted to the dominant physical noise mechanism and that circuit complexity and hardware-aware implementation options influence performance in coherent-error-dominated regimes, providing design insights for error correction in quantum repeaters.

</details>


### [69] [Learning Sparse Graph States with Local Pauli Measurements: Tight Nonadaptive Bounds and an Adaptive Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16456)
*Matteo De Francesco*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在每量子比特于 X/Y/Z 基下测量的设定中，识别 n 量子比特图态的图所需的最小副本数，证明非自适应学习器对 d 的二次依赖在 d^3 ln n ≤ n 时是必要的（Ω(d^2 log n)），但副本间的自适应性可突破该下界，在有对数直径且二分隐藏的连通二分 d-正则图上实现 O(d log^2 n / log log n)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ouyang 与 Tomamichel 证明 d-正则图需 O(d^2 log n) 副本、任意测量至少 Ω(d log n)，并留问：对 d 的二次依赖是否必要。本文旨在厘清该间隙究竟源于算法缺陷还是问题本身的内在下界。

Method: 核心观察：单次副本最多只揭示邻接矩阵某主子矩阵的二元零化度（binary nullity）。据此对非自适应学习器构造 Ω(d^2 log n) 下界（条件 d^3 ln n ≤ n，允许无限制经典解码器及常数比例错误边）。对随机完美匹配并集，用傅里叶估计控制该零化度；二分情形采用类型方法（method of types）。上界方面设计广度优先的自适应学习策略。

Result: 非自适应策略：即使解码器无限制、允许少量错误边，仍需 Ω(d^2 log n) 副本，说明二次依赖必然。二分 d-正则、对数直径、二分隐藏：非自适应需 Θ(d^2 log n)，而 BFS 自适应仅需 O(d log^2 n / log log n)；若公开二分结构，非自适应成本降至 Θ(d log n)。

Conclusion: 对 d 的二次依赖是非自适应学习的本质下界，而非算法不足；跨副本自适应性可显著突破该障碍，二分结构的可知性（隐藏 vs 公开）直接决定 Θ(d^2 log n) 与 Θ(d log n) 的差异。下界统一由单副本零化度信息量限制导出。

Abstract: We study how many copies of an unknown $n$-qubit graph state are needed to identify its graph when every qubit is measured in the $X$, $Y$ or $Z$ basis. Ouyang and Tomamichel showed that $O(d^2\log n)$ copies suffice for $d$-regular graphs, while any measurement needs $Ω(d\log n)$, and asked whether the quadratic dependence on $d$ is necessary. We show that it is for nonadaptive learners: if $d^3\ln n\le n$, every nonadaptive strategy needs $Ω(d^2\log n)$ copies, even with an unrestricted classical decoder and even if a small constant fraction of the edges may be reported wrongly. We then show that adaptivity between copies can beat this barrier. On connected bipartite $d$-regular graphs of logarithmic diameter whose bipartition is hidden, nonadaptive learners still need $Θ(d^2\log n)$ copies, but a breadth-first adaptive learner needs only $O(d\log^2 n/\log\log n)$; revealing the bipartition brings the nonadaptive cost down to $Θ(d\log n)$. The lower bounds rest on a simple fact: one copy reveals at most the binary nullity of a principal submatrix of the adjacency matrix. We control this nullity for random unions of perfect matchings through Fourier estimates and, in the bipartite case, a method-of-types argument.

</details>

Abstract: Krylov spread complexity quantifies how extensively a quantum system explores its Hilbert space during time evolution. These transformations are either symmetry operator of the Hamiltonian, or local $U(1)$ transformations of which the initial state is an eigenstate. The latter naturally arise as quasi-gauge transformations in chiral continuous-time quantum walks, to which the invariance properties are applied. We show that chiral phases in circulant Hamiltonians generically lift spectral degeneracies, leading to a discontinuous change in the long-time-averaged complexity. For a localized initial states and a nondegenerate spectrum, complexity is $\bar{C}=(D-1)/2$, where $D$ is the Hilbert-space dimension, independently of the degree of connectivity. A non-circulant quantum router illustrates the additional role of phase-dependent spectral weights. These examples show that Krylov complexity can change even when probability time-reversal symmetry is preserved, linking its response to chirality to spectral changes rather than directly to transport asymmetry.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: In the Mpemba effect, a state initially farther from stationarity overtakes one that starts closer. After one cycle in a monitored Clifford chain, an unencoded Bell-pair state has lower mean entanglement entropy than a scrambled reference prepared from fewer pairs. The gap grows linearly with chain length. This reversal is driven by logical-information exposure: a stabilizer state loses one ebit per independent logical parity resolved by the record. Scrambling within each half can spread this information over many sites while preserving every Schmidt coefficient. If the stationary entropy is subextensive, the unencoded reversal is a quantum Mpemba effect at large sizes. We further certify this effect at 128 and 256 qubits using stationary-entropy bounds.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a first-principles framework for analyzing photonic quantum communication in an atomic medium. Starting from a real-space quantization of the electromagnetic field coupled to an ensemble of two-level atoms, we derive an effective model for polarization-encoded single-photon states. We apply this framework to superdense coding to study communication efficiency and to the BB84 quantum key distribution protocol for communication security. For superdense coding, we evaluate the protocol capacity of the fixed Bell-state alphabet under erasure, dephasing, and depolarizing channels and show that it decreases monotonically with increasing atom-field coupling. For BB84, we formulate a modified protocol that discards erasure outcomes and uses the microscopic model to estimate the intrinsic loss rate.   <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: RLD界并非普遍紧：等号成立由半定可行性条件精确刻画，且信道RLD信息与最优渐近SLD信息每使用一次的比值可任意大。

Abstract: The channel right-logarithmic-derivative (RLD) Fisher information is a computable upper bound on the optimal asymptotic symmetric-logarithmic-derivative (SLD) Fisher information per channel use over causally ordered, parameter-independent strategies. The bound is not always attained, even if the Choi matrix is positive definite. We determine exactly when equality holds using a semidefinite feasibility condition involving only the Choi matrix and its first derivative. The condition allows a degenerate largest eigenvalue of the RLD matrix and rank-deficient Choi matrices with derivatives supported on their range. The original channel family may change rank nearby. For generalized dephasing channels with a positive-definite correlation matrix at the evaluation point, the condition becomes a system of linear equations for nonnegative certificate weights that sum to one. At a completely dephasing point we also calculate the optimal SLD information for every finite number of uses and show that a repeated, ancilla-free probe attains it, even against adaptive strategies. Explicit examples give an unbounded ratio of the channel RLD information to the optimal asymptotic SLD information per use, either as the dimension grows or at fixed qubit dimension as the smallest Choi eigenvalue tends to zero. We derive the equality criterion and its consequences from the asymptotic optimization of Zhou and Jiang.

</details>

Abstract: The identification of heavy-flavor jets, i.e. collimated streams of particles originating from the fragmentation of beauty and charm quarks produced in proton-proton collisions, represents one of the most challenging classification tasks in measurements at Large Hadron Collider experiments. In recent years, advanced machine learning techniques have been successfully employed to address this problem, while quantum machine learning has emerged as a promising alternative approach. However, the high dimensionality of the problem, typically involving hundreds of observables, raises an open question: whether the current generation of quantum computers, characterized by limited qubit count, can effectively tackle such complex tasks. In this work, a variational quantum circuit for the classification of b- and c-jets at the LHCb experiment is implemented on a 5-qubit superconducting quantum computer manufactured by IQM and installed at the Politecnico di Torino. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of a quantum algorithm for jet identification using simulated data from an LHC experiment in which all stages of the machine-learning workflow, including training and testing, are performed on real quantum hardware. The results demonstrate that the impact of hardware-induced quantum noise does not significantly degrade the performance. A framework for the evaluation of the training time on the quantum hardware and for the circuit optimization is also proposed. These findings pave the way for future applications on larger quantum devices based on superconducting qubits, featuring a larger number of qubits enabling the encoding of information beyond the reach of classical simulation and providing a step toward investigating whether quantum advantage may eventually be achievable in high-energy physics classification tasks.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Computation of eigenvalues and eigenvectors of a sparse Hermitian matrix is a fundamental problem in algebra, arising both as a problem of independent interest and as a key subroutine in a wide range of computational and scientific applications. In this work, we develop a quantum algorithm that determines the spectrum of a sparse Hermitian matrix, defined as the set of its distinct eigenvalues, and for each eigenvalue, prepares a corresponding eigenvector as a quantum state in its eigenspace. The eigenvalues are represented as binary strings with a prescribed precision, while the corresponding eigenvectors are kept as quantum states, eliminating the need to reconstruct their classical counterparts. The algorithm instead enables estimation of expectation values $\braket{x|M|x}$, where $\ket{x}$ is a prepared eigenstate and $M$ is an observable. For an $n \times n$ $s$-sparse Hermitian matrix $H$, dense methods based on Householder tridiagonalization and QR iteration compute all eigenvalues and eigenvectors in $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ time. Exploiting sparsity, the Lanczos method computes all eigenvalues in $\mathcal{O}(n^2 s)$ time in exact arithmetic, but recovering all eigenvectors still requires $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ time. In contrast, our algorithm runs in time $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n s^2 \log^2 n / Δ_{\min})$, where $Δ_{\min}$ denotes the minimum spectral gap of the normalized matrix. For inverse gaps up to $Δ_{\min}^{-1} = \mathcal{O}(n)$, the most favorable scaling for $n$ distinct eigenvalues, attained by nearly uniformly spaced spectra such as those of perfect-state-transfer spin chains and the Sylvester-Kac matrix, and for sparsity up to $s = o(\sqrt{n}/\log n)$, the proposed algorithm achieves an improved asymptotic runtime over the classical $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ approach for computing all eigenvalues and eigenvectors, under standard sparse-access assumptions.

</details>
