Abstract: Quasiperiodically driven systems give rise to a rich set of physical phenomena in contemporary quantum physics, with applications ranging from nonequilibrium dynamics to Hamiltonian engineering and control. However, unlike periodically driven (Floquet) systems, their study is hindered by the absence of a generic theory to describe their evolution using an effective time-independent Hamiltonian and its eigenstates. Based on counterdiabatic driving, we construct a general framework to identify and determine the dynamically invariant spaces of arbitrary quasiperiodic drives (i.e., the generalization of Floquet states). We decompose their unitary evolution using the exact effective average-energy operator Æ and the corresponding gauge potential, which we compute via a variational principle in frequency-lattice operator space. We then formulate a four-fold taxonomy of quasiperiodic drives based on the geometry and topology of their dynamically invariant spaces into (non)abelian and (un)twisted classes. The abelian untwisted class admits a direct extension of Floquet's theorem to incommensurate quasiperiodic drives; going beyond, we derive a nonabelian generalization of Floquet theory. Finally, we propose a concept of ergodicity whose onset is marked by coalescing dynamically invariant eigenspaces, in direct analogy to the emergence of ergodicity in a mixed classical phase space. The effective mathematical framework, variational principle, and nonabelian ergodicity we put forward apply beyond quasiperiodic drives.

</details>

Representative cross-dimensional schemes either switch the computational encoding or consume a 3D encoded state. Reusing the same protected 3D block would remove repeated auxiliary-state preparation and terminal readout, raising a central question: can the 3D module remain encoded and protected while mediating successive $T$ gates on a separate 2D register? We construct a membrane-like joint-logical measurement and combine it with a matched cross-dimensional $CZ$, complementary string-like surgery, and feed-forward to close a deterministic logical $T$-gate cycle. The cycle avoids post-selection and destructive mediator readout and restores the 3D block to its initial logical state. Stabilizer analysis establishes the logical action and preserved spatial distance. Phenomenological and circuit-level simulations of a Clifford surrogate show finite-distance scaling close to the matched 3D memory benchmark, while sequential tests show approximately linear logical-error accumulation in the low-error regime. Persistent mediation enables repeated access to higher-dimensional non-Clifford capability while keeping the computational register in its 2D encoding.

</details>

Result: 质疑方的结论缺乏数学依据；无论采用何种资源计量方式，或引入有限样本统计考量，所展示的量子增强局域相位灵敏度依然成立。

Main category: quant-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非正交态叠加（如光学猫态）作为量子资源的价值取决于其各分量如何干涉，但缺乏在保持"叠加"这一资源不被破坏的操作下，定量刻画转换能力与资源等价性的理论。

Method: 引入叠加自由操作（不能确定性地使各分量干涉更具破坏性且保留全部分量），证明该原理对在分量置换下对称的所有源态都唯一确定最优转换概率；以相干态为参考态时，将每个参考态映射为相干态的映射（如光子损耗、光子减除）均为叠加自由操作；对两个参考态完全求解，并推广至三个及以上等正重叠参考态，还考察多拷贝集体操作。

Result: 对两个参考态完全求解：等模叠加（相干理论中最大相干态的对应物）互不等价——奇猫态经归一化光子减除可确定性变为偶猫态，反之仅以概率实现且在小振幅下趋于零；偶猫态与Yurke-Stoler猫态不能双向确定性互转。多拷贝集体操作通常优于逐拷贝转换，但在相干蒸馏中无增益。对三个及以上等正重叠参考态，无态能确定性到达所有其他态；干涉最破坏的等模叠加可确定性产生最大相干态，并在最坏情况下不优于该类态。

Conclusion: 叠加自由操作下的转换概率由干涉破坏性原理唯一固定，等模叠加构成严格不等价的资源类，猫态之间一般不可确定性互转；该结果给出非正交叠加资源理论的基本结构与操作极限。

Abstract: How well a superposition of nonorthogonal states, such as an optical cat state, serves as a resource depends on how its components interfere. Here we determine the optimal probability of converting one such superposition into another by superposition-free operations. These operations cannot make the components of a superposition interfere more destructively with certainty while keeping all of them, and this principle fixes the optimal probability for every source that is symmetric under permutations of its components. When the reference states are coherent states, as in a cat state, every operation that maps each of them to a coherent state, such as photon loss or photon subtraction, is superposition-free. For two reference states we solve the problem completely. Superpositions with coefficients of equal modulus, the counterparts of the maximally coherent states of coherence theory, are therefore inequivalent resources: an odd cat state becomes even with certainty by a normalized photon subtraction, whereas an even one becomes odd only with a probability that vanishes for small amplitudes, and the even and the Yurke$-$Stoler cat states cannot be converted into each other with certainty in either direction. Collective operations on several copies can do better than converting them one at a time, except in the distillation of coherence, which gains nothing from them. For three or more reference states with equal positive overlaps, no state reaches every other state with certainty; the equal-modulus superpositions that interfere most destructively still yield a maximally coherent state with certainty and reach every superposition of all reference states at least as well as such a state does, but in the worst case no better.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Ground-state spin squeezing is a resource for quantum-enhanced measurements, but is commonly associated with a closing excitation gap near a quantum critical point. Using the anisotropic Lipkin--Meshkov--Glick model, we show that, for a quadratic collective mode, the ratio of two transverse fluctuation costs determines squeezing, whereas their geometric mean sets the collective gap. The pair terms responsible for unequal costs are allowed, but not guaranteed, by $\mathbb Z_2$ parity. To extend this picture beyond a single mode, we derive an exact relation between squeezing, polarization, and excitation energies weighted by their collective-spin transition strengths. For XXZ chains, weak-coupling and spin-wave analyses explain how squeezing can develop while both the collective and many-body gaps increase. Exact finite-size calculations for power-law interactions, including the nearest-neighbor limit, confirm a negative-coupling branch on which the two gaps coincide and increase as squeezing improves. For nearest-neighbor interactions, this branch also retains squeezing at higher temperatures than a gap-closing branch with comparable ground-state squeezing. In the Ising chain, by contrast, a staggered excitation can lower the many-body gap without directly contributing to collective fluctuations. Our results explain why ground-state squeezing can accompany either a closing or an opening collective gap, and identify a gap-opening branch with enhanced thermal robustness and the potential for faster adiabatic preparation.

</details>

Abstract: Continuous quantum error correction promises reduced thresholds and hardware overhead compared to the digital paradigm, but its complex model estimation has greatly reduced its practicality. We recast this problem as a classical control-theoretic state hierarchy made of an exact bilinear reduced model, an exact conditional filter, and standard estimation and feedback design. For any $[[n,k,d]]$ stabilizer code whose generators are all continuously monitored and which is driven by a single Pauli recovery channel, we prove that the exact filter closes at dimension $3\cdot2^{\,n-k-1}$. This dimension is set by the syndrome space instead of the Hilbert space and is minimal within invariant-subspace filtering. The reduced model exposes and corrects two failure mechanisms of the feedback loop. Strong monitoring damps the rotational partner through which the controller steers the stabilizer and injects back-action diffusion along the recovery path; lowering the measurement strength during correction raises the entanglement fidelity of partner-steering LQR control by $0.096$ in the three-qubit code with three monitored parities. Linearizing at the codespace leaves the syndrome uncontrollable, so the controller can stall; a gain designed at a shifted operating point removes this degeneracy. In the limit of zero measurement during correction, we prove that a syndrome-only filter is exact at every recovery boundary. The resulting committed-recovery controller needs only $2^{\,n-k}$ class probabilities and gives the highest entanglement fidelity compared among the methods tested in the literature, $0.947$ against $0.920$ for a measurement-based estimator protocol in the three-qubit bit-flip code, and $0.726$ vs at most $0.407$ for continuous controllers in the five-qubit perfect code.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建模块化、面向部署的QKD发射机，与通用自由空间光终端及独立的GPS授时同步架构对接；采用航空合规光学设计，工作波长785 nm，接收端使用室温单光子探测器且不加自适应光学；在巴塞罗那都市区18.9 km水平链路上开展40分钟外场试验，测量发射机性能与链路特性，并基于实测信道参数估算有限码长密钥率。

Result: 40分钟外场测试中平均信道衰减约19 dB，平均QBER为1.31%；据此估计平均有限码长秘密密钥率超过11 kbit/s。

Conclusion: 结果表明，采用相对简单的架构即可在密集城区实现跨城自由空间QKD，验证了其可行性；模块化发射机与独立同步架构有利于集成到异构光基础设施中。

Abstract: Terrestrial free-space quantum key distribution (QKD) provides a flexible complement to fiber-based networks, but its deployment over long horizontal paths in dense metropolitan environments remains challenging, as atmospheric turbulence, demanding pointing and tracking requirements, and operational constraints can substantially increase system complexity. We experimentally demonstrate the operation of a decoy-state BB84 system over an 18.9~km free-space link across a densely populated urban area in the metropolitan region of Barcelona using an aviation-compliant optical architecture. The system operates at 785~nm using room-temperature single-photon detectors and requires no adaptive optics at the receiver. The modular QKD transmitter, based on a deployment-oriented design, is interfaced with a versatile free-space optical terminal and an independent GPS-disciplined synchronization architecture, facilitating its integration into heterogeneous optical infrastructures. We present the transmitter performance and optical-link characteristics, together with a 40-minute field test characterized by an average channel attenuation of approximately 19~dB and a mean QBER of 1.31\%. From the measured channel parameters, we estimate an average finite-size secret key rate exceeding 11~kbit/s. These results demonstrate the feasibility of inter-city free-space QKD over dense urban areas using a comparatively simple architecture.

*Xi-Jun Yuan,Shuo Zhou,Yizhou Liu,Xuan Chen,Hong-Jing Du,Xiao-Wen Shang,Wen-Hao Zhou,Yu-Xuan Fu,Bao-Jing Liu,Ze-Feng Lan,Hang Song,Fang-Song Nie,Yu-Lin Yu,Xiao-Yun Xu,Jian-Peng Dou,Tongyang Li,Hao Tang,Xian-Min Jin*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 实验验证QTS是有效且高效的非凸优化求解器,可视作近中期模拟量子模拟器在机器学习领域的实际应用。

Abstract: Non-convex optimization plays an important role in modern machine learning since plenty of real-world problems have objective functions or constraints with non-convex properties. The non-convex nature of landscapes gives rise to numerous local minima and saddle points, dramatically hindering the ability of gradient descent-based methods to explore the landscape and obtain global optima. Here we present quantum tunneling search (QTS), an algorithm based on quantum tunneling walk and local annealing, which effectively explores the non-convex landscape by leveraging the quantum tunneling effect. In experiments, we map an objective function to the potential term of the waveguide Hamiltonian by controlling the refractive index with the femtosecond direct laser writing technique. With a proper input state, the average probability distributions on 10-micrometer-spaced 29 x 1 and 21 x 9 waveguide arrays highlight the local minima of various 1D and 2D non-convex landscapes, respectively. By further introducing the varying Hamiltonian approach, more efficient search and even the identification of the global minimum are achieved. Our work experimentally verifies that QTS is a successful and efficient non-convex optimization solver, demonstrating quantum speedups over classical stochastic gradient descent (SGD), which serves as a practical application of near-term analog quantum simulators in the field of machine learning.

Method: 研究两个热态之间的Hong-Ou-Mandel类干涉，在单光子功率区间通过时间符合计数测量频率差，并结合理论模型对比验证，同时比较热态拍频信号与预报单光子源HOM干涉的振荡轮廓。

Conclusion: 该方法可用于在单光子功率下测定窄带光源频率差，并可作为寻找两预报单光子光源零失谐的实用手段，为构建不可区分光源阵列提供支持。

Abstract: Narrowband photonic sources are promising candidates in quantum networks for a direct interface with atomic quantum memories. However, building a large-scale quantum network requires many indistinguishable sources, and matching the frequencies of independent narrowband sources is generally challenging. Here, we propose a method to measure the frequency difference between two narrowband light sources in the single-photon power regime by studying Hong$-$Ou$-$Mandel-like (HOM) interference between thermal states. Our results show that the absolute value of the frequency difference can be extracted from the temporal coincidence counts, and these results agree well with our theoretical model. We also demonstrate that the beat signal of the HOM-like interference between two thermal states has a similar oscillation profile to the HOM interference between two heralded single-photon sources, confirming that this method can be used to find the zero detuning of two heralded single-photon sources.

</details>


### [87] [Quantum Keynesian Beauty Contest with Continuous Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16829)
*Hiu Ming Lau,Kareem Raslan,Kieran Hymas,Qiang Sun,Azhar Iqbal,Derek Abbott,James Q. Quach,Andrew D. Greentree*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文用连续变量量子线路实现Keynesian p-Beauty Contest博弈，通过多模压缩生成的全局变换抑制"繁荣-萧条"理性套利行为，并指出全局调解（global mediation）是这一现象的博弈论机制。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建p-BC的量子连续变量实现：对玩家的出价施加由递增多模压缩（multimode squeezing）生成的全局变换，并在二、三及多玩家（平均场型）情形下分析；同时说明可用经典信道加全局调解者构造完全类似的博弈；推导单次测量下支付概率分布的解析形式，并与期望值测量情形对比。

Result: 递增多模压缩带来的全局变换可有效削弱繁荣/萧条的理性行为；由于经典信道亦可复现同一效果，量子资源并非必需，全局调解才是抑制繁荣-萧条现象的根本博弈论机制；给出支付分布解析表达式，并显示单值测量与期望值测量下的理性行为存在差异。

Conclusion: p-BC中的极端理性行为可通过全局调解机制被"淬灭"，量子实现只是其中一种物理载体；测量方式（单次 vs. 期望值）会改变博弈的理性均衡特征，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: The development of quantum computing has opened up opportunities to re-explore conventional game models by the integration of quantum processes. Through the established framework of quantum game theory, we examine the problem of speculative pricing in the Keynesian Beauty Contest model -- the $p$-Beauty Contest ($p$-BC). In the $p$-BC, players are rewarded with the optimal payoff for successfully bidding at some fraction $p$ of the average of all of the players' bids. Consequently, \textit{rational} plays in the $p$-BC inexorably lead to boom or bust strategies for $p>1$ or $p<1$ respectively. We demonstrate a quantum continuous-variable implementation of $p$-BC where the boom-and-bust rational behavior is mitigated through a global transformation of the players' bids generated by increasing multimode squeezing. While we focus on a quantum mechanical implementation of the $p$-BC, we show that an entirely analogous game can be formulated via classical channels by a global mediator. Thus we identify global mediation as the underlying game-theoretic mechanism for quenching boom and bust phenomena in our model. We analyze the quantum $p$-BC for two, three, and many players in a mean-field type implementation. We derive an analytical form for the probability distribution of the payoff under single-value measurements of the quantum processing circuit, and explore how the rational behavior differs from the expected-value measurement setting.

</details>

Method: 基于Dempster-Shafer理论，将不确定性表示为不仅分配给单个标签、也分配给标签子集（集合）的权重；设计训练策略使参数化量子电路直接从数据学习这些质量分配，从而高效制备对应的量子态，并支持多种后处理策略，包括多项式资源需求的量子Dempster-Shafer融合。

Result: 所提方法可高效制备编码质量函数的量子态，并在质量函数定义在指数级多子集上时，仍以多项式资源实现量子Dempster-Shafer融合，提供实用可行方案。

Conclusion: 该工作使量子模型能够表达对预测的不确定性，将量子模型评估从单一预测性能拓展到不确定性层面，是迈向不确定性感知量子分类的一步。

Abstract: A large body of work in quantum machine learning has centered on characterizing the expressivity and performance of quantum models. In contrast, little attention has been paid to the reliability of predictions beyond average performance measures such as accuracy. In this work, we introduce an uncertainty-aware quantum classification framework based on Dempster-Shafer theory. This theory allows to express uncertainty through weights assigned not only to individual classification labels but to sets of labels. We introduce a training strategy that enables parameterized quantum circuits to learn such assignments directly from data. This provides an efficient way to prepare the corresponding quantum states and enables the application of different post-processing strategies. In particular, it offers a practical setting for quantum Dempster-Shafer fusion procedures with polynomial resource requirements, even when the corresponding mass functions are defined over exponentially many subsets. Overall, our work represents a step toward enabling quantum models to express uncertainty about their predictions, thereby extending the evaluation of quantum models beyond predictive performance alone.

</details>

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究连续监测下含配对项的自由费米子模型，指出准周期性局域化与监测量子场论对称类之间的相互作用决定了测量诱导相变：在量子态扩散模拟中发现临界相（冯诺依曼纠缠熵按(ln L)²增长）与面积律相之间的转变，并给出Zeno区有效非厄米BdG描述及闭式局域化长度。

Abstract: Measurement-induced transitions of free fermions depend strongly on symmetry, but their interplay with quasiperiodic localization and superconducting pairing remains largely unexplored. We study a continuously monitored model of free fermions with pairing, where quasiperiodicity both controls localization and changes the symmetry class of the monitored field theory. Using topological entanglement entropy as a probe of nonlocal correlations in quantum state diffusion simulations, we identify a transition between a critical phase whose von Neumann entanglement entropy scales as $(\ln L)^2$ and an area-law phase, whereas no transition occurs in the clean limit, consistent with the corresponding DIII and D nonlinear sigma models. The transition line exhibits strong multifractality and depends nonmonotonically on quasiperiodicity, which we associate with the symmetry crossover. Deep in the Zeno regime, we derive an effective non-Hermitian Bogoliubov-de Gennes description of individual trajectories and obtain a closed-form localization length that quantitatively agrees with numerics. Our results reveal how symmetry, quasiperiodic localization, and measurement backaction combine to determine both critical entanglement and microscopic trajectory localization in monitored superconductors.

</details>


### [90] [When can local unitaries generate a complete orthogonal basis?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16921)
*Julian Eisfeld,Hermann Kampermann,Nikolai Wyderka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明两qutrit系统的普适局域可编码性（解决3×3猜想），并给出d×2、d×3等维度的构造、有限维多体系统的必要参数条件，以及非ULE与广义测量优势的等价操作解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域可编码性（能否仅用局域酉旋转将给定的多体纯态补全为正交基）表述简单，但其答案对系统局域维数高度敏感：已知2×2、4×4、8×8成立，3×3仅为猜想，而四比特及以上被证伪。作者希望解决这一公开猜想并刻画普适局域可编码（ULE）的结构与边界。

Method: 证明两qutrit（3×3）系统的普适性以解决猜想；构造d×2（d≥2）、d×3（d=3,4及d≥6）及含一或两个无限维子系统的二分系统上的普适局域编码；将启发式参数计数提升为普适性的必要条件；建立非ULE与"未知随机局域酉旋转检测"任务中广义测量相对投影可模拟测量之优势的等价关系。

Result: 证明3×3系统具有ULE；在d×2、d×3及无限维二分系统中构造出普适局域编码；给出排除无穷多情形ULE的必要参数条件；证明非ULE等价于广义测量在旋转检测任务中带来优势。

Conclusion: ULE的性质由局域维数的算术/代数结构精细决定，参数计数条件可排除无限多情形；该概念具有明确操作意义，即刻画了广义测量超出投影可模拟测量的判别能力。

Abstract: The question of local encodability asks: can a given, multipartite pure quantum state be completed to an orthonormal basis using only local unitary rotations? If any such state in a given system has this property, it is called universally locally encodable (ULE), a feature, which has applications in quantum information theory, engineering and math. Despite its easy formulation, the answer to this question depends nontrivially on the local dimensions of the system: While universal local encodability is known for $2\times 2$, $4\times 4$ and $8\times 8$-dimensional systems, it was so-far only conjectured to hold in $3\times 3$, and non-ULE was recently proved for systems of four and more qubits by Pauwels and Gühne.
  We resolve the open conjecture by proving universality for two qutrits. Furthermore, we construct (among others) universal local encodings in dimensions $d\times2$ for $d\ge2$, in dimensions $d\times3$ for $d=3,4$ and $d\ge6$, and in every bipartite system with one or two infinite-dimensional subsystems. For finite-dimensional multipartite systems, we turn a heuristic parameter count into a necessary condition for universality, which excludes universal local encodability in infinitely many cases. Finally, we give universal local encodability an operational interpretation by showing that non-ULE is equivalent to whether general measurements yield an advantage over projectively simulable ones in discrimination tasks where unknown, random local unitary rotations have to be detected.

</details>


### [91] [Communication Supremacy of a Qubit in Holevo Frenkel Weiner Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16940)
*Partha Patra,Sumit Mukherjee,A. K. Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在HFW通信框架下、不引入纠缠或测量不相容性且共享随机性免费的条件下,作者证明qubit通信相对c-bit通信具有严格优势,并构造见证量、给出鲁棒性与相干性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有观点认为:在Holevo–Frenkel–Weiner(HFW)通信任务框架中,除非额外提供量子资源或限制经典共享随机性(SR),否则qubit相对c-bit并无优势。本文旨在挑战这一"普遍认知",证明在无额外量子资源注入、SR完全免费的情形下qubit通信仍可超越c-bit通信。

Method: 构建一个prepare-measure场景:发送方Alice在特定密码学约束下制备六个量子态,接收方Bob执行三结果测量。利用该制备约束对qubit与c-bit通信能力的不同影响产生量子优势;引入可区分量子策略与经典策略集合的见证量(witness),并求解最优量子优势;进一步分析见证量的鲁棒性以适配噪声实验;同时分析Bob三结果qubit POVM的相干性探测能力。

Result: 建立了HFW框架下qubit通信的supremacy;给出了超越全部经典策略的最优量子优势,并证明见证量在噪声环境下优势依然存在;作为应用,在Bob无输入的情形下实现了无需种子随机性的认证随机数生成;进一步表明Bob的三结果qubit POVM具备独特的相干性探测能力。

Conclusion: qubit在HFW通信任务中的优势无须依赖纠缠、测量不相容性或SR限制;该优势可由制备约束与三结果POVM的相干性探测特性共同解释,并具备实验可行性,为基于相干性的量子资源理论与认证随机数生成提供新途径。

Abstract: The prevailing wisdom asserts that in the Holevo Frenkel Weiner (HFW) framework of communication tasks, a qubit offers no advantage over a c bit unless additional quantum resources are supplied or the classical shared randomness (SR) is restricted. In contrast, we establish the supremacy of qubit communication in the HFW framework when additional quantum resources, such as entanglement or measurement incompatibility are not injected and SR is a free resource. We consider a prepare-measure scenario where the sender, Alice, prepares six states subject to a specific cryptographic constraint, and the receiver, Bob, performs a three-outcome measurement. Such a constraint on preparation impacts the power of qubit communication quite differently than that of c-bit communication, ultimately giving rise to the quantum advantage. We introduce a witness capable of distinguishing quantum strategies that lie beyond the set of possible classical strategies and demonstrate the optimal quantum advantage. {As an application, we demonstrate the generation of certified randomness without requiring any seed randomness, since Bob has no input.} We further analyse the robustness of the witness and show that the advantage persists in noisy situations, making it suitable for experimental realisation. Finally, we demonstrate that Bob's three outcome qubit positive operator valued-measure (POVMs) has a distinct capability of detecting coherence, which can be regarded as the non-classical feature responsible for showcasing the quantum advantage.

</details>


### [92] [Unified master equation for driven multilevel quantum systems: Derivation, dynamics, and spectral signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16952)
*Sagarika Basak,D. Blume*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文用Lewis-Riesenfeld动力学不变量，在Born-Markov框架下推导出适用于任意驱动的统一主方程，修正了传统主方程对驱动调制耗散的失准，并以周期驱动三能级Λ系统的荧光谱给出可观测判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主方程无法准确刻画多能级量子系统中由驱动调制的耗散，这一缺陷会误导量子控制、模拟、传感与信息处理中的建模。

Method: 基于Lewis-Riesenfeld动力学不变量，在Born-Markov理论内推导统一主方程；将其应用于恒定Rabi耦合（连续波）、周期调制Rabi耦合以及非周期（ramped、chirped）驱动；并计算周期驱动三能级Λ系统的荧光谱。

Result: 统一主方程可覆盖三类驱动方案；周期驱动Λ系统的荧光谱呈现传统描述遗漏的特征。作者将传统方法的局限归因于浴频率采样不准确以及非久期耗散耦合被忽略，并划定了需要统一处理的参数区间。

Conclusion: 荧光谱提供了实验可及的谱学判据，可作为可靠耗散建模的指南，服务于量子控制、模拟、传感与信息处理。

Abstract: Drive-modified dissipation in multilevel quantum systems is not, in general, accurately captured by conventional master equations. We derive a unified master equation using Lewis-Riesenfeld dynamical invariants, accommodating arbitrary driving within Born-Markov theory. We demonstrate the applicability of the unified master equation to constant-Rabi-coupling (continuous-wave), periodically-modulated-Rabi-coupling, and nonperiodic (ramped and chirped) driving protocols. Fluorescence spectra of a periodically driven three-level $Λ$ system reveal signatures missed by conventional descriptions. We trace the limitations of the conventional descriptions to inaccurate bath-frequency sampling and the neglect of nonsecular dissipative couplings, and map out the parameter regimes that require a unified treatment. The fluorescence spectra provide experimentally accessible signatures, which serve as a spectroscopic guide to reliable modeling of dissipative processes that can inform quantum control, simulation, sensing, and information processing.

</details>


### [93] [Higher-Group Statistics on Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16962)
*Chen Yang,Yu-An Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立格点框架，将高群统计定义为闭合跃迁算符序列的Berry相位，并用Smith标准形与同调两类方法构造统计过程，证明d维球面上统计过程由H_{d+2}(B𝔾,ℤ)分类，从而用高群统计刻画量子多体系统中的耦合缺陷并探测高群SPT边界反常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同维度缺陷之间存在耦合：穿过畴壁可置换任意子，弦相交可产生粒子，这类耦合由高群（higher group）描述。但既有理论缺少可计算的格点判据，且传统±1的loop-flip不变量等指标不足以区分具有相同任意子统计却不同规范化的理论，也难以处理复杂高群结构。

Method: 1）构建格点框架：把高群统计定义为闭合跃迁算符序列的Berry相位，并要求其在算符局域修改下不变。2）核心范例：嵌入三维空间的闭合Majorana链（一维拓扑超导），构造相位为+i的loop-flip过程，通过链与物理费米子之间费米宇称的中间转移细化传统±1不变量。3）二维中利用charge-wall统计区分ℤ₂电荷共轭对称富化的两个ℤ₄ toric code（任意子统计相同，但规范理论分别为D₄与Q₈的量子双）。4）两种互补构造：其一是将Smith标准形算法推广到高群，由晶格几何与给定局域移动生成过程，并证明d维球面上的统计过程由H_{d+2}(B𝔾,ℤ)分类；其二是直接从同调类构造过程，避开指数级缺陷构型枚举。

Result: 得到分类定理：有限高群𝔾在d维球面上的统计过程由H_{d+2}(B𝔾,ℤ)分类；构造出phase=+i的loop-flip过程；用charge-wall统计区分D₄与Q₈量子双；同调方法使复杂高群结构的统计计算成为可能，并在1、2、3维区分出2-group反常，从而探测高群SPT相的边界反常。

Conclusion: 高群统计为刻画量子多体系统中耦合缺陷提供了新的不变量，并可作为探测高群对称保护拓扑相边界反常的有效工具；所提两种构造在可计算性与几何直观性上互补。

Abstract: Crossing a domain wall can permute anyons, and intersecting strings can create a particle. These couplings between defects of various dimensions are specified by higher groups. We develop a lattice framework defining higher-group statistics as Berry phases of closed sequences of hopping operators, invariant under local modifications of the operators. Our central example is a closed Majorana chain, a 1-dimensional topological superconductor, embedded in 3 dimensions. We construct a loop-flip process with phase $+i$, refining the usual $\pm 1$ loop-flip invariant through intermediate transfer of fermion parity between the chain and physical fermions. In two dimensions, charge-wall statistics distinguish two $\mathbb Z_4$ toric codes enriched by $\mathbb Z_2$ charge-conjugation symmetry, with identical anyon statistics but distinct gauged theories: the quantum doubles of the dihedral group $D_4$ and the quaternion group $Q_8$. We provide two complementary constructions of statistical processes. The first extends the Smith normal form algorithm in [Phys. Rev. X $\textbf{16}$, 011010 (2026), arXiv:2412.01886] to higher groups, generating processes from lattice geometry and prescribed local moves. For a finite higher group $\mathbb G$, we prove that statistical processes on a $d$-sphere are classified by $H_{d+2}(B\mathbb G,\mathbb Z)$. The second constructs processes directly from homology classes without examining exponentially many defect configurations, enabling us to study statistics for complex higher-group structures where the previous method is computationally prohibitive. These processes detect boundary anomalies of higher-group symmetry-protected topological phases. For example, we distinguish 2-group anomalies in 1, 2, and 3 dimensions. Higher-group statistics provide new invariants for characterizing coupled defects in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [94] [Lorentz Boosts and Governing Equations of the Quantum Geometric Tensor in 4D Energy-Momentum Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16967)
*Yiming Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将量子几何张量(QGT)从三维动量空间推广到四维能量-动量空间(ω/c, k)，在洛伦兹协变框架下统一描述"类引力度规"的量子度规与"类电磁规范场"的Berry曲率，并建立场方程与流守恒律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QGT的虚部(Berry曲率)决定反常速度与量子化霍尔输运，实部(量子度规)决定量子权重及平带超流刚度、介电响应，但既有理论仅在三维动量空间定义，缺乏对频率作为独立坐标的协变处理，无法统一几何自洽性与物理事件动力学。

Method: 1) 把QGT扩展至4D能量-动量空间，利用实空间平面波相位的洛伦兹不变性固定(ω/c,k)的变换规则；2) 对静态Dirac与Weyl系统施加洛伦兹助推，生成频率度规、四极型频率-动量剪切度规及电学型Berry-Maxwell场；3) 基于静态Weyl几何与洛伦兹协变性，识别五个洛伦兹不变量缩并，推导量子度规与Berry曲率的场方程和流守恒律；4) 将QGT的高斯谱振幅与4D时空中的局域高斯事件对应。

Result: 得到增强的4D QGT结构：包含频率度规、频率-动量剪切项与类电场Berry-Maxwell场；给出五个洛伦兹不变缩并量以及度规与曲率的协变场方程、守恒流；建立高斯谱振幅与局域时空事件的映射；并讨论在时空晶体/时变介质中的实验实现方案。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的量子几何框架，将类引力度规与类电磁规范场纳入同一协变体系，把几何自洽性与物理事件动力学联系起来，并为时空晶体与时变介质中的实验检验提供可行路径。

Abstract: In solids, the quantum geometric tensor (QGT), $Q_{μν}=g_{μν}-\frac{i}{2} Ω_{μν}$, combines the quantum metric and Berry curvature. Its imaginary part governs anomalous velocity and quantized Hall transport, while its real part determines the quantum weight and geometric contributions to flat-band superfluid stiffness and dielectric response. Here, we extend the QGT to four-dimensional (4D) energy-momentum space ($ω/c,k$), treating frequency and momentum as independent coordinates. Invariance of the real-spacetime plane-wave phase fixes their Lorentz transformation in energy-momentum space. Lorentz boosts of static Dirac and Weyl systems then generate frequency metrics, quadrupolar frequency-momentum shear metrics, and electric-like Berry-Maxwell fields. We identify five Lorentz-invariant contractions and derive the governing field equations and flow conservation laws for both the quantum metric and Berry curvature from static Weyl geometry and Lorentz covariance. We further connect Gaussian spectral amplitudes of the QGT to localized Gaussian events in 4D spacetime. Finally, we discuss experimental realizations in spacetime crystals or time-varying media and clarify how this unified quantum geometry of a gravitational-like metric and electromagnetic-like gauge fields connects geometric self-consistency with physical event dynamics.

</details>


### [95] [Improved Low-Cost Quantum Error Mitigation with Benchmarked-Noise Zero-Noise Extrapolation (bnZNE)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16971)
*Joseph Harris,Kevin Lively,Peter Ken Schuhmacher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出并实验验证"基准噪声零噪声外推"(bnZNE)及其随机化版本(randomized bnZNE)：利用由目标线路生成的、经典可模拟且结构镜像的基准线路，在每个噪声缩放水平上灵敏地测量有效错误率（可非整数缩放），从而改进ZNE的经典外推步骤，在总采样代价相同时获得比标准ZNE更准确的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准零噪声外推(ZNE)依赖简单的整数噪声缩放与外推点，外推精度受限；作者此前提出的bnZNE仅在门级去极化噪声下被证明可达到与ZNE同量级的抑制效果，尚缺乏对"每个噪声水平需运行多个随机线路"这一实际场景的支撑，也缺乏在真实硬件与大规模应用上的验证。因此需扩展为随机化bnZNE，并兼容Pauli twirling、读出误差缓解等噪声裁剪技术。

Method: 提出randomized bnZNE：在每一噪声缩放水平上运行多个随机线路，并引入由标准应用线路生成的、经典可模拟且与目标结构镜像的基准线路，用以灵敏测量各缩放水平下的有效错误率，突破整数缩放限制以改善外推拟合；同时给出针对IQM超导器件定制基准线路的生成方法。在IBM与IQM硬件上针对大量大规模应用进行实验测试。

Result: 在IBM与IQM硬件上的大规模应用中，bnZNE一致地比标准ZNE给出更准确的结果；实验验证了定制基准线路的有效性，并表明bnZNE可在同等总采样代价下于utility规模下提升精度。

Conclusion: bnZNE是ZNE的一种通用、低成本替代方案：通过改进经典外推步骤，在等代价条件下实现更高精度，并可与噪声裁剪技术结合，具备在utility规模量子计算中实际部署的潜力。

Abstract: We theoretically extend and experimentally demonstrate benchmarked-noise zero-noise extrapolation (bnZNE), a simple and low-cost method for quantum error mitigation which aims to outperform the highly popular ZNE method for an equal total shot cost by improving the classical extrapolation step. We recently proposed bnZNE as a new error mitigation method (Harris et al., arXiv:2603.10224 (2026)) and showed that it can mitigate noise to the same order as standard ZNE under gate-wise depolarizing noise. Here, we introduce randomized bnZNE which addresses the case of running multiple random circuits per noise level, allowing for the incorporation of noise-tailoring techniques like Pauli twirling and readout error mitigation, and test the method on the IBM and IQM hardware for a plethora of large-scale applications, observing bnZNE to consistently produce more accurate results than standard ZNE. Our approach enhances the extrapolation process to the zero-noise limit by using classically-simulable and structure-mirroring benchmark circuits, generated from the standard application circuit, to sensitively measure the effective error rate at each scaled noise level beyond simple integer values. We give a new method for generating tailored benchmark circuits for the IQM superconducting devices and test them experimentally. Our results demonstrate that bnZNE presents a versatile alternative to ZNE which can enable more accurate results at utility scales with an equivalent cost.

</details>


### [96] [Entanglement detection from moments of the realigned state: application to continuous-variable quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17021)
*Serge Deside,Vojtěch Kala,Nicolas J. Cerf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于实排列算符奇异矩与弱矩的可分离性判据，并给出光路实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量量子系统中纠缠认证需完整层析，实验代价高，亟需易测量的纠缠判据。

Method: 对密度算符施加实排列映射，构造基于实排列算符奇异矩与弱矩的一族可分离性判据；用耦合多份相同态副本的线性干涉仪测量这些矩，类似已有的部分转置矩多副本技术。

Result: 二阶实排列矩判据已足以见证大量量子光学态的纠缠；在含损耗NOON态等高斯与非高斯态上验证了检测能力。

Conclusion: 二阶实排列矩判据实验友好，可作为连续变量纠缠认证的有效工具。

Abstract: Certifying entanglement is a central problem in quantum information sciences, especially for continuous-variable quantum systems where full state tomography is experimentally demanding. We introduce a family of separability criteria based on the singular moments and weak moments of the operator obtained by applying the realignment map to the density operator. In practice, we find that the criterion based on the second-order moments of the realigned operator is already sufficient to witness the entanglement of a wide range of quantum optical states. The realigned moments can be readily accessed using an optical scheme consisting of a linear interferometer that couples several identical copies of the state, similarly to the multicopy technique used to measure the partial-transpose moments of a state [Phys. Rev. Research 8, 033064 (2026)]. We illustrate the detection capability of the second-order realigned-moment method for both Gaussian and non-Gaussian states, including lossy NOON states.

</details>


### [97] [Ergodicity breaking for data classification in a dissipative Ising model with competing two- and four-body interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17036)
*Simon Kochsiek,Mario Boneberg,Igor Lesanovsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将量子神经网络的分类能力映射为耗散量子Ising模型的非遍历动力学，利用竞争性两体与四体相互作用设计稳态流形，实现带非线性决策边界的多类量子态分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图揭示数据处理与相互作用多体系统动力学之间的内在联系：量子神经网络的耗散演化与非遍历（多吸引域）动力学同构，因此希望通过设计多体哈密顿量的稳态结构，把"分类"这一信息处理任务转化为可解析、可设计的物理动力学问题。

Method: 指出一类量子神经网络可映射为Lindblad主方程描述的耗散量子Ising模型；在此框架下研究同时含竞争性两体与四体相互作用的Ising模型，采用平均场计算结合小尺度精确数值（exact diagonalization/精确演化）方法，分析稳态流形与吸引域结构。

Result: emerging非线性导致非遍历动力学，处于不同吸引域的初态演化至可区分的稳态，从而实现分类；稳态流形可被设计以支持多类（multi-class）分类；吸引域结构分析表明该体系可对具有非线性决策边界的量子态进行分类。

Conclusion: 耗散多体Ising动力学可作为量子数据分类的物理实现平台，其稳态流形与吸引域几何直接编码决策边界，为"物理即计算"的可设计量子分类器提供原理性依据。

Abstract: We explore the close relationship between the processing of data and the dynamics of interacting many-body systems. For example, certain classes of quantum neural networks can be mapped onto dissipative quantum Ising models whose evolution is described by a Lindblad master equation. In these architectures, emergent nonlinearities lead to non-ergodic dynamics under which initial states located in different basins of attraction evolve towards distinguishable stationary states. This mechanism underlies the classification of quantum data. Motivated by this connection, we consider an Ising model with competing two- and four-body interactions. Using a combination of mean-field calculations and exact small-scale numerics, we show that the stationary state manifold can be designed to enable multi-class classification. We also investigate the structure of the ensuing basins of attraction, thereby showing that these systems allow for the classification of quantum states with nonlinear decision boundaries.

</details>


### [98] [Physical Observables and Superselection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17051)
*Martin Paul Vaughan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文是一篇综述，从"何谓物理"的角度重新审视超选择规则（superselection rule）：超选择规则限制了哪些自伴算子可作为可观测量，并进一步约束可实现的物理态与物理演化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准量子力学中态由Hilbert空间单位向量表示、可观测量由自伴算子表示，但超选择规则破坏了这一"任意自伴算子皆可观测"的对称图景。作者希望系统梳理超选择规则如何改变我们对"物理上可定义/可实现"的判断，并厘清其来源与有效性问题。

Method: 综述性分析：(1) 以超选择规则对"物理态、可观测量、物理变换"三者的约束为主线重构概念框架；(2) 讨论基于电荷守恒（以及可能的能量守恒）论证超选择规则存在的依据；(3) 讨论由退相干或缺乏共享参考系所导致的"有效超选择规则"。

Result: 指出超选择规则并非单纯数学限制，而是对可观测代数与态空间结构的实质性物理约束；电荷（及能量）守恒可作为其来源论证，而退相干与参考系缺失可在实践层面产生等效的超选择行为。

Conclusion: 超选择规则应从"何谓物理"的层面理解：它既是基本守恒律的体现，也可作为退相干/参考系缺失下的有效现象出现；区分"基本"与"有效"超选择对量子信息与量子测量基础的讨论具有意义。

Abstract: It is well-known that a physical state may be represented by a unit vector in a Hilbert space, whilst a physical observable may be represented by a self-adjoint operator on that same space. However, the introduction of a superselection rule on a Hilbert space imposes constraints such that not all self-adjoint operators can represent observables, which has further consequences for the states themselves as well as the physical processes that transform them. In this article, we review the concept of superselection from the view of how such rules would affect what we consider to be physical. Arguments for the existence of a superselection rule due to charge or possibly energy conservation are given and the emergence of effective superselection rules due to decoherence or the lack of a shared reference frame are also discussed.

</details>


### [99] [Noise robust super-Heisenberg scaling in time-dependent quantum sensing by subspace-twirled dynamical decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17056)
*Bakmou Lahcen,Ke Zeng,Tianyu Yang,Yu Jiang,Kok Chuan Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于子空间twirling的动力去耦方案，在无需自适应控制的情况下抑制噪声并使含时哈密顿量参数估计达到超海森堡极限标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含时哈密顿量可实现超越标准海森堡极限的超海森堡极限估计，但最优精度标度通常依赖资源消耗大、难以实现的自适应量子控制，且环境噪声会严重侵蚀该优势。

Method: 采用基于子空间twirling（在子空间而非整个希尔伯特空间上对随机酉算符取平均）的动力去耦方案：先证明子空间twirling既保留编码信号又去除环境噪声的充要条件，再建立含时哈密顿量在子空间twirling下无需自适应控制即可达到超海森堡标度的条件与性能保证。

Result: 给出子空间twirling保持信号并消除噪声的充要条件，以及超海森堡标度的条件与性能保证；并以经历子空间twirl的量子比特传感器为例，展示其同时抑制噪声并实现超海森堡标度。

Conclusion: 子空间twirling可同时克服自适应控制资源开销与噪声侵蚀两大限制，为含时且含噪条件下的超精密量子传感器设计提供指导。

Abstract: In quantum parameter estimation, time-dependent Hamiltonians can enable ultraprecise estimates of a physical parameter beyond the standard Heisenberg limit, also called the super-Heisenberg limit. However, achieving optimal scaling of the measurement precision typically requires an adaptive quantum control scheme which is resource-intensive and hard to implement in general. Furthermore, this advantage is often severely eroded by environmental noise. We propose the use of a dynamical decoupling scheme based on subspace twirling -- the averaging of random unitaries over a subspace rather than the entire Hilbert space -- to overcome these two limitations. We first prove necessary and sufficient conditions for subspace twirling to preserve the encoding signal while simultaneously removing environmental noise. We then establish conditions and provide performance guarantees for when time dependent Hamiltonians undergoing subspace twirling can achieve super-Heisenberg scaling without adaptive controls. Finally, we illustrate our results using an explicit example where a qubit sensor undergoing a subspace twirl simultaneously suppresses noise and achieves super-Heisenberg scaling. These results offer guidance for the design of ultraprecise quantum sensors under time-dependent and noisy conditions.

</details>


### [100] [Gaussian ergotropy and spatial correlations: accelerated vacuum versus a thermal field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17057)
*Siwei Li,Xiaofen Huang,Shao-Ming Fei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究能否通过功提取（work extraction）探测量子环境的空间关联：以两个耦合到加速真空或匹配惯性热场的谐振子探测器为模型，建立环境空间关联与平衡态高斯能质（Gaussian ergotropy）的定量联系，提出可消除盲点的双设置重构方案，并表明空间关联带来的全局功增益不依赖纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速真空与匹配惯性热场具有相同的局域谱（Unruh效应下的局域不可区分性），因此单探测器或局域测量无法区分二者。作者希望回答：环境的空间关联能否作为可操作的、由功提取读取的资源被揭示出来，从而突破局域谱简并带来的不可辨识性。

Method: 构建两个谐振子探测器分别耦合到加速真空与匹配惯性热场的模型，考察平衡态高斯能质；推导出主导可提取功由"响应加权空间关联"（response-weighted spatial correlation）支配；进一步通过反转探测器间直接耦合构建双设置测量方案，以消除有限距离处的盲点并从功测量中重构空间关联，同时分析超越弱耦合区间的有效性与瞬态演化行为。

Result: 尽管两种环境局域谱相同，其不同的空间关联导致可提取功响应显著不同；发现在某一有限距离处存在"盲点"，使单一测量配置无法区分两种环境；反转直接耦合的双设置方案可消除该模糊性并重构空间关联，且在弱耦合之外仍有效；即使探测器对处于可分离态，空间关联仍能提供额外的全局功，说明纠缠并非该优势的必要资源；瞬态高斯能质呈现振荡衰减，并依赖加速度与探测器参数。

Conclusion: 高斯能质可作为玻色环境中空间关联的操作性探针；空间关联是一种可被功提取量化读取的资源，且其增益不要求纠缠；双设置方案提供了鲁棒（超越弱耦合）的空间关联重构方法。

Abstract: Can the spatial correlations of a quantum environment be revealed through work extraction? We address this question using two harmonic-oscillator detectors coupled to either an accelerated vacuum or a matched inertial thermal field. We establish a quantitative connection between the spatial correlations of the environment and the equilibrium Gaussian ergotropy, showing that the leading extractable work is governed by a response-weighted spatial correlation. Although the two environments have identical local spectra, their different spatial correlations give rise to distinct work responses. We further identify a finite-distance blind point at which a single measurement configuration fails to distinguish the two environments. By reversing the direct coupling between the detectors, we develop a two-setting scheme that removes this ambiguity and reconstructs the underlying spatial correlations from work measurements. This reconstruction remains effective beyond the weak-coupling regime. We also show that spatial correlations can provide additional global work even when the detector pair is separable, demonstrating that entanglement is not a necessary resource for this advantage. Finally, we investigate the transient evolution of Gaussian ergotropy and reveal its oscillatory decay and dependence on acceleration and detector parameters. Our results establish Gaussian ergotropy as an operational probe of spatial correlations in bosonic environments.

</details>


### [101] [Multi-particle quantum walk search on a two-dimensional lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17061)
*Gonçalo Meneses,D. O. Oriekhov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究多粒子量子漫步搜索问题的形式化表述与搜索效率，基于Grover漫步演化算子，重点考察二维网格上的局部相互作用模型，分析搜索渐近时间与成功概率对相互作用强度及粒子间最大纠缠度的依赖，并给出新的广义相互作用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单粒子量子漫步搜索已较成熟，但多粒子体系中粒子间相互作用（尤其局部碰撞型相互作用）如何影响搜索效率尚缺乏系统刻画，需要明确相互作用强度与纠缠对搜索渐近时间和成功概率的作用规律。

Method: 将空间离散时间演化按Grover量子漫步算子处理，构造仅当两个及以上粒子同时占据同一格点时状态才被修改的局部相互作用模型；以二维网格为主要场景，比较相互作用与非相互作用下二粒子、三粒子搜索算法，并量化搜索渐近时间、成功概率与相互作用强度、最大纠缠度的关系；同时提出新的广义多粒子相互作用模型。

Result: 相互作用与非相互作用的二粒子、三粒子搜索算法相较于单粒子对应算法均获得常数因子改进；搜索的渐近成功时间与概率随相互作用强度和粒子间最大纠缠度呈现明确依赖关系。

Conclusion: 多粒子局部相互作用量子漫步可在常数因子层面提升搜索效率，纠缠与相互作用强度是关键调控参数；所提广义相互作用模型为后续多粒子量子漫步搜索的建模与优化提供了统一框架。

Abstract: In the present paper we study the formulation of a search problem and the efficiency of multi-particle quantum walk search algorithms. The discrete-time evolution of each particle in space is treated according to Grover's quantum walk evolution operator. We focus on the local interactions models, where the particles' states are modified only when two or more of them appear on the same grid site. The main focus is given to the two-dimensional grid in space. We study the dependence of the search algorithm asymptotic success time and probability on the interaction strength and the generated maximal entanglement between particles. We show that the interacting and non-interacting two- and three-particle search algorithm attains a constant factor improvement over the single-particle counterpart. In addition, we provide a new generalized model of interactions for multi-particle quantum walks.

</details>


### [102] [Efficient computation of PEPS gradients for optimized quantum state preparation on 2D topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17158)
*Illia Lukin,Vladyslav Bohun,Maciej Koch-Janusz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于二维PEPS梯度优化的、感知硬件拓扑的量子态制备方法，结合局域解纠缠与全局微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂纠缠初态的量子态制备难以用浅层电路高精度实现；现有张量网络方法多用一维矩阵乘积态（MPS），难以准确表达高维纠缠态。

Method: 提出硬件拓扑感知的态制备方法：以二维投影纠缠对态（PEPS）为表示，结合局域解纠缠与基于PEPS梯度优化的全局微调，并给出高效的PEPS梯度计算流程。

Result: 在数值模拟中测试了该方法的收敛性与性能。

Conclusion: 2D PEPS梯度优化结合局域解纠缠可有效实现拓扑感知的浅层量子态制备，二维表示优于一维MPS。

Abstract: Quantum state preparation is a fundamental subroutine for quantum computation. For complex, entangled initial states it is, however, difficult to prepare shallow quantum circuits approximating the state with sufficient accuracy. Tensor networks provide a promising avenue of synthesis of such circuits, but most approaches employ 1D Matrix Products States, which struggle to accurately express entangled states in higher dimensions. Here we present a hardware topology-aware quantum state preparation method based on gradient optimization of 2D projected entangled pair states (PEPS). Our approach combines local disentanglement, with global fine tuning based on PEPS gradient optimization. Towards this end we describe an efficient procedure for PEPS gradient computation. We test the convergence and performance of our method in numerical simulations.

</details>


### [103] [Explicit Absolutely Maximally Entangled states in dimensions twice a prime power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17216)
*Palash Pandya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用量子比特与q能级系统的耦合构造出局部维数为2q的绝对最大纠缠态族，得到AME(9,2q)至AME(17,2q)等新族，并据此完成九体分类：整数d≥2时AME(9,d)存在当且仅当d≥3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AME态在局域维数给定下何时存在是量子信息中的核心问题，尤其九体等小体数情形尚缺完整分类，本文旨在用耦合方法补充构造并闭合分类。

Method: 取q为奇素数幂，采用量子比特与q能级系统的已知耦合构造局部维数2q的态：q能级部分消除分支间干涉，从而只需量子比特部分在平均意义上具有最大混合约化，即满足AME条件。

Result: 对任意奇素数幂q≥5得到AME(9,2q)与AME(11,2q)，q≥17得AME(13,2q)，q≥9得AME(15,2q)，q≥67得AME(17,2q)；对任意奇数N≥3，在q≥max{N,2^{N-2}}下得到AME(N,2q)。结合已知构造与乘积，证明：对整数d≥2，AME(9,d)存在当且仅当d≥3。另外，选择分支配对可得到AME(15,2p)，其对每素数p≡1 (mod 15)具有由局域酉变换补偿的循环移位对称性。

Conclusion: 该耦合给出局部维数2q的AME族并闭合九体存在性分类，同时表明在该耦合框架内所需最小q随N指数增长，说明此途径对大体数N的局限。

Abstract: Absolutely maximally entangled (AME) states are pure multipartite states with maximally mixed reductions on every set of at most half the parties. We construct explicit families in local dimension $2q$, twice an odd prime power $q$, using an established coupling of qubits and $q$-level systems. The $q$-level states remove interference between branches, so the qubit states need maximally mixed reductions only on average. This gives AME$(9,2q)$ and AME$(11,2q)$ for every odd prime power $q\geq5$, AME$(13,2q)$ for $q\geq17$, AME$(15,2q)$ for $q\geq9$, and AME$(17,2q)$ for $q\geq67$. For every odd $N\geq3$, the same coupling gives AME$(N,2q)$ under the sufficient condition $q\geq\max\{N,2^{N-2}\}$ on the odd prime power $q$. Within this coupling, the smallest such $q$ must grow exponentially with $N$. Combined with known inputs and products, these families complete the nine-party classification: for integer $d\geq2$, an AME$(9,d)$ state exists if and only if $d\geq3$. Choosing the branch pairing also gives AME$(15,2p)$ states with a cyclic shift of the parties compensated by local unitaries, for every prime $p\equiv1\pmod{15}$.

</details>


### [104] [Programming Quantum Circuit Parameters with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17246)
*Tiago de Souza Farias,Caio Moraes Porto,Matheus da Silva Fonseca,Nicolas Armando Cabrera Carpi,Anderson de Araujo Oliveira,Celso Jorge Villas-Boas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究冻结的通用LLM能否直接从符号化电路与哈密顿量描述生成变分量子电路参数；发现其在结构化哈密顿量上有效，在随机哈密顿量上不如Adam，且直接预测能量会违反谱下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成变分量子电路参数已初现可行性，但其有效性的适用条件尚不明确，需系统厘清问题结构、迭代反馈与可验证性三方面的影响。

Method: 采用冻结的通用LLM，从符号化电路与哈密顿量描述生成完整参数向量，可选迭代能量反馈；在一般随机哈密顿量、结构化父哈密顿量与分子体系上评测，并与Adam优化对比，同时考察盲电路解读任务。

Result: 一般哈密顿量上LLM参数能量误差大于Adam且反馈增益有限；结构化哈密顿量上可生成有希望的低能参数；直接预测最小能量会违反哈密顿量谱下界，而生成参数可独立评估物理可实现的能量期望；盲电路解读中LLM在未能识别算法的情况下仍可预测测量结果。

Conclusion: 问题结构决定LLM参数推断的有效性；迭代LLM优化存在固有局限；量子参数编程需强调可独立验证的预测而非直接输出目标量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated the ability to generate parameters for variational quantum circuits without conventional optimization. However, the conditions under which this approach is effective remain unclear. Here, we investigate a frozen, general-purpose LLM that generates complete circuit parameter vectors from symbolic circuit and Hamiltonian descriptions, with optional iterative energy feedback. We evaluate the approach on general random Hamiltonians, structured parent Hamiltonians, and molecular systems. For general Hamiltonians, LLM-generated parameters exhibit larger energy errors than conventional Adam optimization, while additional feedback generally provides limited improvement. In contrast, for structured Hamiltonians, the LLM can generate promising low-energy parameter configurations, suggesting that identifiable physical and mathematical structure facilitates parameter inference. We also show that direct LLM predictions of minimum energies can violate the Hamiltonian spectral lower bound, whereas generated circuit parameters allow independent evaluation of physically realizable energy expectation values. Finally, we investigate blind circuit interpretation and find that the LLM can predict measurement outcomes even when it fails to identify the underlying quantum algorithm. These findings highlight the role of problem structure, the limitations of iterative LLM optimization, and the importance of independently verifiable predictions in quantum parameter programming.

</details>


### [105] [Implementation of a 1D Gaussian-modulated CV-QKD under finite-size parameter estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17250)
*Christiano M. S. Nascimento,Maron F. Anka,Gabriel A. V. Menezes,Davi J. G. de Sousa,Nelson A. Neto,Artur A. Matoso,Gustavo C. Amaral,Valéria L. Silva,Guilherme P. Temporão*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 实验演示基于独立激光器与局域本振(LLO)架构的12 km光纤一维高斯调制CV-QKD系统，集成相干探测、DSP、信息协调、参数估计、物理性约束安全性分析与隐私放大，渐近情形密钥率1.421 Mbit/s、有限码长情形0.364 Mbit/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高斯调制CV-QKD需在两个光场正交分量上编码，对态制备、调制与校准要求高，并可能引入额外制备不完美；一维(1D)高斯调制仅在一个正交分量编码，可简化发射端，但缺乏集成的1D实验实现（独立激光器相干接收+实用协调+有限码长参数估计+隐私放大）。

Method: 采用局域本振(LLO)架构与独立激光器，在12 km标准单模光纤上搭建1D高斯调制CV-QKD系统；实现相干探测、数字信号处理、信息协调、参数估计、物理性约束安全分析及隐私放大组成的集成离线经典后处理链。

Result: 渐近安全条件下隐私放大后密钥率1.421 Mbit/s；有限码长条件下0.364 Mbit/s。

Conclusion: 结果为单维高斯调制所对应的性能—复杂度权衡建立了实验基准，并为开发更低复杂度的CV-QKD发射机提供实验依据。

Abstract: Continuous-variable quantum key distribution (CV-QKD) is a promising technology for quantum-secure communications, offering high secret-key rates and straightforward integration with coherent optical communication systems. Conventional Gaussian-modulated CV-QKD encodes information in both optical-field quadratures, increasing requirements for state preparation, modulation, and calibration, while potentially introducing additional preparation imperfections. Unidimensional (1D) Gaussian modulation offers a simpler alternative by encoding information in only one quadrature, while the conjugate quadrature constrains the protocol's security. However, an integrated experimental 1D implementation combining independent-laser coherent reception with practical reconciliation, finite-size parameter estimation, and privacy amplification remains challenging. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a 1D Gaussian-modulated CV-QKD system over 12~km of standard single-mode fiber using a local local oscillator (LLO) architecture with independent lasers and an integrated offline classical post-processing chain. The implementation includes coherent detection, digital signal processing, information reconciliation, parameter estimation, physicality-constrained security analysis, and privacy amplification. The 1D system achieves a privacy-amplified rate of $1.421$~Mbit/s under the asymptotic security treatment and $0.364$~Mbit/s under the finite-size scenario. These results establish an experimental baseline for the performance--complexity trade-off associated with unidimensional Gaussian modulation and provide an experimental basis for developing lower-complexity CV-QKD transmitters.

</details>


### [106] [Non-interferometric tests of gravitational decoherence models with mechanical oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17260)
*Sandro Donadi,Iarley P. Lobo,Matteo Fadel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究三种量子引力诱导退相干模型（形变相对论对称性、随机度规涨落、随机广义不确定性原理）在非干涉测量中的可观测效应，提出用加热效应与连续位置测量作为探测量子引力退相干的互补手段，并给出超越普朗克时间的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力诱导退相干通常依赖干涉实验探测，但干涉平台对质量与环境要求严苛。作者希望证明即使不依赖干涉测量，这些唯象模型的效应也能在自由粒子、谐振子及固体低温实验中显现，从而拓展探测途径。

Method: 构建并对比三种动量基退相干模型：①基于形变相对论对称性；②随机度规涨落；③随机广义不确定性原理。分别分析自由粒子的动量分布、平均能量、位置扩散与动量空间退相干；分析谐振子中坍缩算符与哈密顿量不对易导致的异常加热；推导稳态位置功率谱密度（PSD）的弱噪声领头阶过剩线型；并与机械振子平台及固体多声子模式低温热释放实验对比。

Result: 自由粒子情形下三模型保持动量分布与平均能量，但产生位置扩散与模型相关的动量空间退相干。谐振子中出现异常加热，三模型能量动力学定性不同：第一模型恒定加热率，第二模型加热率正比于动能（可致指数增长），第三模型加热率随激发数立方增长。三者给出相同的弱噪声领头阶过剩线型，且区别于力噪声线型。位移噪声测量已将第一模型涨落时间约束至普朗克时间以下；占据数测量可将第二模型约束较悬浮粒子基态加热提升多达十四个数量级；固体多声子模式热释放使第一模型约束远低于普朗克时间，第二模型达普朗克时间量级。

Conclusion: 加热效应与连续测量构成互补的非干涉探针，可在现有机械与低温平台上对量子引力相关退相干给出强约束，为量子引力唯象检验提供新途径。

Abstract: Phenomenological models of quantum-gravity-induced decoherence can leave observable effects even in experiments that do not rely on interferometric measurements. We study three such models, based respectively on deformed relativistic symmetries, stochastic metric fluctuations, and a stochastic generalized uncertainty principle, all of which induce decoherence in the momentum basis. For a free particle, the models preserve momentum distribution and mean energy but produce position diffusion and momentum-space decoherence with model-specific momentum dependences. For a harmonic oscillator, the noncommutativity of the collapse operators with the oscillator Hamiltonian results in anomalous heating, with the three models leading to qualitatively different energy dynamics. The first gives heating at a constant rate, the second at a rate proportional to the kinetic energy, which can lead to exponential growth, and the third at a rate growing cubically with excitation. We further derive the stationary position power spectral density and show that all three models produce the same leading-order excess lineshape in the weak-noise regime, distinct from that of force noise. Comparing with existing mechanical platforms, we find that displacement-noise measurements can already constrain the fluctuation time of the first model below the Planck time, while occupation measurements could improve the bounds on the second model by up to fourteen orders of magnitude over those from ground-state heating of levitated particles. Summing the heating over all phonon modes of a solid, the residual heat release of low-temperature experiments constrains the first model far below the Planck time and the second at the Planck time. Our results establish heating and continuous measurements as complementary non-interferometric probes of gravity-related decoherence.

</details>


### [107] [Observation of Genuine Multi-Time Quantum Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17268)
*Konstantin Beyer,Valentin Link,Surihan Sean Borjigin,Qian Cao,Orion Lee,Kater Murch,Igor Pikovski,Philip Taranto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 开放量子系统中的记忆效应不等同于量子记忆：存在“成对时间”检验均无法识别的量子记忆，仅当联合考虑三个及以上时刻时才显现。作者给出解析三时刻见证量，在最小量子比特模型与超导量子比特时连续实验中验证了量子记忆效应的严格层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有判据通过比较系统在两个不同时刻的演化来认证环境中的量子记忆，但非马尔可夫动力学常可由经典记忆模型复现。作者质疑：这类成对（两时刻）检验是否完备，量子记忆能否在所有成对检验下隐藏。

Method: 推导解析的三时刻见证量（three-time witness），构造最小量子比特实例，使任意两时刻组合均存在经典记忆解释，但三时刻联合时违反不等式；并在超导量子比特平台上以时间无关耦合、时间连续的实现进行实验演示，无需精细调节的含时哈密顿量或门操作。

Result: 三时刻见证量可认证成对检验完全无法探测的量子记忆，从而确立量子记忆效应的严格层级：两时刻量子记忆 ⊂ 真正的多时刻量子记忆；实验在时连续超导平台上观测到该层级。

Conclusion: 量子记忆的可探测性依赖于所考察时刻集合的规模，成对判据不完备；真正的多时刻量子记忆可在简单、时间无关耦合的连续演化中自然出现，并可实验认证。

Abstract: Memory effects in open quantum systems do not by themselves imply quantum memory, since non-Markovian dynamics can often be reproduced by classical-memory models. Known criteria certify the presence of quantum memory in the environment by comparing the evolution of the system at two different times. We show that quantum memory can remain hidden from all such pairwise tests and become detectable only when three or more times are considered jointly. We derive an analytical three-time witness and use it to certify quantum memory in a minimal qubit example in which every pair of times has a classical-memory explanation, thus establishing a strict hierarchy of quantum memory effects. Genuine multi-time quantum memory does not rely on fine-tuned time-dependent Hamiltonians or gate-based evolution. We experimentally demonstrate the hierarchy of quantum memory in a time-continuous realization on a superconducting-qubit platform with time-independent couplings.

</details>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Quantum Hall Fabry-Pérot Interferometry in Monolayer WSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14633)
*Himadri Chakraborti,Charles L. Tschirhart,James Fang,Saisab Bhowmik,Chengrui Yu,Shujian Cao,William Caro,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Jie Shan,Kin Fai Mak*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在单层WSe₂中实现了首个栅极定义的量子霍尔Fabry-Pérot干涉仪，利用自旋-谷锁定获得单分量朗道能级，观测到稳健的Aharonov-Bohm干涉并测得边缘速度约4.9×10⁴ m/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有van der Waals材料平台的量子霍尔边缘存在多重自旋与谷简并，使Fabry-Pérot干涉信号难以解读；单层WSe₂因强自旋轨道耦合与自旋-谷锁定天然解除简并，提供干净的单分量平台。

Method: 在单层WSe₂中制备栅极定义的量子霍尔Fabry-Pérot干涉仪，在单一器件中连续调节腔体尺寸，并结合静电学估算的体-边耦合参数，开展温度与偏压依赖测量。

Result: 观测到量子霍尔边缘通道的稳健Aharonov-Bohm干涉，AB区占优与弱体-边耦合（Δ≈0.034）一致；温度与偏压测量一致给出边缘速度v≈(4.9±0.2)×10⁴ m/s。

Conclusion: 单层WSe₂被确立为干净、味分辨的量子霍尔干涉平台，为研究单分量分数量子霍尔态及任意子统计干涉探测奠定基础。

Abstract: Electronic Fabry-Pérot interferometers provide a powerful probe of coherent quantum Hall edge transport, yet the presence of multiple spin and valley degrees of freedom in existing van der Waals material platforms can complicate their interpretation. Monolayer WSe$_2$ offers a distinct opportunity because strong spin-orbit coupling and spin-valley locking lift these degeneracies, producing intrinsically single-component Landau levels. Here we realize the first gate-defined quantum Hall Fabry-Pérot interferometer in monolayer WSe$_2$. By continuously tuning the cavity size in a single device, we observe robust Aharonov-Bohm (AB) interference of quantum Hall edge channels across the interferometer; the observed predominance in the AB regime is consistent with a weak bulk-edge coupling ($Δ\approx 0.034$) estimated by electrostatics. Moreover, temperature and bias dependence measurements consistently yield an edge velocity $v \approx (4.9 \pm 0.2) \times 10^4\,\mathrm{m\,s^{-1}}$. Our results establish monolayer WSe$_2$ as a clean, flavor-resolved platform for quantum Hall interferometry and provide a foundation for future studies of single-component fractional quantum Hall states and interferometric probes of anyonic statistics in transition-metal dichalcogenides.

</details>


### [109] [A discrete realization of the boundary sine-Gordon model and the Schmid transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14637)
*Nicolas Paris,Oscar Bouverot-Dupuis,Christophe Mora*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出一个可精确映射到边界sine-Gordon模型(bsG)的离散模型，并用高效团簇Monte Carlo方法数值求解其相图，明确证实了Schmid转变的存在及其性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: bsG模型（等价于电阻分流约瑟夫森结）的相图近年存在争议，特别是Schmid转变是否存在、其临界固定点结构以及它与BKT转变的区别尚无定论，亟需大规模、高精度的数值验证。

Method: 构造一个与bsG模型性质完全等价的离散格点模型，使其可用团簇Monte Carlo算法极高效地模拟；借此扫描广泛参数区域，计算弱隧穿与强隧穿区间之间的完整普适跨接行为，并利用模型的弱-强对偶性（self-duality）定位竖直线上的临界固定点。

Result: 清晰识别出对应于Schmid转变的竖直线临界固定点，并沿该线以高精度复现共形场论(CFT)的预言；同时阐明Schmid转变与BKT转变的本质差异。

Conclusion: 结果明确确立了Schmid转变的存在与性质，验证了Schmid–Bulgageev图景的正确性。

Abstract: The phase diagram of the boundary sine-Gordon model (bsG), or equivalently of the resistively shunted Josephson junction, has been the subject of recent debate. We introduce a discrete model that reproduces exactly the properties of the bsG model and can be simulated extremely efficiently using cluster Monte Carlo algorithms. This enables us to explore a broad range of parameters and compute the complete universal crossover between the weak- and strong-tunneling regimes. We establish the self-duality of the model and unambiguously identify the vertical line of critical fixed points characteristic of the Schmid transition, recovering the conformal field theory predictions along this line with high accuracy. We further clarify the essential distinctions between the Schmid and Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transitions. Our results definitively establish the existence and nature of the Schmid transition and demonstrate the validity of the Schmid-Bulgadaev scenario.

</details>


### [110] [Controlling the Vacuum: Tunable Magnonic Casimir Effect in Magnetic Moiré Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14809)
*Alejandro Roldan,Dante Navarrete,Mario A. Castro,Sebastián Allende,Roberto E. Troncoso,Alvaro S. Nunez*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文证明moiré结构中磁自由度的量子涨落可通过调节moiré晶格来调控，并据此将磁振子真空能与磁振子Casimir力表述为moiré图案参数的函数，进而构建以真空涨落为工作介质的原型引擎，产生新型磁-机械效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注moiré结构是否可作为调控量子真空涨落（磁自由度）的手段，并探索这种可调性在真空能、Casimir力乃至能量转换（引擎）方面的物理后果。

Method: 采用Bistritzer-MacDonald方法处理moiré磁结构，以moiré Ladder为具体算例，计算磁振子真空能与磁振子Casimir力随moiré图案参数（如扭转角/晶格失配）的变化；指出该方法可推广至任意moiré结构。

Result: 磁振子真空能与磁振子Casimir力成为moiré图案参数的函数；借助其可调性可运行一个以真空涨落为工作介质的原型引擎，从而得到一类新型磁-机械效应。

Conclusion: 作者预期这种可调真空涨落效应是moiré结构的普遍性质，与所涉激发（如磁振子）的物理本质无关，可推广至其他类型的激发与moiré体系。

Abstract: We prove that the quantum fluctuations of the magnetic degrees of freedom of moiré structures can be tuned by adjusting the moiré lattice. Using the Bistritzer-MacDonald approach, we explore the consequences of this fact for the magnonic vacuum energy and the magnonic Casimir force, which become functions of the moiré pattern parameters. This allows a prototypical engine to be run with the vacuum fluctuations as a working agent and, in that way, to furnish a novel type of magneto-mechanical effect. Our calculations focus on the moiré Ladder as an example but can be generalized to any moiré structure in a straightforward fashion. We anticipate that our results belong to a generic property of moiré structures, regardless of the physical nature of the excitations at hand.

</details>


### [111] [Interplay of the Fano-Andreev Effect and Nonlinear Screening in Electric and Thermoelectric Transport through a T-Shaped Double Quantum Dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14991)
*Sachin Verma*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文理论研究T型双量子点与正常/超导电极耦合时的弱非线性电输运与热电输运；在特征势框架下处理非线性屏蔽，揭示Fano-Andreev效应与屏蔽导致能级重整化，从而产生偏压不对称的Andreev微分电导、抑制准粒子热电响应，并在电压与热偏压共存时出现符号可控的亚能隙交叉热电响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多集中于线性响应或忽略非线性屏蔽，而T型双量子点中Fano-Andreev效应与非平衡电荷重分布在弱非线性区如何共同影响电与热电输运尚不清楚，需要建立统一的理论描述。

Method: 采用特征势（characteristic-potential）形式处理非线性屏蔽：以内部静电梯势描述电极耦合中心量子点上的非平衡电荷重分布；体系为T型双量子点（中心点与正常、超导电极耦合，侧耦合点诱导Fano-Andreev效应），在电压偏压、纯热偏压及二者同时施加下计算Andreev微分电导、热电压、非线性Seebeck系数与交叉热电响应。

Result: 电压偏压下屏蔽引起能级重整化，产生显著的偏压不对称Andreev微分电导；纯热偏压下，增大点间耦合削弱能级重整化，抑制准粒子热电响应，使热电压与非线性Seebeck系数降低；电压与热偏压同时施加时，在线性区之外出现有限亚能隙交叉热电响应。

Conclusion: 该交叉热电响应的符号取决于能量相关输运贡献（受Fano-Andreev效应修正）与非线性屏蔽贡献之间的竞争，从而决定热偏压是增强还是抑制低偏压Andreev电流；结果强调非线性屏蔽与Fano-Andreev干涉在共存输运中的关键作用。

Abstract: We present a theoretical study of weakly nonlinear electric and thermoelectric transport through a T-shaped double quantum dot coupled to normal and superconducting leads. Nonlinear screening is treated within the characteristic-potential formalism, in which an internal electrostatic potential accounts for nonequilibrium charge redistribution on the lead-coupled central dot. The side-coupled dot induces the Fano-Andreev effect, which, together with nonlinear screening, significantly modifies the transport properties. Under voltage bias, the screening-induced level renormalization produces a pronounced bias asymmetry in the Andreev differential conductance. In response to a pure thermal bias, increasing the interdot coupling weakens the level renormalization and suppresses the quasiparticle thermoelectric response, leading to reduced thermovoltage and nonlinear Seebeck coefficient. When voltage and thermal biases are applied simultaneously, a finite subgap cross thermoelectric response emerges beyond the linear regime. The sign of this response is governed by the competition between the energy-dependent transport contribution, modified by the Fano-Andreev effect, and the nonlinear screening contribution, thereby determining whether a thermal bias enhances or suppresses the low-bias Andreev current.

</details>


### [112] [Momentum-Space Mismatch Enables Non-Hermitian Valley Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15036)
*Kai Shao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文指出系统能带接触节点与耗散库之间的动量空间失配可诱导节点选择性非厄米趋肤效应（NHSE），并据此在多种晶格中实现近100%的谷极化输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谷滤波器大多针对特定材料或晶格几何设计，缺乏普适的微观机制；作者希望寻找一种不依赖具体材料的共同要素来实现谷选择输运。

Method: 将节点与耗散库耦合，使动量失配编码于推迟自能中，产生节点依赖的耗散与准粒子寿命差异；由此构造的有效非厄米哈密顿量在两节点呈现相反的点隙谱缠绕，形成节点分辨的NHSE。模型在最小一维二聚体链以及含真实Dirac谷的二维体系（π-通量矩形晶格与蜂窝晶格）中验证，并进行输运计算。

Result: 节点分辨的NHSE导致强不对称耗散：一个节点快速衰减入库，另一节点在器件中存活，输出电流近乎谷极化；所有案例的输运计算给出近100%谷极化，且可栅压调控，并对无序与边缘缺陷鲁棒。

Conclusion: 动量空间失配是一条实现非厄米谷滤波的通用途径，提供与材料/晶格无关的谷选择性输运机制。

Abstract: Valley-selective transport is central to valleytronics, but existing valley filters are largely tailored to specific materials or lattice geometries, motivating the search for a common microscopic ingredient. Here we identify the momentum-space mismatch between the band-touching nodes of a system and its dissipative reservoir as a route to node-selective non-Hermitian skin effects (NHSE). When the two are coupled, the mismatch is encoded in the retarded self-energy, producing node-dependent dissipation and contrasting quasiparticle lifetimes. The resulting effective non-Hermitian Hamiltonian exhibits opposite point-gap spectral winding at the two nodes, giving rise to a node-resolved NHSE with strongly asymmetric dissipation rates: one node decays rapidly into the reservoir while the other survives over the device, yielding a nearly valley-polarized outgoing current. We demonstrate this mechanism in a minimal one-dimensional dimer chain and in two-dimensional systems hosting genuine Dirac valleys, including $π$-flux rectangular and honeycomb lattices. In all cases, transport calculations yield nearly 100\% valley polarization, while remaining gate-tunable and robust against disorder and edge imperfections. Our results show that momentum-space mismatch provides a route to non-Hermitian valley filtering.

</details>


### [113] [Magnon Chirality Selection at Equal Linewidths in Open Collinear Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15113)
*Dinghui Wang,Lulu Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在有耗散的共线磁振子网络中，空间关联的损耗与相干交换可使两种手性获得不同存活几率，从而在共振线宽完全相同时实现手性选择，残余布居中极化度达26.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为手性选择需要不同线宽或外场破缺；作者希望说明仅凭损耗（被动、无增益）即可分离同线宽的两种磁振子手性，并厘清该效应究竟约束极点、制备、本征矢还是物理库。

Method: 构造被动共线模式网络：令空间关联损耗与相干交换对相反角动量给出不同存活概率；以同谱的正常对照作参照；用非厄米共线自旋-空间小群对极点在手性与复频率上的允许配对进行分类；第二种实现利用零总绕数下相反手性扇区在相对对角边缘的分离。

Result: 初始无极化混合态获得26.9%极化，且保留29.9%布居；同谱正常对照无极化。自旋与晶体联合旋转可反转信号，并交换在更强损耗下到达的异常等值线。第二实现虽总绕数为零，仍在相对对角边缘分离共轭手性扇区。

Conclusion: 损耗本身即可作为手性选择资源；两类读出分别对应极点约束与制备、本征矢及物理库约束，为区分不同非厄米手性机制提供判据。

Abstract: Loss can select one magnon chirality even when all resonances have the same linewidth. We construct a passive collinear mode network in which spatially correlated loss and coherent exchange give opposite angular momenta different survival probabilities. An initially unpolarized mixture reaches 26.9\% polarization with 29.9\% of the population remaining; an isospectral normal control gives none. A combined spin and crystal rotation reverses the signal and exchanges the exceptional contours reached at stronger loss. We organize the allowed pole pairings by the action of a non-Hermitian collinear spin-space little group on chirality and complex frequency. A second realization separates conjugate chirality sectors at opposite diagonal edges despite zero total winding. The two readouts distinguish constraints on poles from constraints on preparations, eigenvectors, and physical reservoirs.

</details>


### [114] [Magnetically Compensated Spin-Polarized Edge States in Coplanar Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15134)
*Dinghui Wang,Lulu Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出边界释放自旋极化电子态的对称性判据：在共面交换磁体中，允许的偶宇称边界自旋空间等于允许的均匀磁化空间，因此对称性保护的磁补偿必然选择垂直于磁矩平面的奇自旋织构；六个极大扇区组织所有可能的边界织构，并在FeSe与NiI$_2$模型中给出材料实现及互补的电学读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性补偿（自旋简并）层通常被认为不具自旋极化，但边界可将其释放为自旋极化边缘态；然而缺乏统一判据说明何种边界织构被允许、其宇称与磁结构的关系，以及如何在电学上区分。

Method: 建立共面交换磁体中边界对称性判据，比较偶宇称边缘自旋空间与均匀磁化空间的允许子空间；按对称性分类出六个极大扇区；在同一交换模型的正交切割上分别实现奇、偶扇区，并用FeSe自旋涡旋与NiI$_2$周期四螺旋Wannier模型作材料验证，计算半无限边界的电学响应。

Result: 对称性保护的补偿选择垂直于磁矩平面的奇自旋织构；六个极大扇区完备覆盖边界织构；正交切割实现奇、偶两类扇区；FeSe与NiI$_2$模型给出材料实现。半无限边界呈互补电学响应：奇边界为垂直自旋积累，偶边界为面内自旋流响应。

Conclusion: 边界对称性把磁补偿与平衡自旋织构及电学自旋读出联系起来，为在补偿磁体中设计自旋极化边缘态和电学探测提供了统一框架。

Abstract: A boundary can release spin-polarized electronic states from a magnetically compensated, spin-degenerate layer. We establish the symmetry criterion governing this release in coplanar exchange magnets: the allowed even-parity edge-spin space equals the allowed uniform-magnetization space. Symmetry-protected compensation therefore selects an odd spin texture perpendicular to the moment plane. Six maximal sectors organize the possible edge textures. Orthogonal cuts of one exchange model realize odd and even sectors, while FeSe spin-vortex and NiI$_2$ period-four spiral Wannier models provide material realizations. Their semi-infinite boundaries exhibit complementary electrical responses: perpendicular spin accumulation at the odd edge and in-plane spin-current response at the even edge. Boundary symmetry thus connects magnetic compensation to equilibrium spin textures and electrical spin readout.

</details>


### [115] [Quantum-Metric-Induced Distinct Optoelectronic Responses in Triple $M$ Valleys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15145)
*Yongliang Song,Shuxian Sun,Zhichao Zhou,Xiao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出利用量子度规诱导的光电响应来区分和操控三重M谷：通过调节电场方向选择任意一对M谷的非线性霍尔电流实现非线性谷霍尔效应，并利用线偏振光选择性激发单个M谷，在反铁磁MoI3双层中得到第一性原理验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统谷电子学主要聚焦于光电性质相反且易于操控的K±谷，而M谷缺乏可靠的操控手段，导致大量含M谷的材料未被开发。因此需要寻找能够区分和操控M谷的新机制。

Method: 紧束缚计算分析量子度规诱导的非线性霍尔电流随电场取向的变化；结合线偏振光实现单谷优先激发与光致谷极化霍尔输运；并用第一性原理计算在反铁磁MoI3双层中验证相关响应。

Result: 三种M谷可通过量子度规诱导的光电响应加以区分；改变电场方向可使任意一对M谷产生相反的非线性霍尔电流，从而实现非线性谷霍尔效应；线偏振光可优先激发单个M谷并驱动光激发的谷极化霍尔输运；反铁磁MoI3双层表现出显著且可输运测量的非线性谷霍尔电导。

Conclusion: 研究将谷电子学从传统二元K±谷拓展到三重M谷，为设计具有多量子态、高可调性的先进光电器件提供了途径。

Abstract: While $K_{\pm}$ valleys with contrasting optoelectronic properties have been the main focus of valleytronics, the lack of reliable manipulation methods for $M$ valleys leaves numerous $M$-valley materials untapped. Here, we discover that three flavors of $M$ valleys become distinguishable via quantum-metric-induced optoelectronic responses. The tight-binding calculations demonstrate that by tuning the electric-field orientation, opposite nonlinear Hall currents from any pair of $M$ valleys can be chosen to realize the nonlinear valley Hall effect. Moreover, linearly polarized light allows for preferential excitation of individual $M$ valleys, driving photoexcited valley-polarized Hall transport. Using first-principles calculations, we further validate the quantum-metric-induced optoelectronic responses in antiferromagnetic $\mathrm{MoI}_3$ bilayers, which exhibit a significant nonlinear valley Hall conductivity amenable to transport measurements. Our findings extend the scope of valleytronics from conventional binary $K_{\pm}$ valleys to triple $M$ valleys and pave the way for designing advanced optoelectronic devices with multiple quantum states of high tunability.

</details>


### [116] [Interface-Driven Remote-Surface States in Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15192)
*Pierre A. Pantaleon,Alejandro Jimeno-Pozo,Francisco Guinea*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文揭示菱面堆垛多层石墨烯中低能态可经由逐层机制从一个表面传播到对侧表面，统一解释了外层面空位、圆孔及锯齿边缘零模以及莫尔界面能带的层极化对Bloch动量与调制周期的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱面多层石墨烯的低能态局域于两个外表面，但近期实验观测到对侧表面出现调制响应，其单粒子起源不明，亟需机制解释。

Method: 基于逐层递推的波函数分析，追踪由某一表面低能态在相邻层上的权重演化；对外层面空位计算波函数在逐层上的空间展布与归一化权重；将同一关系推广至圆孔束缚态、直锯齿终止的亚晶格极化零模；在周期莫尔界面对每个动量谐波分别应用该机制。

Result: 外层空位情形下波函数在后续层上覆盖面积增大，随体系尺寸增加归一化权重向远端表面转移；圆孔与锯齿终止的零模扩展遵循同一规律；莫尔界面处能带的层极化依赖Bloch动量与调制周期。

Conclusion: 逐层传播机制为近期多层石墨烯实验中观测到的远端表面调制提供了内在的单粒子解释。

Abstract: Rhombohedral multilayer graphene hosts low-energy states on its two outer surfaces. We identify a layer-by-layer mechanism through which a low-energy state generated at one surface acquires weight on successive layers up to the opposite surface. For an outer-layer vacancy, the wavefunction spreads over a larger area on successive layers, shifting normalized weight toward the remote surface as the system size increases. The same relation governs the extension of sublattice-polarized zero modes bound to circular voids and straight zigzag terminations. At periodic moire interfaces, it acts separately on each momentum harmonic, making the layer polarization of the low-energy bands depend on Bloch momentum and modulation period. This provides an intrinsic single-particle explanation for the remote-surface modulations observed in recent multilayer graphene experiments.

</details>


### [117] [Chirality-Odd Readout of Coherence-Mediated Magnon Spin Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15214)
*Yanmeng Lei,Ji Zou,Sanchar Sharma,Tao Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文预测铁磁/反铁磁螺旋磁体在电学自旋注入-探测构型中的手性响应：螺旋的螺旋对称性禁止磁振子自旋矩阵的对角元，自旋输运完全由不同模式叠加介导；翻转手性同时翻转磁振子自旋与注入量子相干，注入自旋流不变，而手性效应体现在探测界面，使磁振子积累读出电压反号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构手性在磁体中普遍存在，但其对绝缘体中自旋输运的影响几乎未被探索，仅有少数近期实验给出初步线索，亟需理论预言以明确手性在磁振子相干与电学读出中的作用机制。

Method: 采用理论分析方法，考察铁磁与反铁磁螺旋结构在自旋注入-探测（spin injection-detection）电学构型下的响应：分析螺旋的螺旋（screw）对称性对磁振子自旋矩阵的约束，计算手性翻转时磁振子自旋与注入量子相干的变化，并基于电子-磁振子散射振幅分析探测界面的读出机制；与多铁性BiFeO₃中电场切换下的第一谐波电压反号实验进行对照。

Result: 螺旋对称性使磁振子自旋矩阵所有对角元为零，每个本征模为线偏振且不携带自旋，自旋输运完全由不同模式间的叠加承担；翻转手性会同步翻转沿螺旋轴的磁振子自旋与注入的量子相干，但注入自旋流保持不变；手性效应实际作用于探测界面，电子-磁振子散射振幅随之反向，使磁振子积累被读出为符号相反的电压。该界面读出结果与多铁性BiFeO₃中电场切换下第一谐波电压反号的实验现象一致。

Conclusion: 结构手性可作为调控磁振子相干及其电学读出的内禀控制参量：注入端对整体手性不敏感，而探测界面的电子-磁振子散射是实现手性分辨电学读出的关键。

Abstract: Structural chirality is ubiquitous in magnets, yet its impact on spin transport in insulators remains largely unexplored, with only a few recent experiments offering the first glimpses. Here we predict the chiral response in the electric spin injection-detection configuration in both ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic spirals. The screw symmetry of the spiral forbids any nonzero diagonal element of the magnon spin matrix: every eigenmode is linearly polarized and carries no spin, so spin transport is mediated entirely by superposition between distinct modes. Reversing chirality flips the magnon spin along the spiral axis and the injected quantum coherence in lockstep, leaving the associated injected spin current invariant. Spiral chirality acts instead at the detection interface, where electron--magnon scattering amplitude is reversed and causes the magnon accumulation to be read out as a voltage of opposite sign. This interfacial readout is consistent with reversed first-harmonic voltage under electric-field switching in multiferroic BiFeO$_3$, establishing structural chirality as an intrinsic control parameter for magnon coherence and its electrical readout.

</details>


### [118] [Steering Skyrmion Diffusion with Chiral Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15253)
*Palina Shaban,Ulrich Pototschnig,Gulnaz Rakhmanova,Mikhail Lemeshko,Ragheed Alhyder*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 手性分子层通过对界面磁相互作用的调制，对磁斯格明子产生随手性反向的横向力，并重塑其扩散行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性分子吸附于磁性薄膜可与其磁化耦合并影响斯格明子热运动，但缺乏描述该耦合如何经由界面磁相互作用转化为斯格明子受力的理论框架。

Method: 建立理论模型：一层取向倾斜的分子按其手性改变界面磁相互作用强度；由此推导斯格明子仅响应耦合的空间变化（均匀层无影响，边界与覆盖梯度产生力）；对一维周期势场景用集体坐标做精确分析，并结合随机微磁模拟与集体坐标计算验证。

Result: 在手性区域附近斯格明子被横向偏转，且偏转方向在两种对映体间反转；周期图案中沿势变化方向的扩散被抑制、垂直方向扩散被增强，净扩散效应由回旋响应与耗散响应的平衡决定；模拟与集体坐标计算均验证上述预测。

Conclusion: 分子手性可通过界面磁耦合非接触地操控斯格明子的轨迹与扩散，分子取向、空间图案与斯格明子本征性质共同决定其运动，为手性—磁耦合的可观测特征提供判据。

Abstract: Chiral molecules adsorbed on a magnetic film interact with its magnetization and can thereby alter the thermal motion of magnetic skyrmions. Here we develop a theoretical model in which an aligned layer of tilted molecules modifies the interfacial magnetic interactions according to its handedness. Within this model a skyrmion responds only to spatial variations of the coupling, so a uniform layer leaves its position unaffected, whereas boundaries and coverage gradients exert forces on it. Near a chiral region, these forces deflect the skyrmion transversely in a direction that reverses between enantiomers, which provides a direct signature of the chiral interaction. In extended periodic patterns, the same forces reshape the long-time diffusion, suppressing it in the direction in which the potential varies and enhancing it in the perpendicular direction. The net effect on the mean diffusion is set by the balance between the gyrotropic and dissipative responses of the skyrmion, as we show exactly for one-dimensional landscapes. Stochastic micromagnetic simulations and collective-coordinate calculations confirm these predictions and show how molecular orientation, spatial patterning, and skyrmion properties together determine the motion.

</details>


### [119] [Directed Domain Wall Motion by Radio-Frequency Currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15305)
*Lukas Fischer,Rouven Dreyer,Simon Arnold,Jae-Chun Jeon,Georg Woltersdorf,Stuart S. P. Parkin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 射频电流通过自旋轨道力矩激发低频畴壁振荡,在对称性破缺下被整流为定向运动,并可用于辅助磁化翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直流驱动下的自旋轨道力矩畴壁运动已被广泛研究,但射频电流与畴壁的耦合机制、以及对称周期驱动能否产生定向运动尚不清楚。

Method: 采用时间分辨与静态磁光克尔显微镜,在射频电流激励下观测磁畴壁的振荡行为,并分别通过横向磁场和初始电流脉冲引入对称性破缺。

Result: 射频自旋轨道力矩可在远低于铁磁共振频率处高效激发低频畴壁振荡;无扰动时振荡保持对称,横向磁场打破对称性导致自驱动,初始电流脉冲打破对称性则产生持续畴壁运动;在初始均匀磁态中,脉冲产生的边缘畴在射频驱动下扩张,实现高效的射频辅助磁化翻转。

Conclusion: 建立了将射频驱动畴壁振荡整流为定向运动的通用理论框架,为畴壁的高频调控提供了新途径。

Abstract: Magnetic domain walls (DWs) are central to emerging spintronic technologies for memory, logic and neuromorphic computing, where their controlled displacement is a key functionality. While current-induced DW motion driven by spin-orbit torques is well established under direct currents, it remains unclear how radio-frequency (RF) currents couple to DWs and whether they can generate directed motion under a symmetric periodic drive. Here we show that RF currents drive DW oscillations, which can be rectified into directed motion through symmetry breaking. Using time-resolved and static magneto-optical Kerr microscopy, we demonstrate that RF spin-orbit torques efficiently excite low-frequency DW oscillations well below the ferromagnetic resonance frequency. Whereas these oscillations remain symmetric in the absence of perturbations, symmetry breaking by a transverse magnetic field produces self-propulsion, while symmetry breaking by an initial current pulse gives rise to sustained DW motion. We further show that in initially uniform magnetic states, RF-driven expansion of a pulse-generated edge domain ultimately results in efficient RF-assisted magnetic switching. Together, these results establish a general framework for understanding how RF-driven DW oscillations are rectified into directed motion, providing new opportunities for their high-frequency control.

</details>
