### [120] [Matrix-aware 2D field-effect biosensing of cardiac troponin I using complementary ion-sensitive transduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15347)
*Subhabrata Das,Bharti,Babulu Pradhan,Abhishek R Nath,Kashish Sapra,Jaydeep sharma,Vijayender Bhalla,Satyaprakash Sahoo,Manish Mathew,Md. Ehesan Ali,Kaushik Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文开发了MoS2 2D-FET与石墨烯ISFET组成的双传感器平台，用于复杂生物基质中心肌肌钙蛋白I的检测，并借助机器学习补偿血清离子强度造成的信号失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病是全球主要死因，亟需在床旁实现快速、灵敏、可靠的心肌损伤检测；而FET生物传感器在血清等高离子强度基质中响应被严重削弱（约80%衰减），限制了实际应用。

Method: 构建适配体功能化MoS2场效应晶体管（2D-FET）用于Trop-I高灵敏识别，同时引入石墨烯基离子敏场效应晶体管（ISFET）表征样品的离子环境；采用机器学习辅助融合两种传感器响应，补偿血清诱导的信号畸变，并恢复2D-FET转移特性中的关键电学品质因数（Ids_max、SS、gm）。

Result: MoS2 2D-FET对Trop-I的检测限达0.001 ng mL-1；血清高离子强度导致约80%的场效应响应退化；经ISFET辅助与机器学习融合后，可有效校正基质效应并恢复关键电学参数。

Conclusion: 该双传感器架构整合了分子识别、高分析灵敏度与基质感知的电信号校正，为复杂生物流体中更可靠的FET生物标志物检测提供路径，并可通过引入靶标特异性适配体拓展至心脏生物标志物的多重检测。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diseases remain a leading cause of mortality worldwide, highlighting the need for rapid, sensitive and reliable detection of cardiac injury at the point of care. Here, we develop a dual-sensor platform that combines an aptamer-functionalized molybdenum disulfide (MoS2) field-effect transistor (2D-FET) with a graphene-based ion-sensitive field-effect transistor (ISFET) for the detection of cardiac troponin I (Trop-I) in complex biological matrices. The atomically thin MoS2 channel enables highly sensitive detection of Trop-I, achieving a limit of detection of 0.001 ng mL-1. However, the high ionic strength of serum substantially attenuates the field-effect response, resulting in approximately 80% degradation of the sensor response. To address this matrix effect, the ISFET is employed as a secondary sensor to characterize the ionic environment of the sample. Machine-learning-assisted integration of the 2D-FET and ISFET responses is investigated as a strategy for compensating serum-induced signal distortion and recovering key electrical figures of merit extracted from the 2D-FET transfer characteristics, including maximum on-state current (Ids_max), subthreshold slope (SS) and transconductance (gm). This dual-sensor architecture combines molecular recognition, high analytical sensitivity and matrix-aware electrical signal correction, providing a potential pathway towards more reliable FET-based biomarker detection in complex biological fluids. The approach may further enable multiplexed detection of cardiac biomarkers through incorporation of target-specific aptamers, offering potential for broader assessment of cardiac stress and myocardial injury risk.

</details>


### [121] [Nonlocal multipolar spin accumulation in altermagnets beyond d-wave](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15438)
*J. W. Gonzalez*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在自旋守恒的 g/i 波交错磁体中，均匀纵向电导没有自旋奇分量；点注入器的非局域自旋积累可探测费米速度的高阶矩，其角谐波阶数、径向标度 r^{2-m} 与幅度标度 τ^{m-3} 由首个非零自旋奇速度矩的秩 m 决定，量子输运证实了径向轮廓且幅度超玻尔兹曼预测 15–65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀纵向电导在自旋守恒的 g、i 波交错磁体中缺少自旋奇分量，因此常规输运难以表征交错磁序；需寻找能揭示高阶费米速度矩（即序参量对称性）的非局域探针。

Method: 采用玻尔兹曼输运理论分析点注入器下的非局域自旋积累，推导首个非零自旋奇速度矩秩 m 与角谐波阶数、渐近径向依赖及 τ 标度的关系；并用有限几何玻尔兹曼理论与量子输运模拟（含无序实现）进行对照，在双端接触条带中检验 g 波四次谐波。

Result: m=2,4,6 分别决定主导角谐波、径向渐近 r^{2-m} 与幅度标度 τ^{m-3}；g 波序的四阶矩给出四次角谐波及 r^{-2} 尾。量子模拟在无幅度拟合下复现有限几何玻尔兹曼预言的径向轮廓，各对称性下量子幅度较基于玻恩散射时间的预测高 15–65%；在 1800 个无序实现中以 6.8σ 检出 g 波四次谐波，且幅度随交错磁劈裂减小而下降。

Conclusion: 非局域点注入自旋积累是探测交错磁体高阶速度矩与序对称性的有效手段，其角谐波与径向标度由矩秩唯一确定；量子输运对玻尔兹曼径向预测的良好一致性及显著的四次谐波信号支持该框架，并为 g 波交错磁序提供可测判据。

Abstract: In spin-conserving models of $g$- and $i$-wave altermagnets, the uniform longitudinal conductivity has no spin-odd component. A point injector reveals higher moments of the Fermi velocity through the nonlocal spin accumulation. Boltzmann theory predicts that the rank $m=2,4,6$ of the first nonzero spin-odd velocity moment sets the leading angular harmonic, its asymptotic radial dependence $r^{2-m}$, and its amplitude scaling $τ^{m-3}$ at fixed distance and injected current. For $g$-wave order, the fourth moment produces a fourth angular harmonic with an asymptotic $r^{-2}$ tail. Quantum transport simulations support the radial profiles predicted by finite-geometry Boltzmann theory without an amplitude fit. Across the three symmetries, the quantum amplitudes exceed the Boltzmann predictions based on the Born scattering time by $15-65$\% in the windows analyzed. In a two-contact ribbon, we detect the $g$-wave fourth harmonic at $6.8\,σ$ in an independent set of 1800 disorder realizations using a preselected radial window. Its amplitude decreases when the altermagnetic splitting is reduced.

</details>


### [122] [Symmetry-Enabled Ferroelectric Valley Transduction in Rhombohedral Bilayer WSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15494)
*Katsunori Wakabayashi,Souren Adhikary,Tomoaki Kameda,Kazuhito Tsukagoshi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文指出菱面体堆垛双层WSe₂中三重旋转对称禁止K谷层间隧穿而Γ谷仍强杂化，从而把铁电极化的层不对称转化为K谷边的移动（最大约\polshiftdft meV），并预言两种极化寄存器在零位移场下具有等值反号的微分跨导g_d，理想对称极限下每个寄存器的补偿场处g_d为零（谷依赖屏蔽仅使其偏离约\gdnulllow%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电极化虽与双层中的层自由度耦合，但要使谷选择性响应成立，两个谷必须具有不同的层间特征；此前缺乏将堆垛选择定则与铁电谷响应联系起来的定量与可测输运判据。

Method: 基于菱面体双层WSe₂的对称性分析（三重旋转抑制K谷层间隧穿、Γ谷强杂化），建立将有效铁电层不对称映射为K谷能量偏移的模型；进行受约束与不受约束的有限场拟合（后者给出K耦合≈0）；计算谷依赖屏蔽对g_d零点的影响。

Result: 不受约束拟合下K耦合与零一致；K谷移动达\polshiftdft meV（单谷由不对称可获得的最大值）；两种极化寄存器在零场下g_d等值反号；理想对称下每个寄存器补偿场处g_d为零，谷依赖屏蔽使该零点在读取点偏移仅\gdnulllow%，故精度受器件不对称限制。

Conclusion: 该工作将已知的堆垛选择定则与铁电谷换能联系起来，给出定量的谷偏移与可直接检验的输运特征（g_d的反对称与零点），并指出限制精度的是器件不对称而非谷屏蔽本身。

Abstract: Ferroelectric polarization couples to the layer degree of freedom in a bilayer, but a valley-selective energy response requires the valleys to carry different interlayer character. Rhombohedral bilayer WSe$_2$ meets that condition by symmetry: three-fold rotation forbids interlayer tunneling at the $K$ valley, while strong hybridization remains at $Γ$. The resulting contrast converts the band-effective ferroelectric layer asymmetry into a shift of the $K$ edge, and so displaces the $K$--$Γ$ landscape: a finite-field fit that does not impose the rule leaves the $K$ coupling consistent with zero, and the shift reaches \polshiftdft~meV, the most that asymmetry can give a single valley. Reversing the polarization mirrors the valley landscape in displacement field, so the two registries have equal and opposite differential transconductance $g_d$ at zero field. In the ideal symmetric limit a second parity holds about each registry's own compensation field and places a zero of $g_d$ there. The valley-dependent screening calculated here displaces that zero by only \gdnulllow\% at the read point, so device asymmetry limits the accuracy. The result links a known stacking selection rule to ferroelectric valley transduction, with a quantitative valley shift and a directly testable transport signature.

</details>


### [123] [Filling and Sealing Carbon Nanotubes by Local Anodic Oxidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15567)
*Ruiqing Zang,Jianan Xiao,Jacob Bair,Ian Wang,Chuyue Peng,Matthias Kuehne*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在AFM中利用电容耦合局部阳极氧化（LAO）切割碳纳米管时，会意外地一步完成"切割—填充—密封"，使管段内部被水填充并在干燥氩气氛下长期密封。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳纳米管是研究极端受限物质输运的理想纳米流体通道，但多数合成产物端部封闭，需额外开管/填充步骤；若研究管内封装物，还需密封以防干燥或真空环境下逸出。LAO已被证明可在指定位置切割单根CNT，但切割是否导致开口、填充或密封一直不明确。

Method: 采用AFM电容耦合局部阳极氧化对单根CNT定点切割；利用空间分辨微区拉曼光谱表征切割后管段，重点分析最内壁径向呼吸类模式（RBLM）的蓝移与展宽；并结合140 °C高真空退火实验考察封装物的释放行为。

Result: LAO切割意外地产生了被填充且保持密封的CNT管段，在干燥Ar气氛下可稳定数周。最内壁RBLM的蓝移与展宽幅度与水填充一致，表明填充源为AFM针尖与基底之间凝聚的水弯月面。140 °C高真空退火可使部分（而非全部）管段释放封装物，说明存在可热克服的势垒，而非端帽重新形成。

Conclusion: LAO切割具备一步式"切割—填充—密封"能力，为按直径分辨研究纳米受限水及水溶液、以及为真空分析技术提供仄升（zeptoliter）级样品递送提供了新途径。

Abstract: Carbon nanotubes (CNTs) are prime nanofluidic conduits and nanopores for studying matter under extreme confinement. Since most synthesis routes yield CNTs with closed ends, opening and filling strategies are required to access their interior for fluid and ion transport, whereas studies of the encapsulated species themselves may additionally require sealing to prevent their escape in dry or vacuum environments. Capacitively coupled local anodic oxidation (LAO) in an atomic force microscope (AFM) has been shown to cut individual CNTs at predefined positions, but whether cutting induces opening, filling, or sealing has remained unclear. Using spatially resolved micro-Raman spectroscopy, we find that LAO cutting surprisingly produces filled CNT segments that remain sealed under dry Ar atmosphere for weeks. The magnitudes of the blueshift and broadening of the innermost-wall radial breathing-like mode are consistent with water filling, pointing to the water meniscus condensed between the AFM tip and the substrate as the source of CNT filling during cutting. High-vacuum annealing at 140 °C releases the encapsulated species from some, but not all, segments, indicating a thermally surmountable barrier rather than re-formed end caps. This one-step cut-fill-seal capability offers a route to diameter-resolved studies of nanoconfined water and aqueous solutions, and to zeptoliter-scale sample delivery for vacuum-based analytical techniques.

</details>


### [124] [Single Spin Hall Conversion in the Hall Response of Magnetic-Insulator/Heavy-Metal Bilayers Identified by Odd Spin-Hall-Angle Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15571)
*Akashdeep Akashdeep,Ewiese Mohammad Ababneh,Botros Gerges Mikhail Fenhas,Duc Minh Tran,Mathias Kläui,Steven S. -L. Zhang,Vivek Amin,Gerhard Jakob,Timo Kuschel*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在YIG/Ta与YIG/Pt双层中通过磁场窗口分离霍尔电压，发现存在自旋霍尔角奇宇称的霍尔响应，并以含界面自旋流的漂移-扩散模型解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁绝缘体/重金属双层霍尔响应通常被认为源于两次相继的自旋霍尔转换，因而应为自旋霍尔角的偶函数；作者检验这一传统图像是否完备。

Method: 选取Ta与Pt（自旋霍尔角相反）的YIG双层，利用不同磁场区间分离出磁场驱动进动、磁化驱动进动及普通霍尔效应三部分贡献，并用含界面产生自旋流的漂移-扩散模型进行拟合分析，同时评估有限虚自旋混合电导替代解释的可行性。

Result: 磁场驱动分量在Ta与Pt间反号，表明霍尔响应为自旋霍尔角奇宇称；磁化驱动分量在Ta与Pt间亦反号，仅最薄重金属层除外。模型表明磁场驱动分量主要由单次自旋霍尔转换携带，磁化驱动分量亦与之相符；虚自旋混合电导解释需异常大数值，故不可信。

Conclusion: 霍尔响应并非简单的两次自旋霍尔转换，而包含奇宇称成分；界面自旋流图像可统一描述，推进了对该类双层霍尔响应的更一般理解。

Abstract: The Hall response of magnetic-insulator/heavy-metal bilayers is usually ascribed to two successive spin Hall conversions and is therefore expected to be even in the spin Hall angle. Using distinct magnetic-field windows, we separate the Hall voltage of yttrium iron garnet bilayers with Ta and Pt of opposite spin Hall angle into contributions from magnetization- and magnetic-field-driven precession as well as from ordinary Hall effect. The field-driven component reverses sign between Ta and Pt, revealing a Hall response odd-in-spin-Hall-angle. The magnetization-driven component also has opposite signs for Ta and Pt, except for the thinnest heavy-metal layers. A drift-diffusion model with interface-generated spin currents describes this behavior: the field-driven component is predominantly carried by a single spin Hall conversion, and the magnetization-driven component is consistent with one as well. Other potential explanations, based on a description with a finite imaginary spin-mixing conductance rather than interface-generated spin currents, require unusually large values of this conductance, making this mechanism unlikely. Our findings advance the understanding of Hall responses in magnetic-insulator/heavy-metal bilayers toward a more general description.

</details>


### [125] [Non-Bloch Topology of Frustrated Non-Hermitian Flat Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15863)
*Fan Yang,Jiong-Hao Wang,Hui Liu,Emil J. Bergholtz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文构造了一类二维阻挫非厄米晶格模型，其具有严格平带并可在非Bloch能带理论框架下自然描述，揭示了平带在非厄米趋肤效应下的本征基无关趋肤、奇异性及非Bloch拓扑体-边对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阻挫晶格中严格平带与紧致局域态为研究平带拓扑提供理想平台；但由厄米推广至非厄米体系后，非厄米趋肤效应会剧烈改变能带结构，且在一维之外理解不足，二维非厄米平带的性质尚不清楚。

Method: 构造一大类二维阻挫非厄米晶格模型（严格平带），以非厄米呼吸Kagome模型与棋盘模型为例，采用非Bloch能带理论分析；通过几何方法由紧致局域趋肤模式的宏观叠加构造边界趋肤模，并借助非Bloch环面上的两条线性无关不可收缩环路态追溯拓扑起源，提出基于零能角模双正交极化的不变量。

Result: 发现平带可因其他能带诱导而获得本征基无关的趋肤效应；在呼吸Kagome模型中，该效应恰在平带进入色散带周期边界谱所张区域时出现；当平带本征矢在能带交叉处具有不可移动间断时平带变为奇异，且此奇异对边界条件敏感、不受平带平坦性保护；体-边对应表现为体内奇异带接触与边界稳健平带趋肤模，后者可追溯至非Bloch环面上两条不可收缩环路态。此外，破坏严格平坦性可实现近平坦非Bloch陈带。

Conclusion: 二维非厄米阻挫平带可由非Bloch能带理论统一描述，其平带趋肤、奇异性与非Bloch拓扑构成可表征的体-边对应；所提双正交极化不变量为刻画该对应提供有效手段，并可推广至近平坦非Bloch陈带。

Abstract: Frustrated lattices with exactly flat bands and compact localized states provide an ideal setting to uncover the topology of flat bands, leading to intriguing physics in two dimensions. Extending from Hermitian to non-Hermitian systems, however, band structures are drastically changed by the non-Hermitian skin effect, which is less well understood beyond one dimension. Here, we construct a broad class of two-dimensional frustrated non-Hermitian lattice models with exactly flat bands which can be naturally described in a non-Bloch band theory. Using non-Hermitian breathing Kagome and checkerboard models as illustrations, we reveal how flat bands acquire a basis-independent skin effect induced by the other bands; in the breathing Kagome model it appears exactly when the flat band enters the area spanned by the periodic-boundary spectra of the dispersive bands. A flat band becomes singular when its eigenvectors hold immovable discontinuities at band crossings. Such singularity in non-Hermitian systems is sensitive to boundary conditions and unprotected by band flatness. Non-Bloch topology underlying is uncovered in a bulk-boundary correspondence with the formation of a singular band touching in the bulk and a robust flat band skin mode on the boundary. We present a geometric approach to construct this boundary skin mode from a macroscopic sum of compact localized skin modes and trace its topological origin to two linearly independent noncontractible loop states on a non-Bloch torus. To characterize the correspondence, an invariant is proposed based on biorthogonal polarization of the zero-energy corner mode. Breaking exact flatness, we further realize nearly flat non-Bloch Chern bands.

</details>


### [126] [Emergence of Phonon Hydrodynamics as a Non-Hermitian Symmetry-Breaking Phase Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16161)
*Xin Qian,Guanda Quan,Ning Wang,Yaoming Chu,Run Hu,Te-Huan Liu,Xiangfan Xu,Jianming Cai,Ronggui Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 将声子流体动力学的起始表述为非厄米动力学相变：从Peierls-Boltzmann方程导出温度场与声子漂移场的有效薛定谔方程，用一对奇异点定量界定流体动力学窗口的下、上边界，并给出解析判据（有效热扩散率大于声子动量扩散率），结合第一性原理输入定量复现超快第二声实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声子流体动力学可实现超越经典扩散极限的集体热输运，是低维材料与纳米器件热调控的重要途径；但"何时进入该流体动力学态、其边界在哪"缺乏定量判据，长期仅有Guyer-Krumhansl六十年前提出的定性散射率不等式。

Method: 以Peierls-Boltzmann输运方程为起点，针对温度场与声子漂移场的耦合动力学推导等效薛定谔方程；将流体动力学区的出现映射为非厄米动力学转变，用一对奇异点（exceptional points）定义流体动力学窗口的上下边界；进一步解析推导流体输运存在的判据；并以第一性原理声子性质为输入进行定量验证。

Result: 一对奇异点定量给出流体动力学窗口的下界与上界，构成超越Guyer-Krumhansl定性不等式的临界设计准则；解析判据表明仅当有效热扩散率超过声子动量扩散率时才存在有限流体动力学窗口；结合第一性原理计算可准确复现实验观测到的随温度与长度变化的超快第二声动力学。

Conclusion: 建立了基于对称性的集体能量输运理解框架，把微观声子散射性质转化为波状热输运的预测性边界，为识别支持波状热输运的材料、温度与特征长度尺度提供定量设计原则。

Abstract: Phonon hydrodynamics enables collective heat flow beyond the classical diffusive limit and offers a promising route to controlling thermal transport in low-dimensional materials and nanoscale devices. However, quantitative criteria for determining when this hydrodynamic state emerges and where its boundaries lie remain a longstanding challenge in condensed matter physics. Here, we show that the onset of phonon hydrodynamics can be formulated as a non-Hermitian dynamical transition that converts microscopic phonon scattering properties into predictive boundaries for wavelike heat transport. Starting from the Peierls-Boltzmann transport equation, we derive an effective Schrodinger equation for the coupled dynamics of temperature and phonon drift fields, allowing the hydrodynamic regime to be identified directly from material transport properties. Within this framework, a pair of exceptional points quantitatively define the lower and upper boundaries of the hydrodynamic window, providing a critical design criterion beyond the qualitative scattering-rate inequalities proposed by Guyer and Krumhansl six decades ago. Furthermore, we derive an analytical criterion for the existence of hydrodynamic transport, showing that a finite hydrodynamic window can be supported only when the effective thermal diffusivity exceeds the phononic momentum diffusivity. Using ab initio phonon properties as inputs, our theory accurately captures the experimentally observed temperature- and length-dependent ultrafast second-sound dynamics. These results establish a symmetry-based framework for understanding collective energy transport and provide quantitative design principles for identifying the materials, temperatures, and characteristic length scales that support wavelike heat transport in low-dimensional materials and functional devices.

</details>


### [127] [Controlled Growth and Disorder Mitigation of GaAs1-xSbx/InAs Core-Shell Quantum Wires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16203)
*Ran Zhuo,Huading Song,Yanlin Wu,Wenlong Yang,Haiyan Shi,Xiyu Hou,Sijun Chen,Zhichuan Wang,Donghai Li,Jinjin Bai,Runan Shang,Hao Zhang,Dong Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在GaAs1-xSbx/InAs核壳纳米线中引入Sb降低核壳晶格失配，使晶体质量与电学性能显著提升，并观察到类Aharonov-Bohm振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GaAs-InAs核壳纳米线体系存在较大晶格失配，会引入堆垛层错等缺陷，成为器件中的无序来源，限制晶体质量与器件性能。作者希望寻找一种能降低核壳失配的途径来减小无序。

Method: 生长GaAs1-xSbx/InAs核壳纳米线，将Sb组份x从0.15提高到0.35以减小核壳晶格失配；通过结构表征统计堆垛层错等缺陷密度，并进行场效应输运测量（迁移率、夹断特性）以及磁电导测量。

Result: x从0.15增至0.35时，InAs壳中堆垛层错等晶体缺陷密度减半；场效应迁移率由3850 cm²/Vs提升至4800 cm²/Vs，与失配降低一致；纳米线更易被栅压夹断，电场调控能力增强；磁电导显示类Aharonov-Bohm振荡，表明InAs壳为相位相干的管状导电通道。

Conclusion: 晶格失配是核壳纳米线中的关键无序来源；通过Sb合金化降低失配可有效提升晶体质量与器件性能，为低无序核壳量子器件提供了一条可行途径。

Abstract: We report the growth and transport properties of GaAs1-xSbx/InAs core-shell nanowires. Compared with the well-studied GaAs-InAs system, incorporating Sb substantially reduces the lattice mismatch between the core and shell. Accordingly, both the nanowire crystal quality and device performance improved significantly. Increasing x from 0.15 to 0.35, the density of crystalline defects, such as stacking faults in the InAs shell, decreases by half. Moreover, the field-effect mobility increases from 3850 cm2/Vs to 4800 cm2/Vs, consistent with the reduction of the lattice mismatch. The incorporation of Sb makes the nanowire easier to pinch off via the gate voltage, enhancing the electric field tunability over the nanowire. Magneto-conductance measurements reveal Aharonov-Bohm-like oscillations, suggesting a phase-coherent tubular InAs shell. Our results identify lattice mismatch as a critical disorder source in core-shell nanowires and provide a practical route toward low-disorder core-shell quantum devices.

</details>


### [128] [High-Quality InSb Nanowires Grown by Molecular-Beam Epitaxy via Precise Catalyst Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16234)
*Wenlong Yang,Jiaye Xu,Shuai Yang,Wenyu Song,Haiyan Shi,Donghai Li,Xiyu Hou,Sijun Chen,Ke He,Hao Zhang,Dong Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作报道了一种无外来催化剂的自催化MBE生长InSb纳米线方法：先生长GaAs纳米线茎，再原位将Ga液滴转化为In液滴，实现InSb轴向异质外延生长，并获得高迁移率、无孪晶缺陷的高质量纳米线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: InSb纳米线是拓扑量子计算的重要候选材料，但现有器件多用MOCVD生长，无法与超导体的原位生长兼容，而低无序器件恰恰需要这一能力。MBE可解决该问题，但MBE生长InSb的工艺窗口极窄、重复性差，且使用外来催化剂会引入深能级杂质，因此研究有限。

Method: 采用自催化MBE方法：先制备GaAs纳米线茎，随后原位将Ga催化剂液滴转化为In液滴，借助顶部In液滴在GaAs茎上实现InSb的轴向异质外延生长；并研究通量对径向生长的调控，通过低通量抑制径向生长。

Result: 低通量下可抑制径向生长，使长度随生长时间从0.8 μm线性调控至2.6 μm，而直径基本不变；所得InSb纳米线为纯闪锌矿结构，无孪晶和层错；输运测量显示电子迁移率约19000 cm² V⁻¹s⁻¹，并观测到准弹道输运、量子点与Kondo关联，证明其具备相干量子输运能力。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条不依赖衬底、自催化的高质量InSb纳米线可控合成路线，为高性能InSb基电子与量子器件建立了稳健的材料平台。

Abstract: Semiconductor InSb nanowires are a major material candidate for topological quantum computation. The nanowires in current devices were mostly grown using metal organic chemical vapor deposition, incompatible with in situ growth of superconductor, a crucial requirement for low-disorder devices. Growth of InSb nanowires using molecular-beam epitaxy (MBE) can address this issue. So far, studies on MBE-grown InSb remain limited due to an extremely narrow parameter window for the growth and reproducibility. Moreover, the use of foreign catalysts can introduce deep-level impurities. Here, we report a self-catalyzed MBE growth of InSb nanowires without those foreign catalysts. We first grow GaAs nanowire stems, and then convert Ga catalyst droplets into In droplets in situ. Axial heteroepitaxial growth of InSb can be realized through the In droplets on top of GaAs stems. We further find that a low flux suppresses radial growth, enabling linear length tuning from 0.8 to 2.6 micrometer with growth time, while diameter stays nearly constant. These InSb nanowires have a pure zinc-blende structure free of twins, stacking faults. Transport measurement reveals an electron mobility near 19000 cm2 V-1s-1. Moreover, the observation of quasi-ballistic transport, quantum dots, and Kondo correlations demonstrates the coherent quantum transport capability of the MBE-grown InSb nanowires. Our work provides a substrate-independent, self-catalyzed route for the controllable synthesis of high-quality InSb nanowires, establishing a robust material platform for high-performance InSb-based electronic and quantum devices.

</details>


### [129] [Ferroelectric control of exciton photoluminescence in rhombohedrally stacked bilayer WSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16475)
*Wenlong Zhong,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Xiaoxiang Xi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在近零扭转菱方堆叠的人工WSe2双层中，通过激子光致发光观察到可逆、滞回的电致极化翻转，并解析出四阶段能带演化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑动铁电为电可编程光电功能提供非易失路径，但激子发光的确定性调控常被莫尔重构与畴平均所掩盖，需要一种能明确读出极化状态与能带结构的手段。

Method: 构建近零扭转、菱方堆叠的人工WSe2双层，施加面外电场，测量动量间接QK激子发光、动量直接KK层间激子与层内激子杂化行为，并结合共振拉曼散射作为互补读出。

Result: QK激子发射呈现斯塔克斜率反转与能量突变，区分出两次能带重排与一次铁电翻转构成的四阶段序列；极化反转选择性点亮与层内激子杂化的KK层间激子分支，产生历史依赖发光；共振拉曼强度滞回与极化相关层间电场一致。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了基于激子的铁电WSe2电子结构定量表征方法，并为电可编程光电子功能提供途径。

Abstract: Sliding ferroelectricity offers a non-volatile route to electrically programmable electronic and optical functionality, but deterministic control of excitonic photoluminescence is often obscured by moiré reconstruction and domain averaging. Here we study an artificially assembled WSe$_2$ bilayer with near-zero twist in rhombohedral stacking and observe reversible, hysteretic electrical switching through excitonic photoluminescence. Under an out-of-plane electric field, momentum-indirect QK exciton emission exhibits Stark-slope reversals and abrupt energy jumps, resolving a four-stage sequence separated by two band realignments and one ferroelectric switching event. Polarization reversal also selects the momentum-direct KK interlayer-exciton branch that hybridizes with an intralayer exciton, producing history-dependent photoluminescence. Ferroelectric modulation of resonant Raman scattering provides a complementary readout, with hysteretic intensities correlated with the polarization-dependent interlayer field. These results establish an exciton-based quantification of the electronic structure in ferroelectric WSe$_2$ and open a route to electrically programmable optoelectronic functionality.

</details>


### [130] [Micromagnetic modelling and simulation of inertial magnetization dynamics in ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16567)
*Massimiliano d'Aquino*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该综述文章系统讨论了铁磁纳米结构中惯性磁化动力学的理论建模与微磁模拟，从惯性LLG方程的力学类比出发，介绍其空间非均匀微磁公式、惯性自旋波的线性动力学，以及频域与时域微磁模拟技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚皮秒时间尺度的磁化动力学会表现出超越传统Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert描述的惯性效应，产生太赫兹频率的章动运动；在铁磁体中该效应已获理论预言与实验观测，磁惯性因此成为理解和调控超快自旋动力学的研究热点，亟需对其理论建模与模拟方法进行系统梳理。

Method: 从惯性LLG方程所基于的力学类比出发，推导其空间非均匀的微磁学表述，分析磁化长度守恒、角动量弛豫与广义自由能平衡等主要性质；综述惯性自旋波的线性动力学（GHz进动支与THz章动支共存、进动模的惯性红移、谱线宽，以及交换作用、静磁相互作用、限域、曲率与扭率的影响），重点讨论大波数区间中惯性导致的有限极限自旋波传播速度；最后介绍面向空间非均匀惯性动力学的频域与时域微磁模拟技术，包括保持连续动力学基本约束与能量性质的几何中点积分器。

Result: 文章阐明惯性LLG理论下GHz进动支与THz章动支的共存关系及进动模的惯性红移效应；指出在大波数区间磁惯性使自旋波传播速度趋于有限极限值；并通过超短章动自旋波的激发与传播、超快惯性磁化翻转等应用实例，展示了微磁建模在连接惯性LLG理论与实验可及的纳米尺度磁化动力学方面的作用。

Conclusion: 微磁模拟是连接惯性Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert理论与实验可观测纳米尺度超快磁化动力学的关键桥梁；保持连续动力学约束与能量性质的几何积分方法为空间非均匀惯性动力学的可靠模拟提供了保证，并支撑THz章动自旋波激发与超快惯性磁化翻转等应用研究。

Abstract: Magnetization dynamics at sub-picosecond time scales can exhibit inertial effects beyond the conventional Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert description, giving rise to nutational motion at terahertz frequencies. Following its theoretical prediction and experimental observation in ferromagnets, magnetic inertia has attracted increasing interest for understanding and controlling ultrafast spin dynamics. In this topical review, we discuss the theoretical modelling and micromagnetic simulation of inertial magnetization dynamics in ferromagnetic nanostructures. Starting from the mechanical analogy underlying the inertial Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation, we introduce its spatially inhomogeneous micromagnetic formulation and discuss its main properties, including magnetization-length conservation, angular-momentum relaxation, and the generalized free-energy balance. We then review the linear dynamics of inertial spin waves, highlighting the coexistence of GHz precessional and THz nutational branches, the inertial redshift of precessional modes, spectral linewidths, and the effects of exchange, magnetostatic interactions, confinement, curvature and torsion. Particular attention is devoted to the large-wavenumber regime, where magnetic inertia leads to a finite limiting spin-wave propagation velocity. Finally, we address frequency- and time-domain micromagnetic simulation techniques for spatially nonuniform inertial dynamics, including geometric midpoint integrators preserving the fundamental constraints and energy properties of the continuous dynamics. Applications to the excitation and propagation of ultrashort nutational spin waves and ultrafast inertial magnetization switching illustrate the role of micromagnetic modelling in connecting inertial Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert theory with experimentally accessible nanoscale magnetization dynamics.

</details>


### [131] [A superconducting quantum point contact in Ge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16705)
*Anna Tacchini,Inga Seidler,Isabell Grandt-Ionita,Thibault Thuegaz,Stephen W. Bedell,Fabrizio Nichele*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在Ge/SiGe中实现栅极可调的半导体超导量子点接触(SQPC)，并通过非对称SQUID获取其电流-相位关系与开关电流，观察到电导量子化与开关电流量子化的对应、近第一电导平台处的强栅控等效透射率，以及微波辐照下的光子辅助多重安德烈夫反射与Shapiro复制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合超导-半导体电路是研究介观弱连接中相干输运的平台，而SQPC的超电流由离散数量的弹道输运模式承载，故需在少模式、栅极可调体系中实现相敏测量，以将正常态输运模式与超导响应直接关联。

Method: 在Ge/SiGe异质结构中制备栅极可调QPC，将其嵌入高度非对称超导SQUID，从而提取器件的电流-相位关系(CPR)与开关电流，并结合微波辐照开展相敏与光子辅助输运测量。

Result: 观察到电导量子化与开关电流量子化的直接对应；在第一条电导平台起始处实现等效透射率的强栅极调控；微波辐照下，低透射率时出现光子辅助多重安德烈夫反射，高透明少模式区出现Shapiro复制。

Conclusion: Ge/SiGe基SQPC是可实现少模式、相敏超导输运研究的可调平台，能将弹道正常态模式与超导宏观量子响应定量联系，并可通过微波驱动区分少模式体系中的MAR与Shapiro物理。

Abstract: Hybrid superconducting-semiconducting circuits provide a versatile platform for investigating coherent transport in mesoscopic weak links. Superconducting quantum point contacts (SQPCs) are particularly interesting in this context, as their supercurrent is carried by a discrete number of ballistic transport modes. Here, we investigate a gate-tunable superconducting quantum point contact realized in Ge/SiGe. By incorporating the QPC into a highly asymmetric superconducting quantum interference device (SQUID), we gain access to its current-phase relation (CPR) and switching current, enabling phase-sensitive studies of few-mode superconducting transport. We observe a direct correspondence between conductance quantization and switching-current quantization, linking the normal-state transport modes to the superconducting response. In addition, we demonstrate strong gate tunability of the effective transmission near the onset of the first conductance plateau. Under microwave irradiation, the QPC exhibits photon-assisted multiple Andreev reflections at lower transparencies and Shapiro replicas in the high-transparency, few-mode regime.

</details>


### [132] [Interfacial Effects of Thermal Reorganization on Charge and Spin Transport in Organic Spin Valves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16852)
*Zihao Liu,Yunzhe Ke,Hajar Komekoz,Shuai Gu,Rico Ehrler,Chen Wang,Olav Hellwig,Frank Ortmann,Dietrich R. T. Zahn,Carsten Deibel,Michael Sommer,Shuaishuai Ding,Georgeta Salvan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究原位热退火对NDI基有机自旋阀电学与磁输运性能的影响，比较直接蒸镀Co/Au与转移石墨烯/Co/Au两种顶电极；退火虽提升体相有序度与有效纵向电导，却使磁阻响应消失，表明自旋阀性能受界面（spinterface）主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机半导体自旋轨道耦合弱、超精细相互作用小、柔性好、电子性质可调，适于自旋电子学；但有机自旋阀的磁阻响应对铁磁电极/有机半导体界面（spinterface）高度敏感，界面形貌、化学相互作用、能级排列与自旋极化度的变化会显著影响自旋注入、输运与探测，导致器件性能难以重复。

Method: 制备完整的NDI基有机自旋阀，对比两种顶电极构型：直接蒸镀Co/Au与转移石墨烯(Gr)/Co/Au；对器件进行原位热退火，测试其电学与磁输运特性，并用伴随薄膜的结构表征及电子单载流子器件提取有效纵向电导，以区分体相有序度与界面效应对完整器件响应的贡献。

Result: 两类as-cast器件均表现出清晰的磁阻响应，转移电极器件的绝对响应更强；原位退火后两类器件电阻均显著增大，且无法分辨出可重复的磁阻响应，尽管伴随薄膜结构有序度提高、电子单载流子器件提取的有效纵向电导提升约36倍。

Conclusion: 体相有序度的改善不足以确保自旋阀性能提升；退火引起的界面接触变化、接触均匀性与局域电流分布会成为决定完整器件响应的主导因素。结果凸显spinterface稳定性与界面工程对实现可靠、热稳定的有机自旋阀运行的关键作用。

Abstract: Organic semiconductors are attractive for spintronic applications owing to their weak spin-orbit coupling, small hyperfine interactions, mechanical flexibility, and tunable electronic properties. Organic spin valves (OSVs) provide a representative platform for investigating spin-dependent transport. However, the magnetoresistance (MR) response is highly sensitive to the ferromagnetic electrode/organic semiconductor interface (spinterface). Variations in interfacial morphology, chemical interaction, energy-level alignment, and spin polarization can strongly affect spin injection, transport, and detection, making reproducible OSV performance difficult to achieve. Here, we investigate how in situ thermal annealing affects the electrical and magnetotransport properties of complete NDI-based OSVs. Two top-electrode configurations are compared: directly evaporated Co/Au and transferred Gr (graphene)/Co/Au. Both as-cast devices exhibit clear MR responses, with a stronger absolute response observed for the transferred-electrode device. After in situ annealing, the resistance of both device types increases substantially, and no reproducible MR response is resolved, despite the enhanced structural order observed in companion films and an approximately 36-fold increase in the effective vertical conductivity extracted from electron-only devices. This contrasting behavior indicates that improved bulk order alone is insufficient to ensure enhanced spin-valve performance. Instead, annealing-induced changes in interfacial contact, contact homogeneity, and local current distribution can become dominant in determining the complete-device response. These results emphasize the critical role of spinterface stability and interface engineering in achieving reliable and thermally robust OSV operation.

</details>


### [133] [Squeezed terahertz light from nonlinear diamagnetism in ultrastrongly coupled two-dimensional electrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16924)
*Dasom Kim,Junichiro Kono,Stephen Hughes*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 二维电子气太赫兹腔中电子能带非抛物线型使体系成为量子Kerr振荡器，可压缩太赫兹光；预测现有GaAs样品在弱约束腔（Q~10²）下输出压缩1.8–2.4 dB，小幅改进可达约9 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维电子气太赫兹腔是最成熟的超强耦合平台之一，但其集体响应仅由线性Hopfield模型描述，该模型有精确的经典对应，无法体现量子非线性效应。作者希望利用近期由非线性抗磁性揭示的能带非抛物线型，赋予体系真正的量子非线性（Kerr效应），从而实现太赫兹光的量子压缩。

Method: 采用无旋转波近似（non-RWA）理论，配以与超强耦合自洽的输入-输出理论、ab initio腔浴模型以及电子加热的自洽模型，对能带非抛物线型诱导的Kerr非线性及输出压缩进行定量预测。

Result: 预测现有GaAs样品在弱约束、品质因数约10²的腔中可获1.8–2.4 dB输出压缩；在迁移率或腔频率适度改进下可提升至约9 dB。载流子加热通过材料品质因子设定限制，并给出最优腔品质因数与超强耦合强度成反比的设计规则。

Conclusion: 能带非抛物线型将成熟的超强耦合二维电子气平台转化为量子Kerr振荡器，是可实现的太赫兹压缩光源方案；其性能受载流子加热约束，并可用简单的材料品质因子与Q-耦合强度反比关系指导器件设计。

Abstract: Two-dimensional electron gases in terahertz cavities are among the most mature platforms for ultrastrong light--matter coupling, yet their collective response is captured by the linear Hopfield model, which has an exact classical correspondence. We show that the intrinsic band nonparabolicity of the electrons, recently revealed through nonlinear diamagnetism, turns this system into a quantum Kerr oscillator that squeezes terahertz light. Using a non-rotating-wave theory with ultrastrong-coupling-consistent input--output, an ab initio cavity bath, and a self-consistent model of electron heating, we predict output squeezing of 1.8--2.4\,dB with present GaAs samples in weakly confined cavities with quality factors of order $10^2$, and up to $\approx9$\,dB with modest improvements in mobility or cavity frequency. Carrier heating sets limits through a material figure of merit, which yields a simple design rule that the optimal cavity quality factor scales inversely with the ultrastrong-coupling strength.

</details>


### [134] [Achiral-Chiral Dissociation and Structural Zero of the Current in the Spinterface Model of CISS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16998)
*Seif Alwan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 将spinterface模型推广到自旋分辨耗散，揭示自旋电流的手性与非手性两部分在铅自旋不对称下解耦、最优工作点不具普适标度，并给出一个精确结构零点及其在非共线铁磁铅下线性-二次型形变层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性诱导自旋选择（CISS）的理论描述中，此前量子非平衡格林函数形式的spinterface模型仅处理自旋简并的铅展宽，缺乏对自旋分辨耗散如何影响手性/非手性输运通道的刻画，也无法判断耦合与偏压最优值是否为普适规律。

Method: 在Ref. Alwan2026QSpinterface的量子非平衡格林函数框架基础上，引入自旋分辨的铅展宽；把自旋电流分解为由界面磁矩驱动的非手性部分与由Rashba自旋翻转驱动的手性部分；解析分析其随铅自旋不对称的变化规律与对耦合/偏压的依赖；在共线铅构型下通过键矩阵秩一结构定位结构零点；在非共线铁磁铅下做微扰分析，考察横向极化导致的自旋翻转子过程及其干涉。

Result: 铅自旋不对称增大时非手性部分线性增长、手性部分按几何平均规律被抑制，两扇区发生解耦，且沿铅展宽在不同耦合尺度处各自达峰；耦合与偏压最优值只是参考构型的工作点而非普适标度律。共线铅构型下在λ_R sin(φ/2)=t_mol处出现结构零点，手性信号塌缩至机器精度，且对链长、频率分辨率、扭转角普遍成立；该零点源于秩一奇异键矩阵，其零本征矢量将电子困于不自旋跃迁的自旋叠加暗态。非共线铁磁铅下零点被可控破缺：残余量一般对横向极化成线性（源于边界处单次相干自旋翻转），在调谐抵消角处两边界贡献相消干涉变为二次，标度指数从1陡跳到2。

Conclusion: spinterface模型被确立为一个具有双轴解耦结构、非标度性工作最优值与精确结构零点的输运平台；其非共线形变呈干净的线性加二次层级，为CISS中手性信号的调控与判据提供了可解析、可验证的理论基准。

Abstract: We study the transport sector of the spinterface model of chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS) under spin-resolved dissipation, extending the quantum non-equilibrium Green's function formulation of Ref.~\cite{Alwan2026QSpinterface} to spin-resolved lead broadenings. The spin current decomposes into an achiral part, driven by the interfacial moment, and a chiral part, driven by the Rashba spin flip. Under increasing lead spin asymmetry, the achiral part grows linearly while the chiral part follows the geometric-mean suppression, so the two sectors dissociate; they also peak at different coupling scales along the lead broadening. The coupling and bias optima are operating points of the reference configuration, not universal scaling laws. A structural zero appears at $λ_R\sin(φ/2)=t_{\mathrm{mol}}$ in the collinear-lead configuration, where the chiral signal collapses to machine precision universally across chain lengths, frequency resolutions, and twist angles; the zero originates from the rank-one singular bond matrix, whose null eigenvector traps an electron in a spin-superposition dark state that does not hop. With non-collinear ferromagnetic leads, the zero is broken in a controlled way: the residual is generically linear in the transverse polarization, arising from a single coherent spin flip at a boundary, and becomes quadratic at a tuned cancellation angle where two boundary contributions interfere destructively, with the scaling exponent jumping sharply from one to two. These results establish the spinterface model as a transport platform with a two-axis dissociation structure, non-scaling operating optima, and an exact structural zero whose non-collinear deformation is a clean linear-plus-quadratic hierarchy.

</details>


### [135] [Reflection Zeros Set the Scattering Invariant of Finite Majorana Wires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17001)
*Alex Krasnok*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文证明零能电子反射符号 \(Q\) 计数"无反射态"数，从而厘清有限器件中 \(Q\) 的物理含义，并给出从零偏压电导读取该不变量的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Majorana 模的输运特征（零偏压电导峰）易被普通束缚态模仿，其存在性仍有争议；长导线的拓扑相可由零能反射符号 \(Q\) 标记，但 \(Q\) 在有限器件中究竟"认证"什么此前不清楚。

Method: 解析论证：\(Q\) 计数无反射态（从一端进入、完全从另一端流出的波）；当复能平面下半平面存在奇数个此类态时 \(Q=-1\)。结合有限长导线中 Majorana 模劈裂与其与接触耦合的比较，以及等耦合接触下的能级合并分析（例外点）。

Result: 有限导线中 \(Q=-1\) 意味着两个 Majorana 模的劈裂小于其与接触的耦合；等耦合接触下，\(Q\) 的每次符号变化（含体拓扑转变）均为例外点，此时两态合并、透射谱最平坦；零温下 \(Q=-1\) 当且仅当接触处零偏压电导超过 \(e^2/h\)。

Conclusion: 明确了输运测量能对 Majorana 器件认证什么，并给出从电导直接读取拓扑不变量的实验可行方案。

Abstract: Majorana modes, zero-energy excitations of superconducting wires that are their own antiparticles, could store quantum information protected from local noise. In transport experiments, however, ordinary bound states mimic their signature, a conductance peak at zero voltage, and whether Majorana modes have been observed remains debated. A sign, $Q$, of the electron reflection at zero energy marks the topological phase of a long wire, but what $Q$ certifies in a finite device has been unclear. Here we show for the first time that $Q$ counts reflectionless states, waves that enter through one contact and leave entirely through the other: $Q=-1$ when an odd number of them lie in the lower half of the complex energy plane. In a finite wire this means that the two Majorana modes are split by less than their coupling to the contacts. With equal contacts, every sign change of $Q$, including the bulk topological transition, is an exceptional point at which two such states merge and transmission is maximally flat. At zero temperature, $Q=-1$ exactly when the zero-bias conductance measured at a contact exceeds $e^2/h$. These results show what transport can certify about a Majorana device and how to read the invariant from conductance.

</details>


### [136] [Acoustically driven motion of single molecules at hBN surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17112)
*Hande N. Açıkgöz,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Peter G. Steeneken,Sabina Caneva*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出声表面波(SAW)-六方氮化硼(hBN)集成平台，无需标记即可实现对hBN表面DNA分子的无接触、可调定向输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单分子成像与传感技术使界面生物分子动力学得以直接观测，但如何在表面实现主动输运、定位与分子运动控制仍具挑战，限制了单分子器件的发展。

Method: 构建SAW-hBN集成平台：hBN提供原子级平整表面，使DNA被限制在表面同时保持面内迁移性；SAW作为无接触驱动源，不依赖分子的电学、介电或磁学性质；采用基于轨迹和跳跃的分析方法定量表征输运行为。

Result: 随SAW功率增大，分子在声流方向上的位移偏置增强，输运速度提高且轨迹更趋线性化，实现可调的定向输运。

Conclusion: SAW可在hBN表面诱导DNA的可调定向输运，为纳流控系统中生物分子的片上操控提供新途径，对先进单分子测序平台及生物分子操控与分析技术具有意义。

Abstract: Developments in single-molecule imaging and sensing techniques have enabled direct observation of biomolecular dynamics at interfaces, which has important implications for the fields of membrane biophysics, nanofluidic sensing and transport. However, integrating active surface transport, localization and control over molecule motion remains challenging, limiting further progress in the realization of single-molecule devices. Here, we introduce an integrated surface acoustic wave (SAW)-hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) platform as a contactless actuation platform for directional transport of biomolecules over hBN surfaces. hBN provides an atomically flat surface on which DNA can be confined and still retain in-plane mobility, whereas SAW actuation enables active and guided transport without requiring specific electrical, dielectric or magnetic properties of the biomolecules. Trajectory- and jump-based analyses show that increasing the SAW power leads to higher displacement bias in the streaming direction, resulting in transport velocities along increasingly more linearized trajectories. We thus demonstrate that SAWs can induce tunable directional transport of DNA molecules confined on hBN surfaces, providing a new approach for the on-chip manipulation of biomolecules in nanofluidic systems. Our findings have important implications for the development of advanced single-molecule sequencing platforms and other technologies for biomolecular manipulation and analysis.

</details>


### [137] [Exact characterization of three-terminal fermion counting under one-body scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17122)
*Zhanning Wang,Yongtao Li,Rubén Seoane Souto,José C. Abadillo-Uriel*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 将双端情形下的广义二项分布统计推广到三端/三通道散射区，给出出射费米子统计的精确边界，并提出四条可检验的非相互作用判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双端体系中非相互作用费米子的出射统计服从由泡利不相容原理导出的广义二项分布，偏离即为相互作用信号；但缺乏多端（三端）情形下的严格界限，限制了对冷原子与介观体系中相互作用效应的判定。

Method: 将双端广义二项分布结果推广至耦合三个出射端（通道）的散射区，针对两费米子情形推导六个可测概率之间的精确不等式（共四条），并将这些不等式应用于近期冷原子散射实验数据。

Result: 获得三端情形下出射费米子统计的精确边界；四条涉及六个测量概率的不等式可判定观测统计能否由非相互作用散射体解释；应用于冷原子实验后识别出相互作用对出射粒子有显著贡献的参数区域。

Conclusion: 该工作为多端体系中区分非相互作用与相互作用费米子统计提供了严格判据，使在介观导体到冷原子光阱等纳米尺度体系中识别费米子相互作用成为可能。

Abstract: Quantum scattering is a fundamental probe of the scattering region and the quantum dynamics of outgoing particles. In two terminals, the statistics of non-interacting fermions is described by a generalized binomial distribution, that arises from the Pauli exclusion principle, while deviations are a signature of interactions. In this work, we extend this result to a scattering region coupled to three outgoing terminals (or channels), obtaining exact bounds for the statistics of the outgoing fermions. In particular, for two fermions and three terminals, four inequalities involving six measured probabilities determine whether a non-interacting scatterer can explain the observed statistics. Applying these inequalities to recent scattering experiments in cold atoms, we are able to identify a regime where interactions have a significant contribution on the outgoing particles. Therefore, our work opens up the possibility of identifying fermion interactions at the nanoscale in systems that range from mesoscopic conductors to optical traps in cold atoms.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [AI-assisted search for self-compressing gigawatt subcycle field waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14672)
*Aleksei M. Zheltikov,Youchan Park,Petr Zhokhov,Julia M. Mikhailova*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 研究气体填充空芯光纤中多模非线性动力学下的脉冲自压缩物理，利用生成式预训练Transformer模型加速寻找高功率亚周期光场波形的最优压缩参数区间，并指出亚周期场精确调控仍需相位敏感非线性过程管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索脉冲自压缩至高功率亚周期场波形的物理机制，并在多模非线性动力学刻画的大参数空间中寻找最优压缩区域；传统参数扫描耗时冗长，需借助AI加速。

Method: 以脉冲对比度和无脉冲分裂等物理判据形式化为可操作的停止规则，将繁琐的搜索前期工作委托给生成式预训练Transformer（GPT）模型；结合对场相位敏感非线性过程的管理实现亚周期场工程。

Result: 借助GPT模型使参数空间搜索速度大幅提升；但压缩动力学的精细调节（涉及亚周期场结构的细微方面）仍无法委托给AI代理，因相关判据仍高度主观、难以形式化为明确的搜索停止指令。

Conclusion: 亚周期场工程的最终精度依赖场相位敏感非线性过程的调控，可实现高功率亚周期场波形的脉冲自压缩，并为光纤光学位相阿秒光波工程开辟路径。

Abstract: We explore the physics behind pulse self-compression to high-power subcycle field waveforms and search for the optimal regimes of such compression over the parameter space as laid out by multimode nonlinear dynamics of ultrashort laser pulses in gas-filled hollow fibers. With the pertinent physical criteria for pulse contrast and no pulse breakup formalized as operational stopping rules, a radical speedup of such search is achieved by delegating its tedious preliminary stages to generative pretrained transformer models. The precise adjustment of self-compression dynamics vis-a-vis the desired compressed pulse output is, however, not yet delegable to AI agents, as the criteria for such search are still largely subjective, operating with fine aspects of subcycle field structure, not yet formalizable as clear search-stopping instructions. An ultimate precision in such subcycle field engineering is attainable via a management of field-phase-sensitive nonlinear processes, enabling pulse self-compression to high-power subcycle field waveforms and opening paths toward fiber-optic attosecond lightwave engineering.

</details>


### [139] [High-directivity pulsed beams for effective power and information transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14860)
*Richard W. Ziolkowski*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 利用矢量球面波展开设计出一种超指向性、极端单向的针状波束多极子单元，并证明圆阵与零面积脉冲（ZAP）驱动可在时域完整继承该性能；进一步表明波束能量宽度由脉冲波形而非孔径决定，据此设计的增强型ZAP可将焦区强度提升近200倍、焦斑缩小十倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上提高定向辐射与聚焦能力主要依赖增大孔径，而对单元辐射体在超指向性极限（Harrington 正常指向性）下的实现、以及时域脉冲驱动下能量波束与强度波束的关系缺乏闭式刻画。作者希望识别真正决定局域电磁能量与信息传输的设计变量究竟是孔径还是波形。

Method: 以矢量球面波展开（VSWE）构造单元多极子源，使电、磁多极子在反向精确逐阶抵消，得到远场后瓣恒为零的线极化、幅值方位对称针状波束，并给出任意有限远场半径下前后比的闭式表达；将该单元组成均匀圆阵（UCA）并以零面积脉冲（ZAP）激励，把频域性能无损迁移到时域；通过分析辐射波形性质给出能量波束与强度波束宽窄判据，并进一步设计强度增强型（IE）ZAP，用脉冲激励的 Huygens 偶极子阵进行数值验证。

Result: 解析证明并经数值确认该单元沿波束轴达到 Harrington 正常指向性值；后向远场旁瓣严格为零。UCA 继承 SDEU 特性，ZAP 驱动下时域性能与频域一致。判据表明能量波束相对强度波束的宽窄由波形单一属性决定，与孔径尺寸无关；单周期 ZAP 对应更窄的能量波束，该判据同样适用于近场聚焦脉冲阵的焦斑。定制 IE-ZAP 使焦区强度较窄带参考提高近200倍，轴向与横向焦斑均缩小十倍以上，且相对其自身有效波长远小于波长；该效果仅需脉冲激励的 Huygens 偶极子阵即可实现。

Conclusion: 脉冲波形而非辐射孔径是控制局域电磁能量与信息传输的关键设计变量。所提出的 SDEU 单元、ZAP 驱动的 UCA 及 IE-ZAP 方案为超指向、深亚波长聚焦与定向能量投送提供了闭式可分析、实现简单的设计框架。

Abstract: A vector spherical wave expansion (VSWE) is used to engineer an elemental multipole source whose radiated field is a superdirective, extremely unidirectional (SDEU) needle beam. It is proved analytically and confirmed numerically that the element attains Harrington's normal-directivity value along its beam axis. Its radiated field is linearly polarized with an azimuthally symmetric magnitude; its back lobe vanishes identically in the far field. The unidirectional behavior is quantified at any finite far-field radius by a closed-form front-to-back ratio, obtained from an exact per-degree cancellation of the electric and magnetic multipole contributions in the backward direction. Uniform circular arrays (UCAs) of these elements inherit the SDEU behavior. Driving the arrays with zero-area pulses (ZAPs) transfers the frequency-domain performance intact into the time domain. It is shown that whether the radiated energy beam is narrower or wider than the corresponding intensity beam is decided by a single property of the radiated waveform, not by the size of the radiating aperture. A simple single-cycle ZAP is used to quantify the performance of the UCAs; its energy beam is the narrower one. The same criterion is shown to govern the focal spot of near-field-focused pulse-driven arrays. A custom-designed intensity-enhancement (IE) ZAP is introduced that raises the focal intensity by almost 200-fold over a narrow-band reference while producing a focal spot more than ten times smaller in both the axial and transverse directions, deeply subwavelength with respect to its own effective wavelength. It is demonstrated that these stunning IE results are produced simply with an array of pulse-excited Huygens dipole sources. These results identify the pulse shape, rather than the aperture, as the design variable that governs localized electromagnetic energy and information delivery.

</details>


### [140] [A Field-Deployable 2U Ultra-Stable Laser with Sub-Hertz Linewidth Based on Optoelectronic Oscillator Locking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15005)
*Dongdong Jiao,Linbo Zhang,Xue Deng,Jing Gao,Yue Gao,Mengfan Wu,Qi Zang,Guanjun Xu,Tao Liu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 报道了目前最紧凑的可外场部署超稳激光器，封装于标准2U机箱，采用光电子振荡器(OEO)锁定的50 mm、19 mL低振动敏感度参考腔，积分线宽约0.42 Hz，1 s稳定度约5E-15，体积约为同类可搬运超稳激光的四分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超稳激光长期受限于体积与实验室环境依赖，亟需可在实验室外运行的紧凑、高性能超稳激光器，以满足时频计量、量子技术与环境传感等外场应用需求。

Method: 构建小型化光参考腔（长50 mm、体积19 mL、低振动敏感度），并采用光电子振荡器(OEO)锁定方案稳定激光；该方案特别适合紧凑、可运输系统。对线宽、频率稳定度与相位噪声进行测量，并与腔热噪声极限及OEO锁定理论极限对比。

Result: 积分线宽约0.42 Hz；分数频率稳定度1 s约5E-15、0.4 s约2.2E-15；100 Hz以下相位噪声接近腔热噪声极限，100 Hz至100 kHz接近OEO锁定理论极限；整机体积约为已报道同级性能集成可搬运超稳激光的四分之一。

Conclusion: 该2U机箱超稳激光器在保持接近噪声极限的性能同时显著压缩体积，具备外场部署能力，适用于时频计量、量子技术及环境传感等应用。

Abstract: Compact, high-performance ultra-stable lasers that can operate outside the laboratory remain a long-standing goal. Here we report, to our knowledge, the most compact field-deployable ultra-stable laser, packaged in a standard 2U rack chassis. It is stabilized to a 50 mm-long, 19 mL optical reference cavity with low vibration sensitivity by optoelectronic oscillator (OEO) locking, an approach particularly suited to compact, transportable systems. The laser delivers an integrated linewidth of about 0.42 Hz and a fractional frequency stability of about 5E-15 at 1 s averaging time (about 2.2E-15 at 0.4 s). Measured phase noise approaches the cavity thermal-noise limit below 100 Hz and the theoretical OEO-locking limit from 100 Hz to 100 kHz. The system volume is roughly one quarter of the volume of previously reported integrated, transportable ultra-stable lasers of comparable performance, making it well suited for field applications in time-frequency metrology, quantum technologies, and environmental sensing.

</details>


### [141] [Analytic T-Matrix Formalism for Helmholtz Resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15208)
*Egor V. Domoratskii,Polina A. Medvedeva,Dmitrii N. Maksimov,Lana A. Glazko,Alexey A. Proskurin,Nikolay S. Solodovchenko,Mingzhao Song,Yong Li,Mihail I. Petrov,Andrey A. Bogdanov*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 建立了二维圆环形声学亥姆霍兹共振器（薄刚性壁）的解析T矩阵形式，用孔径投影算子给出显式耦合系数，并用孔径-傅里叶映射与边缘自适应基加速收敛；据此分解出Willis耦合对应的奇偶通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T矩阵方法缺乏对带薄壁孔径腔体共振与外场耦合的简洁显式描述，难以分辨Willis耦合通道，且收敛慢。作者希望给出紧凑、可扩展至任意多孔径、并能揭示方向性散射/吸收机理的解析框架。

Method: 采用孔径投影算子构造T矩阵的紧凑解析表达式，系数显式刻画外场与腔共振之间的耦合与解耦；提出等价但收敛更快的表述——孔径到傅里叶的映射加针对孔径通量的边缘自适应基；方法可直接推广到任意角宽度与位置的多孔径。将T矩阵在反演下分解为偶部与奇部以识别Willis耦合通道，并研究内部损耗与展开原点平移的影响。

Result: 计算得到的散射谱与全波数值模拟一致。对单、双孔径共振器，反演奇偶分解识别出与Willis耦合相关的通道；表明在反演对称性破缺时内部损耗可实现方向相关的散射与吸收，而互易性保证相反入射方向具有相同的消光。平移展开原点可在多极通道间重新分配辐射，从而获得更紧凑的T矩阵表示。

Conclusion: 该解析T矩阵框架为薄壁多孔径亥姆霍兹共振器提供了紧凑、快速收敛且物理透明的描述，可用于分析Willis耦合、非互易吸收效应及T矩阵优化表示。

Abstract: We develop an analytic T-matrix formalism for two-dimensional circular acoustic Helmholtz resonators with thin rigid walls. The central idea is to use an aperture projection operator to obtain a compact expression for the T-matrix with explicit coefficients describing coupling and decoupling between external fields and cavity resonances. An equivalent formulation providing much faster convergence uses an aperture-to-Fourier map and an edge-adapted basis for the aperture flux. The method extends directly to multiple apertures of arbitrary angular widths and positions. The calculated scattering spectra agree with full-wave numerical simulations. For single- and double-aperture resonators, we decompose the T-matrix into even and odd parts under inversion to identify the channels associated with Willis coupling. We show how internal losses enable direction-dependent scattering and absorption when inversion symmetry is broken, while reciprocity ensures equal extinction for opposite incidence. Finally, we show how shifting the expansion origin redistributes radiation among multipole channels, enabling more compact T-matrix representations.

</details>


### [142] [Ultrahigh-speed digital holography reveals blood flow beneath the skin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15535)
*Yann Fischer,Zacharie Auray,Maxime Boy-Arnould,Michael Atlan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 激光多普勒全息术（LDH）利用超高速数字全息记录，在闭眼眼睑及眼周皮肤下可视化血流信号，单例受试者验证可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有眼部成像系统多局限于眼表或眼底血管，难以在无需复杂固定装置的条件下观察闭眼状态下眼睑皮肤下方的血流分布；本文旨在将LDH扩展至该场景。

Method: 采用约16,000帧/秒的超高速数字全息记录，在10 mW漫射近红外照明下对一名志愿者成像；通过数值菲涅耳重建与主成分（principal-component）滤波提取血流信号，呈现分支状血管图案。

Result: 成功重建出眼睑及眼周皮下分支状血管形态与血流信号分布，验证了在低功率漫射照明与高速采集条件下的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作将眼成像系统拓展至无需繁琐固定流程的皮下血流可视化，为闭眼状态下的眼部血流监测提供了新途径；但结果基于单例受试者，尚需更大样本验证。

Abstract: Laser Doppler holography (LDH) leverages ultrahigh-speed digital holography to reveal blood-flow signals in vessels beneath the skin of the closed eyelid and around the eye. We demonstrate this in one volunteer using recordings acquired at approximately 16,000 frames/s under 10~mW of diffuse near-infrared illumination. Numerical Fresnel reconstruction and principal-component filtering reveal branching vascular patterns. These results extend an ocular imaging system to blood-flow visualization beneath the skin without requiring elaborate immobilization procedures.

</details>


### [143] [Laser-induced damage threshold of periodic plasmonic metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15590)
*Sebastian Beer,Amit Shaha Surja,Jeetendra Gour,Alessandro Alberucci,Uwe D. Zeitner,Stefan Nolte*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 研究周期性金纳米棒超表面中表面晶格共振(SLR)、二次谐波产生(SHG)与激光损伤阈值(LIDT)的相互作用：基频SLR增强SHG但降低损伤阈值，而二次谐波波长处的SLR不影响损伤阈值，可据此最大化SHG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离激元超表面因亚波长场局域化而具有强非线性响应，但其性能受低激光损伤阈值(LIDT)的根本性限制；需要厘清SLR、SHG与LIDT之间的相互制约关系，以指导高功率非线性应用的设计。

Method: 对周期性金纳米棒超表面开展实验与数值相结合的研究；采用角度分辨测量，分别在基频波长和二次谐波波长处激发表面晶格共振(SLR)，并同步表征SHG效率与损伤阈值。

Result: 基频处激发SLR显著提升SHG效率，但同时降低LIDT；在二次谐波波长处激发SLR则不影响损伤阈值，从而使SHG信号得以最大化。

Conclusion: 揭示了非线性效率与光学鲁棒性之间的基本权衡关系，并为优化周期性等离激元超表面用于高功率非线性应用提供了设计准则。

Abstract: Plasmonic metasurfaces enable strong nonlinear optical responses due to sub-wavelength field confinement, yet their performance is fundamentally limited by low laser-induced damage thresholds (LIDT). Here, we present a comprehensive experimental and numerical study of the interplay between surface lattice resonances (SLRs), second-harmonic generation (SHG), and LIDT in periodic gold nanobar metasurfaces. Angle-resolved measurements reveal that excitation of SLRs at the fundamental wavelength strongly enhances SHG efficiency while simultaneously reducing the LIDT. In contrast, excitation of an SLR at the second-harmonic wavelength does not affect the damage threshold, allowing the SHG signal to be maximized. Our findings establish a fundamental trade-off between nonlinear efficiency and optical robustness and provide design guidelines for optimizing periodic plasmonic metasurfaces for high-power nonlinear applications.

</details>


### [144] [Gradient-Free Inverse Design of 3D Nanophotonic Devices Using the Level-Set-Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15656)
*Oliver Kuster,Carsten Rockstuhl,Thomas Jebb Sturges*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文将无梯度优化与水平集方法(LSM)结合，用高斯径向基函数表示三维几何，实现面向3D纳米光子增材制造的逆向设计，并能严格施加制造约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D纳米光子增材制造使复杂自由形状器件成为可能，向三维扩展带来额外设计自由度，但需要逆设计方法能高效搜索大设计空间并同时满足制造约束。传统伴随法梯度优化依赖可微性，对二元制造约束只能近似处理。

Method: 采用高斯径向基函数构造水平集表示三维几何，仅用数百个设计变量保持充分几何自由度；以此紧凑表示使无梯度优化在完全三维纳米光子器件上可行，无需伴随计算，可调用任意Maxwell求解器或不需导数的辅助算法，并支持高度并行的流程。结合连通分量分析算法强制结构完整性。

Result: 以Y型分束器优化为例，在严格保证材料与空隙结构完整性（连通性）的前提下完成优化，验证了方法的适用性。

Conclusion: 无梯度LSM为3D增材制造逆向设计提供了实用途径：紧凑参数化+无梯度优化可严格施加二元制造约束，避免梯度法的可微近似，并具备良好并行性与求解器通用性。

Abstract: Advances in 3D nanophotonic additive manufacturing enable increasingly complex, almost free-form devices for manipulating light at sub-wavelength scales. Extending nanophotonic designs into three dimensions introduces additional degrees of freedom. Exploiting this freedom requires inverse-design methods that can efficiently navigate this large design space while accommodating fabrication constraints. Here, we combine gradient-free optimization with the level-set method (LSM) to provide such an approach for 3D additive manufacturing. Representing the 3D geometry as a level set constructed from Gaussian radial basis functions retains substantial geometric freedom with only a few hundred design variables. This compact representation makes gradient-free optimization practical for fully 3D nanophotonic devices, eliminating the need for adjoint calculations. This unlocks the use of any Maxwell solver or auxiliary algorithms that should express fabrication constraints for which the required derivatives are unavailable or impractical to obtain, and enables embarrassingly parallel workflows. Consequently, binary fabrication requirements, such as structural connectivity, can be rigorously enforced, avoiding the differentiable approximations commonly used in gradient-based optimization. Here, we demonstrate the combination of the gradient-free LSM with a connected-component analysis algorithm to enforce the structural integrity of the design. We demonstrate the applicability of the gradient-free LSM by optimizing a Y-splitter while strictly enforcing the structural integrity of the material and the void in the design.

</details>


### [145] [Towards dense optical communications with partially coherent light: I. Bimodal Stokes communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15701)
*Ayman F. Abouraddy,Bahaa E. A. Saleh*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 论文提出“Stokes通信”（S-Comm）新方案：不调制各模式的振幅，而是调制多模部分相干光场的 N_f×N_f 厄米相干矩阵中的各广义Stokes参数，从而在 N_f 个光模式中复用 N_D = N_f²−1 个独立数据流，显著提升信道容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光复用中 N_f 个正交模式仅承载 N_f 个独立数据流（容量与模式数线性增长），未能充分利用部分相干光较高的信息携带能力。

Method: 以部分相干多模光场为载波，将数据编码到相干矩阵的广义Stokes参数上。文中给出双模场（N_f=2）下 N_D=3 个数据流的 S-Comm 收发机设计：发射端包含相干度调制器（将数据流编码为双模场的相干度）、Stokes编码器（把各数据流分配到指定Stokes参数）与Stokes复用器（将双模场合成物理模式）；接收端执行相应逆操作恢复数据流，采用开关键控（OOK）调制，且设计便于片上集成。

Result: 在 N_f=2 的演示设计中实现 3 个数据流经OOK编码并复用进两个模式；容量随模式数按 N_f²−1 增长，模式越多提升越显著。

Conclusion: 结果表明部分相干光长期被认识但未被开发的高信息容量可用于实现高密度光通信，且方案具备片上集成的可行性。

Abstract: In conventional optical multiplexing, $N_{\mathrm{f}}$~orthogonal spectral or spatial modes multiplex $N_{\mathrm{f}}$ independent data streams transmitted over an optical channel (e.g., a multimode optical fiber supporting $N_{\mathrm{f}}$~guided modes). We introduce here a new optical communications scheme that we call `Stokes communications' (S-Comm), whereby $N_{\mathrm{D}}=N_{\mathrm{f}}^{2}-1$ independent data streams are multiplexed into $N_{\mathrm{f}}$~optical modes, potentially yielding a dramatic increase in the channel capacity. Rather than modulating the amplitude of each mode to encode a data stream, we make use of multimode partially coherent light and modulate each generalized Stokes parameter in the $N_{\mathrm{f}}\times N_{\mathrm{f}}$ Hermitian coherence matrix associated with the field. We present here designs for the S-Comm transmitter and receiver making use of bimodal fields ($N_{\mathrm{f}}=2$) in which $N_{\mathrm{D}}=3$ data streams are encoded via on-off keying and multiplexed in two modes. We describe the main blocks in the S-Comm transmitter: coherence modulators that encode a data stream in the degree of coherence of a bimode (a two-mode partially coherent field), Stokes encoders that assign each data stream to a prescribed Stokes parameter, and Stokes multiplexers that combine bimodes into physical modes. We also describe the reverse operations implemented in the S-Comm receiver to recover the data streams. Significantly higher channel capacity is expected for a larger number of physical modes, which is facilitated by the amenability of the presented designs to on-chip integration. These results suggest that the long-appreciated but unexploited higher information-carrying capacity of partially coherent light can help realize dense optical communications.

</details>


### [146] [Towards dense optical communications with partially coherent light: II. Multimode Stokes communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15715)
*Ayman F. Abouraddy,Bahaa E. A. Saleh*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出将“Stokes通信（S-Comm）”从双模推广到多模（N_f>2）部分相干光场，利用广义Stokes参数复用N_D=N_f²−1路数据流，并给出对角与非对角Stokes参数分别处理的收发机设计方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在相同模式张成的条件下，部分相干光比相干光具有更高的信息承载能力，有望提升光通信信道密度；已有工作仅针对双模（N_f=2，3路数据流）实现了S-Comm，需推广至多模情形。

Method: 以相干矩阵唯一对应的广义Stokes参数作为复用维度；指出对角与非对角Stokes参数需不同处理，并针对对角复用与非对角复用数据流分别设计发射机与接收机；方案构建单元可在集成光子回路平台上实现。

Result: 给出了N_f>2多模部分相干场中N_D=N_f²−1路数据流复用的完整收发机设计方案，且各功能模块可由现有集成光子学器件实现。

Conclusion: 多模部分相干光（“结构化相干”）的片上处理对光通信及更广泛的光信号处理具有深远意义。

Abstract: Partially coherent light has a higher informational-carrying capacity than coherent light when both fields are spanned by the same modes, which implies that partially coherent light may increase the channel density in optical communications. A partially coherent field spanned by $N_{\mathrm{f}}$ modes can multiplex $N_{\mathrm{D}}=N_{\mathrm{f}}^{2}-1$ data streams in the generalized Stokes parameters that uniquely identify its coherence matrix -- a scheme we call `Stokes communications' (S-Comm). We have proposed in an accompanying paper a realization of S-Comm for bimodal fields $N_{\mathrm{f}}=2$ that multiplex $N_{\mathrm{D}}=3$ data streams. Here we extend this proposed strategy for S-Comm to multimode fields $N_{\mathrm{f}}>2$. We find that the diagonal and off-diagonal Stokes parameters require different treatments and we provide detailed designs for the transmitter and receiver for both diagonally and off-diagonally multiplexed data streams. The building blocks of the proposed system can be readily implemented in integrated photonic circuit platforms. Our results indicate that on-chip processing of multimode partially coherent light (or `structured coherence') may have profound implications for optical communications and for optical signal processing in general.

</details>


### [147] [Metasurfaces with Anisotropic Electric and Magnetic Conductivities: Parameter Retrieval for Arbitrary Incidence and Polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15752)
*Dimitrios Chatzidimitriou,Alexandros Pitilakis,Odysseas Tsilipakos*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种在斜入射、任意极化下从单向散射数据解析提取各向异性超表面等效表面电导率与磁导率（表面磁流/磁化率）张量的方法，并给出其逆表达式，可用于反射/透射系数的正向计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超表面参数反演方法常受限于各向同性单元、单一均匀基底，或需同时测量两侧反射与透射系数而增加复杂度；实验上双向散射信息往往难以获取。

Method: 在斜入射平面波（任意极化）激励下，将超表面建模为同时具有电、磁可极化性的各向异性表面，推导等效表面电导率（及表面磁化率）张量的解析反演公式；仅使用单向散射（单侧反射/透射）信息，避免双向散射需求。

Result: 用微波频段的电谐振 meta-atom 与 THz 频段的磁谐振 meta-atom 进行验证；基于反演得到的各向异性电导率从物理上解释并演示了其极化转换能力；同时给出由已知表面电导率张量计算反射/透射系数的逆表达式。

Conclusion: 所提方法简化了各向异性超表面的参数提取流程，特别利于实验研究，并为超表面综合与辐射问题提供了正向计算工具。

Abstract: Metasurfaces come in a great variety of unit cell geometries and material composition. A common approach for describing them relies on retrieving effective surface parameters (e.g., electric and magnetic conductivities) from plane-wave scattering data. Conventional retrieval methods are often limited to isotropic unit cells, a uniform host material, or lead to increased complexity by requiring reflection and transmission coefficients from both sides of the metasurface. Here, we derive analytical expressions for the equivalent surface conductivities of an electrically- and magnetically-polarizable anisotropic metasurface under oblique plane-wave incidence with arbitrary polarization. The proposed formulation does not require bidirectional scattering information, thus simplifying the process especially in experimental studies. The method is validated through representative electric and magnetic meta-atoms at microwave and THz frequencies, respectively. Their ability for polarization conversion is demonstrated and discussed relying on the physical insight provided by the retrieved anisotropic conductivities. In addition, we present the inverse expressions for calculating the reflection and transmission coefficients from known surface conductivity tensors, which can prove useful for metasurface synthesis and radiation problems.

</details>


### [148] [Subpicosecond Optical Time Transfer under Simulated Motion with Electro-Optic Frequency Combs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15763)
*Nolan Matthews,Joshua Olson,Robert Rockmore,Sean Krzyzewski*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文利用电光频率梳构建运动条件下双向光学时间传递的实验室模拟器，在km级光纤链路上验证了0至400 m/s线性加速速度剖面下亚皮秒精度的连续钟差跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能光学频率梳时间传递走向实际系统时，平台运动（尤其大相对速度）是主要挑战，而缺乏可控、可重复的运动场景测试手段。

Method: 采用敏捷电光频率梳，通过直接调控其参数人为引入相对运动效应，搭建双向光学时间传递的运动实验室模拟器；在km级光纤链路上测试速度从静止线性增至400 m/s的速度剖面，并进行后处理数据分析。

Result: 在整个速度扫描过程中实现两站点间钟差的连续跟踪，精度达亚皮秒量级。

Conclusion: 结果表明电光频率梳在已部署系统的光学时间传递中具有良好通用性与适用性，为运动平台下的高精度时间传递提供了灵活的实验验证途径。

Abstract: Extension of high performance optical frequency comb-based time transfer to real-world systems is challenged by platform motion, particularly for large relative velocities. Here, we utilize agile electro-optic frequency combs to construct a laboratory-based simulator of a two-way optical time transfer system under motion. Through direct control of the electro-optic frequency comb parameters, we can artificially induce the effects of relative motion. We performed tests of the time transfer performance across a km-scale fiber link for velocity profiles that linearly increase from rest up to 400 m/s. Through a post-processed data analysis, we demonstrate continuous tracking of the clock offset between sites with subpicosecond precision throughout the velocity sweep. These results illustrate the versatility of using electro-optic frequency combs for optical time transfer in deployed systems.

</details>


### [149] [Intense and stable second-harmonic generation in robust MePEA-based quasi-2D halide perovskite crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15772)
*Grigorii Verkhogliadov,Dmitry Semyonov,Elizaveta Sapozhnikova,Dmitry Tatarinov,Marina Tepliakova,Nina Ratmanova,Ivan Andreev,Ivan Melnichenko,Natalia Kryzhanovskaya,Alexey Zhukov,Quynh Le-Van,Daria Khmelevskaia,Anatoly Pushkarev*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 研究PEA与4-MePEA间隔阳离子对准二维钙钛矿晶体对称性和二次谐波响应的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准二维卤化物钙钛矿可通过有机间隔阳离子调控结构与光学性质，是潜在的非线性光学平台，但间隔阳离子如何影响晶体中心对称性破缺及二次谐波（SHG）响应尚需系统研究。

Method: 采用腔体限域结晶法生长横向尺寸达数百微米的高质量钙钛矿单晶；对比苯乙基铵（PEA）与4-甲基-2-苯乙基铵（4-MePEA）间隔阳离子及不同[PbX6]^4-层数n的准二维相；通过功率依赖与偏振分辨SHG测量表征非线性响应。

Result: 晶体表现出强且时间稳定的SHG信号；功率依赖与偏振分辨测量证实其来源于二阶非线性过程，并呈现各向异性发射图样；观察到间隔阳离子诱导的中心对称性破缺与非线性光学活性之间的直接关联。

Conclusion: 准二维卤化物钙钛矿可作为对称性工程化材料，用于高效频率转换与集成非线性光子学。

Abstract: Quasi-2D halide perovskites offer a unique platform for nonlinear optics applications, where structural and optical properties are tuned by the variation of organic spacer cations. Notably, quasi-2D perovskite single crystals can demonstrate intense and stable second-harmonic generation signals when a proper composition is selected. Here, the influence of phenethylammonium and 4-methyl-2-phenylethylammonium spacers on the crystal symmetry and second-harmonic signal response of quasi-2D perovskite phases with a varying number of single-octahedron [PbX$_6$]$^{4-}$ layers ($n$) is investigated. High-quality perovskite crystals with lateral dimensions of several hundred micrometers are fabricated using a cavity-confined crystallization synthesis method. Optical characterization of the crystals shows strong and temporally stable second-harmonic generation. Power-dependent and polarization-resolved measurements confirm the second-order nonlinear origin of the observed response and reveal anisotropic emission patterns. Our results demonstrate a direct correlation between spacer-induced center-symmetry breaking and nonlinear optical activity. These results establish quasi-2D halide perovskites as a promising class of symmetry-engineered materials for efficient frequency conversion and integrated nonlinear photonics.

</details>


### [150] [Efficient refractive-index sweeps in the discrete dipole approximation using shifted Krylov methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15909)
*Clément Argentin,Konstantin G. Inzhevatkin,Maxim A. Yurkin*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 将位移Krylov子空间方法引入离散偶极近似（DDA），复用同一粒子几何在大量复折射率下的线性系统，显著加速光学数据库生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDA中同一粒子几何需对大量复折射率重复独立计算，计算时间成正比增长；而均质粒子的折射率变化仅影响相互作用矩阵的对角极化率项，使线性系统仅相差单位矩阵的标量位移，存在复用潜力。

Method: 采用适配复对称矩阵形式的位移Krylov求解器，并实现于ADDA代码中；对均质球形粒子在二维复折射率域上评估，验证消光截面并分析加速比与折射率取值数、计算后端及收敛率空间变异的关系。

Result: 位移求解器复现的消光截面与独立模拟相对差异低于迭代容差；测试配置下矩阵-向量乘积次数减少130倍；大规模折射率扫描墙钟时间加速最高达40倍。

Conclusion: 位移Krylov方法可高效复用DDA计算，大幅加速折射率扫描；散场计算成为新瓶颈，进一步优化可提升加速比。

Abstract: The discrete dipole approximation (DDA) is a flexible numerical method for simulating electromagnetic scattering by particles of arbitrary shape and internal structure. In many applications, such as optical-database generation, the same particle geometry must be simulated for many values of the complex refractive index, which are currently handled independently with proportional increase of computational time. For homogeneous particles, however, variations of the refractive index affect only the diagonal polarizability term of the interaction matrix. The corresponding linear systems therefore differ only by scalar shifts of the identity matrix, making them suitable for shifted Krylov-subspace methods. In this work, we use a shifted Krylov solver adapted to the complex-symmetric formulation of the matrix and implement it in the ADDA code. The method is evaluated for a homogeneous spherical particle over a two-dimensional domain of complex refractive indices. The shifted solver reproduces the extinction cross sections obtained from independent simulations with relative differences below the iterative-method tolerance, while reducing the number of matrix-vector products by a factor of 130 for the tested configuration. We further analyze how the achievable speedup depends on the number of refractive-index values, the computational backend, and the variation of the convergence rate across the parameter space. The final wall-time acceleration for large refractive-index sweeps was demonstrated to be up to 40 times, which can be further improved by optimizing the scattered-fields calculations that became a new bottleneck.

</details>


### [151] [What Can Next Generation Ultrafast Light Sources Tell Us About Quantum Materials?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15997)
*Allan S. Johnson*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该综述从超快光谱学视角出发，梳理研究量子材料时当前超快光源"缺失的能力"，并指出下一代超快光源可在哪些悬而未决的关键科学问题上发挥决定性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快光谱已成为现代超快光源的核心应用：飞秒脉冲能在时域"解耦"量子材料中通常强耦合的内部自由度，揭示涌现序的关键相互作用。随着光源覆盖硬X射线到THz、场强可达非平衡态、脉宽短至阿秒，工具箱日趋完备，因此有必要反思当前仍缺乏何种能力，以及哪些问题需要正在建成或设计中的下一代光源。

Method: 以观点/综述形式，系统盘点超快光学光源在量子材料研究中的现有能力边界，归纳尚缺的光源参数（如能量、脉宽、重复频率、相干性、波段覆盖等维度）并对应到具体科学问题。

Result: 作者给出了一份"缺失能力清单"，并将之与量子及强关联材料中的若干突出问题相匹配，论证这些新光源如何能带来决定性影响（摘要未给出具体实验数据）。

Conclusion: 下一代超快光源并非现有光源的简单升级，而应针对时域解耦多自由度、探测非平衡态与强关联序的具体需求来设计，以解决当前工具链无法触及的核心问题。

Abstract: Ultrafast spectroscopy of quantum materials has emerged as a principle application of modern ultrafast optical sources. Measurements made with ultrashort pulses allow the normally strongly coupled internal degrees of freedom inside these systems to be de-coupled in the time-domain, revealing the key interactions for the different orders that lead to dramatic emergent phenomena. This has naturally motivated a wide range of experiments, and quantum materials have now been studied with ultrafast sources spanning from the hard X-ray to the THz, with fields intense enough to create non-equilibrium states, and using pulses down to the attosecond duration. As the optical spectroscopist's toolbox becomes increasingly complete, it is worth pausing and reflecting on what are the capabilities that we currently lack for examining quantum materials, and what are the problems that require the next generation of ultrafast sources now coming on-line or being designed. Here I will offer an overview of the remaining missing capabilities in ultrafast optical sources as required for studying quantum materials, and identify some outstanding problems where I believe these new sources can provide decisive impact in our understanding of quantum and strongly-correlated materials.

</details>


### [152] [Mapping the High-Field Response of Superconducting Nanowire Single-Photon Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16071)
*Harshit Agarwal,Yueh-Chun Wu,Katyayani Seal,Niels Noordzij,Andreas Fognini,Ronan Gourgues,Gábor B. Halász,Benjamin J. Lawrie,Nirjhar Sarkar*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作表征了NbTiN超导纳米线单光子探测器在最高14 T磁场下的暗计数响应，揭示其性能对角度的强各向异性：垂直磁场迅速压缩可用偏置电流范围，而近平行取向可在12 T保持有限工作裕度、在14 T仍有可测暗计数响应，角度依赖主要由磁场的垂直分量主导（符合涡旋介导的超导电流抑制），并指出同一电流标度决定脉冲幅度从而关联读出性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNSPD正越来越多地集成到需在大磁场下工作的低温量子传感平台，但其高场极限与取向依赖尚未被系统刻画，亟需明确可用的磁场—取向工作窗口以指导器件部署。

Method: 利用原位探测器取向控制，在最高14 T磁场中对NbTiN SNSPD的暗计数响应进行表征，并以较小磁场下的常规工作性能作为基准进行对比；通过角度扫描分析磁场各分量对偏置电流范围与脉冲幅度的影响。

Result: 性能呈强各向异性：垂直磁场快速压缩可用偏置电流范围；近平行取向在更大总场下仍可工作，12 T时保留有限工作裕度，14 T极限处仍可测得暗计数响应。角度依赖主要由垂直场分量决定，符合涡旋介导的超导电流标度抑制；该场依赖电流标度同时决定电脉冲幅度，将高场工作与读出性能直接关联。

Conclusion: 通过取向优化（近平行配置）可使NbTiN SNSPD在数特斯拉乃至约12–14 T场下保持可用性能，为在多特斯拉量子传感与高场低温实验中使用SNSPD提供了切实可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Superconducting nanowire single-photon detectors (SNSPDs) are increasingly being integrated into cryogenic quantum-sensing platforms that operate in large magnetic fields. Here, the high-field limits of these measurements were probed by characterizing the dark count response of a NbTiN SNSPD at up to 14~T using in situ control of the detector orientation and benchmarking that performance against normal detector operation in smaller magnetic fields. The detector performance is strongly anisotropic: perpendicular magnetic fields rapidly reduce the usable bias-current range, whereas near-parallel alignment enables operation at substantially larger total fields, with a finite operating margin retained to 12~T and measurable dark-count response at the 14~T field limit. The angular dependence is governed predominantly by the perpendicular component of the magnetic field, consistent with vortex-mediated suppression of the superconducting current scale. The same field-dependent current scale also determines the electrical pulse amplitude, linking high-field detector operation directly to readout performance. These results establish a practical route for deploying SNSPDs in multi-tesla quantum-sensing and high-field cryogenic experiments.

</details>


### [153] [Optical symmetry breaking for single-image field reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16094)
*Egor Manuylovich*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过设计相位掩模打破测量空间中的模态对称性，实现单幅强度图像直接重建复光场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机只记录强度，传统方法依赖多次测量或迭代相位恢复；在已知空间模态下，强度由复系数的成对乘积决定，而光学对称性会使不同光场产生近乎相同的图像，导致不可区分。

Method: 优化相位掩模并配合传播，分离模态成对特征同时保留探测器上的光强；标定线性逆变换与秩一投影恢复模态振幅与相对相位，无需迭代。

Result: 仿真中优化掩模满足随机掩模无法达到的条件数与光捕获要求；实验中用空间光调制器与无源扩散器分别重建42模光纤场，相对全息参考的中位复场误差为9.1%与4.6%。

Conclusion: 确立测量空间对称性破缺作为快速单帧光场重建的途径；单帧计算2.1 ms，批量吞吐超4000帧/秒。

Abstract: Light carries information in both amplitude and phase, although cameras record only intensity. Reconstructing the optical field is at the heart of wavefront sensing, microscopy and coherent communications. Existing methods often rely on multiple measurements or iterative phase retrieval. In fields described by known spatial modes, intensity is determined by pairwise products of their complex coefficients. Yet optical symmetries can make these products indistinguishable, leaving different fields with nearly identical images. Here, we show that engineering the optical measurement to break these symmetries enables direct reconstruction of the complex field from a single intensity image. We optimise a phase mask followed by propagation to separate pairwise modal signatures while retaining light on the detector. A calibrated linear inverse and rank-one projection then recover modal amplitudes and relative phases without iterative phase retrieval. In simulations, optimised masks satisfy conditioning and light-capture requirements unmet by random masks. Experiments reconstruct 42-mode fibre fields using either a spatial light modulator or a passive diffuser, with median complex-field errors of 9.1% and 4.6%, respectively, against holographic references. This extends an earlier five-mode experimental demonstration of fast intensity-only modal decomposition. Per-frame computation takes 2.1 ms, and batched throughput exceeds 4,000 frames per second. These results establish measurement-space symmetry breaking as a route to rapid single-image optical field reconstruction.

</details>


### [154] [Integrated optical isolators enabled by 2D semiconductors via spin-momentum locking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16125)
*Fernando Martín-Romero,Víctor J. Gómez,Alberto G. Curto*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文通过计算模拟研究了基于非互易二维半导体的集成光隔离器，并提出了基于自旋-谷极化与光可调磁性的全光隔离器新概念，证明二维半导体可作为先进非互易器件平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集成光隔离器多为磁光型，需外加磁场；而大量非互易效应在替代材料平台中尚未被充分挖掘，尤其是与自旋-动量锁定关联、可通过偏振调控传播方向的效应。

Method: 采用计算模拟方法，系统研究多种基于非互易二维半导体的集成隔离器设计，分析器件几何结构与光学性质对性能的影响；并提出基于过渡金属硫族化合物单层中自旋-谷极化与光可调磁性的全光隔离器概念。

Result: 揭示了器件几何与材料光学参数对隔离性能的影响规律，并验证了全光隔离器方案的可行性。

Conclusion: 二维半导体可确立为集成非互易器件的先进平台，可实现传统磁光材料难以实现的功能（如全光操作）。

Abstract: Integrated optical isolators based on spin-momentum locking hold great potential for protecting photonic integrated circuits from unwanted reflections. Currently, the most common implementations are magneto-optical isolators that require an externally applied magnetic field. Nevertheless, a wide range of nonreciprocal effects remains to be exploited in alternative material platforms. Although the majority of these effects induce changes to the propagation of light as a function of its polarization, they can be directly connected to the propagation direction through spin-momentum locking. We employ a computational approach to investigate various designs of integrated isolators based on nonreciprocal 2D semiconductors, studying the influence of their geometry and optical properties on the device performance. Additionally, we propose a novel concept for an all-optical isolator based on spin-valley polarization and optically tunable magnetism in transition metal dichalcogenide monolayers. Our results establish 2D semiconductors as an advanced platform for integrated nonreciprocal devices, enabling functionalities that are difficult to achieve with conventional magneto-optical materials like all-optical operation.

</details>


### [155] [Axial von Mises Framework for Tensor-Dependent Second-Harmonic Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16232)
*Sui Ip Ng,Yi-Ping Ho,Ken-Tye Yong*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 基于单一轴向von Mises取向分布，推导了投影纤维轴统计量与张量相关SHG谐波之间的闭式关系，并给出谐波零点、灵敏度损失与反演歧义的几何条件，辅以角膜实测示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHG谐波强度依赖纤维取向统计与张量参数，但二者耦合使物理量反演缺乏明确解析条件；需澄清何种条件下可从谐波推断取向或张量信息。

Method: 假设域内相干发射、域间强度相加，采用单一轴向von Mises分布，推导各角谐波分解为局域张量系数与轴向圆矩乘积的闭式表达；分别讨论平行检偏（2、4、6次谐波）与理想等权无检偏（2、4次谐波）情形，并分析固定角度比的二次反演歧义；以制备角膜前向/反向记录的束状ROI与中央ROI作经验谐波提取示范，并给出热扰动扩展的形式分析。

Result: 平行检偏含二、四、六次谐波，理想无检偏仅含二、四次；公式给出几何相关的谐波零点与灵敏度损失，指出无检偏四次谐波在ρ=1/3处为零并不意味完全丧失取向灵敏度；实测中束状ROI两次扫描均可解析二次谐波但归一化幅度不同、四次谐波未解析，中央ROI呈不同的前向/反向剖面，低信号空间覆盖与表观纤维排列在记录间略有差异。

Conclusion: 贡献在于对SHG谐波提供显式物理推断条件的解析诠释；测量支持角向测量的可行性，但未确立归一化形状的定量一致性，亦未确定绝对磁化率比ρ、轴向浓度κ及热无序与降解的实验分离。

Abstract: We derive closed-form relations between projected fibril-axis statistics and tensor-dependent second-harmonic generation (SHG) harmonics using a single axial von Mises population. Under coherent emission within domains and detected intensity addition across domains, each angular harmonic factors into a local tensor coefficient and an axial circular moment. Parallel-analyzer detection contains second, fourth and sixth harmonics; ideal equal-weight analyzer-free detection contains second and fourth harmonics. The resulting formulas identify geometry-specific harmonic zeros, sensitivity loss and ambiguous inversion of a secondary fixed-angle ratio. In particular, the analyzer-free fourth-harmonic zero at $ρ=1/3$ does not imply loss of all orientation sensitivity. Prepared-cornea forward/reverse recordings illustrate extraction of empirical analyzer-free harmonics. In the bundle ROI, second harmonics are resolved in both sweeps, while their normalized amplitudes differ and fourth harmonics remain unresolved. An exploratory central ROI from the same recordings yields a different forward/reverse profile. Within the same operator-identified tissue, low-signal space coverage and apparent fibre alignment vary slightly across recordings. These observations support angular-measurement feasibility while leaving quantitative normalized-shape agreement unestablished. The thermal extension illustrates consequences of assumed kinetics rather than calibrated tissue trajectories. The contribution is an analytical interpretation of SHG harmonics with explicit conditions for physical inference; the measurements do not establish absolute susceptibility ratio $ρ$, axial concentration $κ$, or experimental separation of thermal disorder from degradation.

</details>


### [156] [Bright and Uniform Silicon Carbide Nanophotonic Arrays for Scalable Nanoscale Quantum Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16252)
