*Chenyang Liao,Chihang Luo,Xin Chen,Cunliang Xin,Hongfang Zuo,Haoyu Sun,Fazhan Shi,Mengqi Wang,Ya Wang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文通过自对准工艺结合局部氧离子注入，在4H-SiC纳米柱中心确定性定位PL5与PL6色心，实现可规模化、超高亮度且高度均匀的SiC量子纳米光子阵列，并演示了纳米尺度核自旋探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SiC自旋缺陷因近表面稳定性、近红外发射和CMOS兼容性成为纳米尺度量子传感的有力平台，但光子收集效率低、缺陷随机分布限制了实用化大面积器件的实现。

Method: 将自对准制备工艺与局部氧离子注入相结合，在4H-SiC纳米柱的精确中心位置确定性放置PL5和PL6色心，构建可规模化的纳米光子阵列。

Result: 获得创纪录的光子计数率：PL5约1 Mcps、PL6约2 Mcps，树立SiC色心的新基准，同时阵列内亮度近乎一致；并将器件用于核磁共振，成功实现外部纳米尺度核自旋探测。

Conclusion: 该SiC纳米柱可作为扫描量子探针与可扩展传感器网络的稳健构建单元。

Abstract: Silicon carbide (SiC) spin defects are emerging as powerful platforms for nanoscale quantum sensing due to their near-surface stability, near-infrared emission, and CMOS compatibility. However, poor photon collection efficiency and the reliance on randomly distributed defects hinder the realization of practical, large-scale devices. Here, we demonstrate scalable, ultra-bright, and highly uniform SiC quantum nanophotonic arrays. By combining a self-aligned fabrication process with localized oxygen ion implantation, we deterministically position PL5 and PL6 centers at the exact center of 4H-SiC nanopillars. This architecture yields record-high photon count rates of $\sim$1 Mcps (PL5) and $\sim$2 Mcps (PL6), establishing a new benchmark for SiC color centers while maintaining near-identical brightness across the array. Furthermore, we apply these devices to nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR), successfully demonstrating external nanoscale nuclear-spin detection. These results establish our SiC nanopillars as robust building blocks for scanning quantum probes and scalable sensor networks.

</details>


### [157] [Theory of interferometric background suppression for highly sensitive electro-optic sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16296)
*Hiroaki Ito,Daiki Okazaki,Shigeki Tokita,Masato Ota*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出利用电光调制探测光与未调制参考光之间的相消干涉抑制背景的EO采样方法，可在不饱和光电二极管的前提下允许更高输入光功率，从而增强EO信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EO采样中增大探测光功率可提升EO信号，但与被测电场无关的庞大探测光背景会使光电探测器饱和，限制了可用功率。

Method: 通过EO调制探测光与未调制参考光的相消干涉抑制背景；建立Jones矩阵模型比较五种探测构型，指出平衡探测适用于在强背景抑制下保持线性弱场响应；并在单管饱和约束下对构型进行优化。

Result: 背景抑制后可提高输入光功率而不使光电二极管饱和，从而增强EO信号；在单管饱和约束下，优化后的信号增强近似随总输入功率的平方根增长。总输入功率5 W、单管饱和5 mW时，优化构型相对传统平衡探测获得约12倍信号增强。

Conclusion: 相消干涉背景抑制结合平衡探测可在探测器饱和约束下有效提升EO采样信号，且信号增强随总输入功率呈平方根标度，为高灵敏度EO采样提供可行方案。

Abstract: In electro-optic (EO) sampling, increasing the probe power enhances the EO signal, but the usable power can be limited by photodetector saturation caused by the much larger probe background independent of the applied electric field. Here, we propose a method for suppressing this background through destructive interference between an EO-modulated probe and an unmodulated reference beam. Using a Jones-matrix model, we compare five detection configurations and identify balanced detection as suitable for maintaining a linear weak-field response under strong background suppression. This suppression allows higher input optical power to be used without saturating the photodiodes, thereby enhancing the EO signal. Under a per-photodiode saturation constraint, the optimized signal enhancement scales approximately as the square root of the total input power. For a total input power of 5 W and a saturation limit of 5 mW per photodiode, the optimized configuration yields an approximately 12-fold signal enhancement over conventional balanced detection.

</details>


### [158] [An optical-frequency-comb-enabled unified infrastructure for quantum networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16349)
*Yiting Lin,Zishuo Ren,Yunkun Zhao,Jiayu Ma,Zimeng Li,Yang Sun,Yufei Zhang,Yanhao Sun,Lei Yang,Xiangyu Wang,Kemi Xu,Ziyang Chen,Hong Guo*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种基于光频梳的统一光纤基础设施，在同一链路上同时实现精密授时、经典通信与量子通信（CV-QKD），并给出噪声模型与优化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络需要精密时钟同步与经典通信来协调分布式量子操作，但二者通常由独立基础设施承载，导致系统复杂度上升、可扩展性受限。因此需要一种共享物理层的集成方案。

Method: 利用光频梳承载强度调制的经典数据同时保留脉冲时序；采用频谱工程策略抑制数据调制对定时提取的耦合；建立在共传播CV-QKD信道中引入额外噪声的功率依赖模型，识别光功率、波长分配与传播方向为主要控制参数并进行优化。

Result: 在100 km链路上同时实现100 Mbit/s数据传输与1 s处0.85 fs的定时稳定度；优化后CV-QKD仅引入0.0037 Shot Noise Unit的额外噪声，密钥率损失限制在6.7%；结合量子安全时间戳传输实现1 s处0.26 fs的绝对时间同步。

Conclusion: 光频梳可作为集成量子网络服务的公共物理层，统一授时、经典与量子通信，为可扩展量子网络提供可行路径。

Abstract: Quantum networks require precision timing and classical communication to coordinate distributed quantum operations, yet these functions are typically supported by separate infrastructures, increasing system complexity and limiting scalability. Here we demonstrate an optical-frequency-comb-enabled infrastructure that unifies precision timing, classical communication and quantum communication over a shared fibre link. By encoding classical data onto the intensity of the comb while retaining its pulse timing, we simultaneously transmit data at 100 Mbit/s and achieve a timing stability of 0.85 fs at 1 s over 100 km. We develop a spectral-engineering strategy to suppress the coupling between data modulation and timing extraction. We further establish a power-dependent model of the excess noise induced in a co-propagating continuous-variable quantum key distribution channel, identifying optical power, wavelength allocation and propagation direction as the principal control parameters. After optimization, the system introduces only 0.0037 shot noise unit of additional excess noise and limits the secret-key-rate penalty to 6.7%. Furthermore, quantum-secured timestamp transmission additionally enables absolute time synchronization with a stability of 0.26 fs at 1 s. These results establish optical frequency combs as a common physical layer for integrated quantum-network services.

</details>


### [159] [Speckle Angle: An Intrinsic Geometric Metric for High-Precision Speckle Metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16438)
*Alexander C. Trowbridge,Gabriel Britto Monteiro,Kishan Dholakia*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 将归一化散斑图视为高维单位球面上的点，提出"散斑角"作为散斑演化的内在几何坐标，实现小扰动下的线性响应与最大灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有散斑计量依赖相似度相关指标量化散斑演化，但相似度趋近1时其梯度消失，导致工作点附近局部灵敏度急剧下降，限制了对小扰动的探测与噪声分析能力。

Method: 把归一化散斑模式映射为单位球面上的点，定义两散斑模式间的散斑角（球面几何坐标）；在保持传统相似度方法物理标定的前提下推导其线性响应与灵敏度特性；并基于角坐标区分扰动动态与测量噪声，分离不相关噪声与固定模式噪声，导出一个受采集限制的分辨率极限；用积分球产生的散斑进行实验验证。

Result: 实验验证了该几何框架的有效性：散斑角在小扰动区具有线性响应和最大灵敏度，可定量估计不相关与固定模式噪声贡献，并给出采集受限的分辨率下界；同时提供统一指标以比较不同散斑计量架构。

Conclusion: 散斑角不仅改进了灵敏度表征与噪声分析，还建立了跨架构的通用度量，并为基于散斑矢量空间轨迹的多维传感奠定基础。

Abstract: Speckle-based metrology exploits the extreme sensitivity of complex interference patterns to perturbations in wavelength, refractive index, displacement and other physical parameters. Existing approaches typically quantify speckle evolution using similarity-based correlation metrics, whose local sensitivity deteriorates near the operating point because their gradient vanishes as the similarity approaches unity. Here we show that normalized speckle patterns represent points on the surface of a high-dimensional unit sphere and introduce the speckle angle: the intrinsic geometric coordinate between speckle patterns. The speckle angle preserves the physical calibration of established similarity approaches while providing a linear response and maximal sensitivity in the small-perturbation regime. This geometric perspective enables discrimination of perturbation dynamics from measurement noise, enabling direct estimation of uncorrelated and fixed-pattern noise contributions and yielding a fundamental acquisition-limited resolution bound. Using speckle generated by an integrating sphere, we experimentally validate the framework and demonstrate quantitative noise analysis using angular coordinates. Beyond improving sensitivity characterization, the speckle angle establishes an original common metric for comparing disparate speckle metrology architectures and provides a foundation for multidimensional sensing based on trajectories through speckle-vector space.

</details>


### [160] [TARANG: an open-source, cost-controlled design workflow for silicon nitride microcomb chips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16469)
*Divyanshu Tiwari,Esraaj Sarkar Gupta,Anshuman Kumar*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: TARANG是一个开源Python工具包，可在14个阶段内完成从氮化硅材料折射率到GDSII掩模版的全流程微腔光频梳芯片设计，并通过网格外延/内延解平均法将群速度色散计算稳定到约1 ps/(nm·km)，输出了两个可流片的完整设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微环谐振腔频率梳芯片的设计是一连串耦合问题：需同时满足抗工艺误差的异常色散波导、合适FSR与Q值的环、决定耦合区的总线间隙以及光栅耦合器，且须在单次光刻中实现。现有流程缺乏端到端自动化工具，且色散计算对网格对齐方式敏感，结果随计算机浮点舍入而漂移。

Method: 提出TARANG开源Python包（基于Tidy3D的mode、FDTD与特征模展开求解器），将设计流程拆为14个阶段。云仿真按预算计价与限额、结果以完整仿真指纹缓存、每个数值附数值校验。方法学上指出现有"将网格吸附到芯层界面"的常规做法会使界面场采样陷入浮点舍入问题，改用网格分别略微内缩与略微外扩两次求解取平均。

Result: 平均法使群速度色散D与计算平台无关，且跨网格稳定在约1 ps/(nm·km)。给出两个空气包层完整设计：1550 nm（700×1000 nm芯层，D = 372.9 ± 0.6 ps/(nm·km)）与780 nm（350×500 nm芯层，D = 196.4 ± 0.9 ps/(nm·km)），各含200 GHz微环、临界与过耦合间隙、光栅耦合器及光纤到光纤预算。预测色散与文献中两个实测器件的偏差分别为1%与4%；独立Lugiato-Lefever求解器用两设计导出的参数均形成稳定单孤子。

Conclusion: TARANG实现了氮化硅微腔光频梳芯片从材料数据到GDSII的自动化、可复现设计流程；其网格内外延平均策略解决了色散计算的数值不确定性；两个设计经实验数据与孤子仿真双重验证，已具备流片条件。

Abstract: Designing a microresonator frequency comb chip means solving a chain of coupled problems: a waveguide with anomalous dispersion that survives fabrication errors, a ring with the right free spectral range and quality factor, a bus gap that sets the coupling regime, and grating couplers, all made in one lithography step. We present TARANG, an open-source Python package that takes a silicon nitride platform from tabulated refractive indices to a GDSII mask in fourteen stages, built on the Tidy3D mode, FDTD and eigenmode-expansion solvers. Every cloud simulation is priced before it runs and capped, every result is cached by a fingerprint of the full simulation, and every quoted number carries a numerical check. We show that snapping the grid onto the core's faces, a common way to steady dispersion calculations, leaves the field samples on the faces to floating-point rounding, and that averaging solves with the grid just inside and just outside the faces makes the group-velocity dispersion $D$ independent of the computer and steady to about 1 ps/(nm km) from mesh to mesh. Two complete air-clad designs are presented, at 1550 nm ($700\times1000$ nm core, $D = 372.9 \pm 0.6$ ps/(nm km)) and at 780 nm ($350\times500$ nm, $D = 196.4 \pm 0.9$ ps/(nm km)), each with a 200 GHz ring, critical and over-coupled gaps, grating couplers, and a fibre-to-fibre budget. The predicted dispersion agrees with two measured devices from the literature to within 1 and 4%, and an independent Lugiato-Lefever solver forms stable single solitons from the exported parameters of both designs, which are ready for fabrication.

</details>


### [161] [Design and optimization of multilevel zone plates for flying focus applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16497)
*Zhengkun Li,Yisong Zhou,Changbo Fu,Yugang Ma*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种2^M台阶调制宽度波带片(MWZP-2^M S)，通过正弦随机宽度的蒙特卡洛采样抑制高阶焦点并提升聚焦效率，实现速度可调的飞焦点脉冲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞焦点脉冲速度可编程、与群速度解耦，在高强度激光-物质相互作用中潜力巨大，但低成本制造的同时抑制高阶焦点并保持高效率仍是关键难题。

Method: 设计调制宽度波带片MWZP-2^M S：对正弦随机宽度进行蒙特卡洛采样，使MWZP-8S在统计上模拟Gabor波带片；并利用多级相位调制将不透明区域转化为相长相位调制区。

Result: 高阶焦点被抑制约四个数量级；M=3时聚焦效率提升至73.7%；数值模拟显示焦点速度可在亚光速与超光速区间灵活调节，并在100个瑞利长度上保持稳定。

Conclusion: 该方案兼具可扩展性与低成本，为EUV产生和X射线光学中的飞焦点应用提供了稳健平台。

Abstract: Flying focus pulses, characterized by programmable velocities decoupled from the group velocity, offer remarkable potential for high-intensity laser-matter interactions. However, achieving low-cost manufacturing while simultaneously suppressing higher-order foci and maintaining high efficiency remains a key challenge. Here, we propose a modulated width zone plate with $2^{M}$ steps (MWZP-$2^{M}$S). By employing Monte Carlo sampling of sinusoidal random widths, the MWZP-8S design statistically mimics a Gabor zone plate, suppressing higher-order foci by four orders of magnitude. Furthermore, the multilevel phase modulation converts opaque regions into constructive phase-shifting zones, significantly boosting the focusing efficiency to 73.7$\%$ when $M=3$. Numerical simulations demonstrate that the focal velocity is flexibly tunable across subluminal and superluminal regimes while remaining stable over 100 Rayleigh lengths. This scalable and cost-effective scheme provides a robust platform for flying focus applications in EUV generation and X-ray optics.

</details>


### [162] [PISim: A Unified Photonic Inference Simulator for Hardware-Aware Cross-Layer Design-Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16522)
*Andreas Falkenberg*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: PISim：跨层光子推理仿真器，耦合预训练DNN执行与物理非理想模型，用于流片前设计空间探索与精度-性能Pareto优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子神经加速器具备超低延迟线性代数能力，但可用数值精度、可持续吞吐与应用精度受电光接口及物理非理想性严格制约；流片前设计空间探索缺乏能在相同神经负载与物理假设下评估不同光学拓扑的跨层EDA框架。

Method: 构建统一光子推理仿真器PISim：将预训练DNN执行与六类加速器架构、六种电光调制器、四种光源组织、直接/相干读出方案的物理模型耦合；嵌入分级损伤算子（控制与ADC量化、有限消光比、散粒噪声、相关RIN、热漂移、传播损耗、通道串扰）及解析周期级接口时序模型。

Result: 在MNIST MLP基准上：多损伤压力下识别最优精度工作点（MZM与微环调制器峰值6 bit）；量化VCSEL阵列与并行分光激光架构间52.5个百分点的精度差距（源于功率分配与相关源噪声）；建模32–128通道发射阵列宽度下的接口受限吞吐扩展。

Conclusion: PISim为光子流片前的精度约束Pareto优化提供可扩展、可复现的跨层基础框架。

Abstract: Photonic neural accelerators offer ultra-low latency linear algebra, yet their usable numerical precision, sustainable throughput, and application accuracy remain tightly bounded by surrounding electro-optical interfaces and physical non-idealities. Pre-fabrication design-space exploration (DSE) requires a cross-layer electronic design automation (EDA) framework capable of evaluating disparate optical topologies under identical neural workloads and physical assumptions. We present \textbf{PISim}, a unified Photonic Inference Simulator that couples pretrained deep neural network execution with physics-based models of six accelerator architecture families, six electro-optic modulator technologies, four optical source organizations, and direct or coherent readout schemes. PISim embeds stage-resolved impairment operators---covering control and analog-to-digital quantization, finite extinction ratio, shot noise, correlated relative-intensity noise (RIN), thermal drift, propagation loss, and channel crosstalk---alongside an analytical cycle-level interface timing model to expose electronic-optical bottlenecks. Using simulated MNIST multilayer perceptron (MLP) benchmarks, we demonstrate how PISim identifies optimal precision operating points under multi-impairment stress (peaking at 6 bits for Mach--Zehnder and microring modulators), quantifies a 52.5 percentage-point accuracy gap between VCSEL-array and parallel-fanout laser architectures arising, under the modeled conditions, from power partitioning and correlated source noise, and models interface-limited throughput scaling across emitter-array widths from 32 to 128 channels. This comprehensive framework establishes an extensible, reproducible foundation for accuracy-constrained Pareto optimization prior to photonic tape-out.

</details>


### [163] [Phonon-mediated visible upconversion luminescence in femtosecond 1030-nm pumped Tm3+ -doped silica fibres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16582)
*Nicolò Pisani,Raffaele Filosa,Alessia Cirimele,Lucas Zanaglia,Jan Aubrecht,Michael Kamrádek,Ivan Kašík,Ivo Bartoň,Ondřej Podrazký,Jan Mrázek,Mario Ferraro*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 在1030 nm飞秒脉冲串激发的掺铥铝硅酸盐光纤中观测到多光子吸收驱动的可见发光，并发现457 nm与475 nm发射带随功率递增发生主导地位转换及792 nm近红外发射的双线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高功率超快光纤系统的热与光谱稳定性缺乏可预测认识，需厘清功率升高时可见光谱演化的物理机制。

Method: 在MHz重频、1030 nm飞秒脉冲串激发掺铥铝硅酸盐光纤；采用定标的光谱带积分，并结合基于基质热力学的结构信息速率方程模型进行机理分析。

Result: 随入射平均功率增加，457 nm深蓝发射带被475 nm青色发射带逐步取代，呈反常光谱交叉；792 nm近红外发射持续单调增强并呈明确的双线性斜率转折。模型表明光谱演化由累积纤芯加热激活的非辐射多声子转移与声子辅助交叉弛豫主导，而非永久性色心日晒效应。

Conclusion: 该研究给出热致光谱演化的定量解释，为高功率超快光纤系统的热与光谱稳定性提供可预测性见解。

Abstract: We investigate multiphoton absorption-driven visible luminescence in a thulium-doped aluminosilicate optical fiber excited by an ultrafast 1030-nm femtosecond pulse train in the {MHz domain}. We report the observation of an unusual spectral crossover: the predominance of an emission band centered at 457-nm (deep blue) is progressively overtaken by a second emission, centered at 475-nm (cyan), as launched average power increases. Concurrently, a third near-infrared emission around 792~nm exhibits persistent, monotonic growth characterized by a well-defined bilinear slope transition. By combining calibrated spectral band integration with a structure-informed rate-equation model based on host thermodynamics, we show that the visible spectra evolution is governed by non-radiative multi-phonon transfer and phonon-assisted cross-relaxation activated by cumulative core heating, rather than permanent color-center solarization. These results offer new predictive insights into the thermal and spectroscopic stability of high-power ultrafast fiber systems.

</details>


### [164] [Optical fiber shape sensing using picosecond spatiotemporal single-photon reflectometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16717)
*Harikumar K Chandrasekharan,Saba N. Khan,Muhammad Afnan Ansari,Robert R. Thomson*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种仅从近端单端测量、利用多模光纤中返回单光子的时空动力学重建光纤三维形状的新传感范式，结合激光刻写多模光纤与32×32时间分辨SPAD阵列，实现弯曲角连续估计与未见形状重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单一可达端推断柔性光纤的三维构型是光子传感中的基础难题；现有方法依赖专用多芯光纤、相干干涉测量或分布式光谱编码，硬件复杂、成本高且适用性受限，亟需一种单端、非干涉、信息丰富的替代方案。

Method: 采用激光工程化多模光纤，用时间分辨32×32单光子雪崩二极管（SPAD）阵列进行询问；揭示形变会在背反射光子的空间分布与到达时间上留下独特签名；将高维光子返回态映射到低维几何流形，从而实现连续弯曲角估计及二维/三维形状重建，全部测量均在光纤近端完成。

Result: 像素级信噪比中位数约26 dB，重复性变化低于1%；可连续估计弯曲角，并重建此前未见的二维与三维形状。

Conclusion: 将多模单光子动力学确立为分布式几何传感的高信息量通道，提出单端光纤形状重建新框架，为微创光子导航等应用提供可能。

Abstract: Inferring the three-dimensional configuration of a flexible optical fiber from a single accessible end remains a fundamental challenge in photonic sensing. Existing approaches typically require specialised multicore fibers, coherent interferometry, or distributed spectral encoding. Here, we demonstrate a fundamentally different sensing paradigm in which fiber geometry is reconstructed from the spatiotemporal dynamics of returning single photons. Using a laser-engineered multimode fiber interrogated by a time-resolved 32x32 single-photon avalanche diode array, we show that fiber deformation imprints unique signatures onto the distribution and arrival time of back-reflected photons. We map these high-dimensional photon-return states onto a low-dimensional geometric manifold, enabling continuous bend-angle estimation and reconstruction of previously unseen two- and three-dimensional shapes from measurements acquired entirely at the proximal fiber end. Measurements show a median pixel-wise signal-to-noise ratio of approximately 26 dB and below 1% repeatability variation. Our results establish multimode single-photon dynamics as an information-rich channel for distributed geometric sensing, introducing a new framework for single-ended fiber shape reconstruction and minimally invasive photonic navigation.

</details>


### [165] [Chiral high harmonic generation via coherent superposition of multi-channel interband transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16721)
*Ruoxuan Ding,Chen Qian,Zhilin Li,Tianchen Hu,Chunmei Zhang,Tao Dong,Dong Wu,Li Yue,Nan-Lin Wang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 在Weyl半金属NbP中，单一线偏振近红外驱动场即可产生具有显著椭圆率的高次谐波，其机制为多个带间跃迁的相干叠加，从而实现无需复杂驱动场或材料工程的 chiral HHG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆偏振光可用于探测材料的结构、电子、磁性与拓扑 chirality，现有实现手性HHG的方法依赖多维驱动场、材料工程或复杂光学架构，缺乏简单普适的方案。

Method: 以Weyl半金属NbP为对象，用单一线偏振近红外驱动场激发HHG并测量谐波椭圆率；采用半导体Bloch方程（SBEs）分析其物理起源；并将驱动光子能量移至中红外以检验机制。

Result: 线偏振驱动在NbP中产生显著椭圆率的高次谐波；SBE表明椭圆率源于多个带间跃迁谐波的相干叠加——各分量具有不同线偏振方向与相对相位；将驱动光子能量移至中红外后，可及带间跃迁通道数减少，椭圆率显著被抑制，验证了该机制。

Conclusion: 提出了一种简单且普适的手性HHG机制：量子材料可将表观非手性的驱动场转换为偏振可调的超快手性辐射。

Abstract: Circularly polarized light, with its specific handedness, is a valuable tool for investigating the chirality related properties of materials, including their structural, electronic, magnetic and topological characteristics. This has led to efforts in creating wavelength-tunable coherent light with circular or elliptical polarization through a sub-cycle ultrafast process high-harmonic generation (HHG). Existing approaches to chiral HHG, however, typically rely on multidimensional driving fields, materials engineering or complex optical architectures. Here we uncover a distinct mechanism without such complexity, where a single linearly polarized driving field generates chiral HHG from a pristine crystal. We show that, in the Weyl semimetal NbP, one linearly polarized near-infrared driving field generates high harmonics with pronounced ellipticity. Semi-conductor Bloch equations (SBEs) reveal that this ellipticity originates from the coherent superposition of multiple interband transitions, each contributing harmonics with distinct linear polarizations and relative phases. We further substantiate this mechanism by shifting the driving photon energy into the mid-infrared, which strongly suppresses the ellipticity as the number of accessible interband transition channels is reduced. These results establish a simple and general mechanism for chiral HHG, and show that quantum materials can transform an apparently achiral driving field into polarization-tunable ultrafast chiral emission.

</details>


### [166] [Enabling Mie Resonances for Optomagnetism in Epitaxially Grown Garnet Nanodisks via Photonic Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16788)
*P. V. Zorina,D. O. Ignatyeva,D. M. Krichevsky,V. I. Belotelov*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种可与GGG外延生长的全石榴石光子晶体衬底，通过与单枚铋铁石榴石纳米盘的光子环境耦合来调控其光磁响应，逆法拉第效应的局域光场贡献最高提升约7.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望在保持纳米盘几何结构不变的前提下，通过光子环境（衬底）而非结构改形来调控光磁响应（逆法拉第效应）的幅度与空间分布，并获得与外延生长兼容的衬底方案。

Method: 设计全石榴石光子晶体衬底（与钆镓石榴石外延兼容），承载单个铋铁石榴石纳米盘；对比均匀衬底参考样品，分析电偶极与磁偶极贡献的谱位与相对强度变化，以及盘内光场再分布，并计算轴向光场对逆法拉第效应的贡献。

Result: 在磁偶极主导与电偶极主导区域，所考察截面的轴向光学贡献峰值分别提升约7.3倍与6.3倍；在多极散射总贡献最大处提升约7.9倍。

Conclusion: 光子晶体衬底可在不改动纳米盘几何的前提下，实现对局域光磁响应幅度与空间分布的谱调控，为全石榴石集成光磁器件提供可行途径。

Abstract: An all-garnet photonic-crystal substrate compatible with epitaxial growth on gadolinium gallium garnet is proposed for controlling the optomagnetic response of a single bismuth iron garnet nanodisk. The photonic environment modifies the spectral positions and relative strengths of the electric- and magnetic-dipole contributions and redistributes the optical field inside the disk. Compared with a reference nanodisk on a homogeneous substrate evaluated near the broad maximum of its summed multipolar response, the peak axial optical contribution to the inverse Faraday effect in the examined cross-sections is enhanced by factors of approximately 7.3 and 6.3 in the magnetic- and electric-dipole-dominated regimes, respectively. The enhancement reaches approximately 7.9 at the maximum of the summed multipolar scattering contributions. These results demonstrate spectral control of the magnitude and spatial distribution of the local optomagnetic response through the photonic environment, without changing the nanodisk geometry.

</details>


### [167] [Ultracompact and Nonvolatile On-chip Switch Based on DBS and PSO Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16855)
*Sanaz Zarei*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 基于SOI-Sb2Se3混合平台的1×2非易失光子开关，采用DBS与PSO逆设计，通过Sb2Se3相变单元整体切换实现开关功能，尺寸仅6.3μm×31μm，在1550nm处插损<0.96dB、串扰<-12dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光子开关多依赖热光或电光效应，存在功耗高、易失性等问题；利用Sb2Se3相变材料的非易失特性，并结合逆设计提升器件紧凑性与性能。

Method: 在SOI-Sb2Se3混合平台上，以最少可切换相变单元构建设计域，采用直接二进制搜索（DBS）与粒子群优化（PSO）进行逆设计，器件工作于所有Sb2Se3单元在晶态与非晶态之间整体（en-masse）切换的机制。

Result: 器件足迹仅6.3μm×31μm；1550nm波长下插入损耗小于0.96dB，三维FDTD仿真中串扰小于-12dB。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了逆设计结合Sb2Se3相变材料可实现紧凑、低损耗、低串扰的非易失1×2光子开关，为高密度集成光子开关阵列提供可行方案。

Abstract: A 1x2 photonic switch based on silicon-on-insulator (SOI)-Sb2Se3 hybrid platform is inverse-designed using direct binary search (DBS) and particle swarm optimization (PSO) algorithms. The nonvolatile on-chip switch is designed to consist of minimum switchable phase-change units and operates based on the en-masse switching of all Sb2Se3 cells from crystalline to amorphous and vice-versa. The device has a small footprint of only 6.3umx31um. At the wavelength of 1550nm, it exhibits an insertion loss of less than 0.96dB, and its crosstalk is measured to be less than -12dB within the three-dimensional (3D) finite-difference time-domain (FDTD) simulations.

</details>


### [168] [Inverse Design of an On-chip Phase-Change Switch Composed of Sparse Heterogeneous Metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16863)
*Sanaz Zarei*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作逆向设计了一种基于片上异质一维超表面的1×2硅基光子开关（尺寸6.3 μm×31 μm），利用SiO2与相变材料Sb2Se3构成超原子，在非晶态下获得更高消光比，并采用稀疏参数化结合三元搜索与粒子群优化实现结构简化的逆向设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电光、热光光子开关存在固有缺陷（如功耗、损耗、调谐速度等）。将相变材料与硅光子开关结合可规避这些问题，但Sb2Se3非晶态与硅的折射率对比度小，导致开关两输出的消光比不足，因此需要提升非晶态的消光比，同时兼顾结构与制造、切换的简便性。

Method: 逆向设计1×2片上开关：构建由SiO2或相变材料Sb2Se3两种材料组成的异质一维片上超表面（meta-atoms），利用不同态（晶态/非晶态）折射率差异实现开关切换；为简化结构、制造与切换，采用稀疏参数化，并以三元搜索（TS）和粒子群优化（PSO）算法进行优化。

Result: 获得尺寸仅6.3 μm×31 μm的紧凑1×2片上开关；异质超原子设计提升了开关输出间的消光比，尤其在非晶态下（与硅折射率对比度小的情况）效果显著；稀疏参数化使结构保持简单、易于制造和切换。

Conclusion: 异质一维超表面结合相变材料Sb2Se3与稀疏参数化逆向设计（TS/PSO），可有效实现紧凑、高消光比、制造与切换简便的硅基光子开关，为相变材料集成光子开关提供可行方案。

Abstract: Photonic switches are exploited for optical signal routing and control within optical communication systems. The integration of phase change materials with silicon photonic switches overcomes the drawbacks of the state-of-the-art photonic switches based on electro-optic and thermo-optic effects. In this work, a 1x2 on-chip switch with a footprint of 6.3umx31um based on on-chip one-dimensional metasurfaces is inverse-designed. The on-chip metasurfaces are heterogeneous, which their meta-atoms can be composed of either SiO2 or the phase-change material Sb2Se3. This helps to the realization of higher extinction ratio between the switch outputs, especially at the amorphous state which has a small refractive index contrast to that of the silicon. To benefit from structural, fabrication and switching simplicity, sparse parameterization is followed in the inverse design of the switch using ternary search (TS) and particle swarm optimization (PSO) algorithms.

</details>


### [169] [Chlorophyll-a Fluorescence-Based Optical Temperature Sensor Using Food-Grade Chlorella Extract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16896)
*Sinei Felix Gonçalves Junior,Giovanna G. S. de Miranda,Leomar S. Marques,Flavio A. M. Marques,Alexandre A. C. Cotta,Jefferson E. Tsuchida,Julio C. Ugucioni,Lidia O. Rosa,Eric Fujiwara,Cristiano M. B. Cordeiro,Jonas H. Osório*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 基于小球藻提取物叶绿素a荧光热猝灭的光学温度传感器，在28.6–43.0 °C呈现线性响应，灵敏度约−8.9×10⁻³ °C⁻¹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索可持续、生物来源的微藻色素作为功能性光活性材料用于光学测温的可行性，替代合成荧光材料。

Method: 采用简便的乙醇萃取法从食品级小球藻生物质中提取叶绿素a，以其特征荧光发射作为光学换能参数；基于热致荧光猝灭（温度升高、发射强度下降）实现测温，并进行连续升降温循环的动态测试与光漂移表征。

Result: 在28.6–43.0 °C区间呈线性响应（R²=0.9963），灵敏度为(−8.9±0.2)×10⁻³ °C⁻¹；动态循环测试证实可分辨亚摄氏度热振荡，并测得光漂移率为3.65%·h⁻¹。

Conclusion: 结果证明生物源微藻色素可作为功能性光活性材料用于光学测温，为低成本、可持续的温度传感平台提供了可行性依据。

Abstract: This letter reports the development of an optical temperature sensor based on the fluorescence of an extract obtained from \textit{Chlorella}, a unicellular microalga commercially available as food-grade biomass. Using a straightforward ethanolic extraction route, the sensing platform exploits the characteristic emission of chlorophyll-\textit{a} as the optical transducing parameter. The operating principle relies on thermal fluorescence quenching, which promotes a decrease in emission intensity with rising temperature. In the temperature range from $28.6$ to $43.0~^{\circ}\text{C}$, the sensor exhibited a linear response ($R^2 = 0.9963$) with a sensitivity of $(-8.9 \pm 0.2) \times 10^{-3}~^{\circ}\text{C}^{-1}$. Dynamic testing over consecutive heating--cooling cycles confirmed the sensor operation under sub-degree thermal oscillations and enabled the characterization of the system's photobleaching drift ($3.65\%\cdot\text{h}^{-1}$). The results reported herein demonstrate the feasibility of exploiting sustainable, bio-derived microalgal pigments as functional photoactive materials for optical thermometry.

</details>


### [170] [Enhancing optomechanical oscillation amplitude and frequency comb bandwidth by intensity-modulated driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16917)
*Menno H. Jansen,Ewold Verhagen*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作研究通过腔内反作用与调制光驱动结合，在真实资源约束下光机械自持振荡所能达到的最大振幅，并证明强度调制驱动可在相同平均功率与线宽下逼近带宽所设定的振幅极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光机械自持振荡可作为集成光子电路中紧凑、低噪声的GHz信号源与光频梳，而振幅直接决定时钟功率与梳带宽；但有限腔带宽使振幅饱和，反作用单独无法突破该限制，需寻找在功率受限条件下的简单提升途径。

Method: 采用周期平均能量平衡分析识别将稳定周期轨道推向更大振幅的附加功率；并对完整运动方程进行数值积分，在跨越两个数量级的协同度以及0.1–10边带分辨率比值范围内验证。

Result: 在机械共振频率附近调制输入强度，可在相同平均输入功率与腔线宽下将振幅提升至接近带宽极限；振幅相对增幅在自振阈值附近及边带未分辨区最大，实现反作用单独无法达到的无阈值大振幅振荡；增幅同时带来更大的光场调制深度、更宽的频率梳及更稳定的梳间距。

Conclusion: 强度调制驱动为功率受限平台提供了一种简单的开环方案，可获得更大的机械振幅与增强的梳带宽。

Abstract: Self-sustained oscillations in optomechanical systems are attractive as compact, low-noise sources of GHz signals and optical frequency combs for integrated photonic circuits. For these applications, the achievable oscillation amplitude directly sets clock power and comb bandwidth. This work studies the maximum amplitude that can be reached through combinations of cavity backaction and modulated optical driving, with realistic resources. For an optically driven mechanical resonator, the oscillation amplitude saturates at a level set by the finite cavity bandwidth. We show that modulating the input intensity near the mechanical resonance frequency raises the achievable amplitude toward this limit at equal mean input power and cavity linewidth. We use a cycle-averaged energy-balance analysis to identify the added power that shifts the stable periodic orbit to larger amplitudes. Numerical integration of the full equations of motion confirms the enhancement across two decades of cooperativity and sideband-resolution ratios from 0.1 to 10. The relative increase in amplitude is largest near the self-oscillation threshold and in the sideband-unresolved regime, enabling threshold-free large-amplitude oscillation where backaction alone fails to do so. The enhanced amplitude leads to a larger modulation depth of the optical field, enabling broader frequency combs, with improved stability of the comb spacing. Intensity-modulated driving thus offers power-constrained platforms a simple open-loop route to larger mechanical amplitudes and enhanced comb bandwidth.

</details>


### [171] [Asymmetric Frequency Conversion and Violation of Kirchhoff's Law of Thermal Radiation in Phased Floquet Metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17062)
*Yao-Ting Wang,Emanuele Galiffi,Sander A. Mann,Mauro Antezza,Andrea Alù*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 三层近距离导电薄片组成的系统中，通过对其中一层超表面进行时间调制，利用Floquet现象驱动的表面模式转变，实现高度不对称的频率转换，使热发射大幅违背基尔霍夫定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热发射受基尔霍夫定律约束，实现非互易热辐射通常依赖磁光材料或外磁场，集成困难。作者希望借助Floquet时间调制在无磁条件下突破这一限制。

Method: 构建三层紧邻导电薄片体系；对其中一层超表面施加时间调制（Floquet调制），诱导表面模式在时间维度上发生转变，从而产生非互易的频率转换通道。

Result: 热发射大幅违背基尔霍夫定律，频率转换呈现显著的非对称性。

Conclusion: Floquet超表面可用于波转换与能量调控，为无磁非互易光子器件提供途径，并有望应用于热管理与高效光子制冷。

Abstract: We study a system of three closely spaced conducting sheets, whose thermal emission largely violates Kirchhoff's law through nonreciprocal frequency conversion enabled by Floquet phenomena. We leverage surface mode transitions controlled by temporal modulation of one of the metasurfaces, resulting in largely asymmetric frequency conversion. Our findings broaden the capabilities of Floquet metasurfaces for wave conversion and energy manipulation, paving the way towards magnet-free nonreciprocal photonic devices with opportunities for thermal management and efficient photonic refrigeration.

</details>


### [172] [Thulium- and Holmium-Doped Silica Fibres for 2 μm High-Power and Highly-Efficient Lasers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16948)
*Richard Svejkar,Pavel Peterka,Bara Svejkarova,Michal Kamradek,Petr Varak,Martin Grabner,Jan Aubrecht,Ondrej Podrazky,Ivo Barton,Ivan Kasik,Pavel Honzatko*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该综述总结掺铥/掺钬石英光纤在2 μm波段的研究进展，重点讨论光谱温度特性、结构化纤芯设计与纳米颗粒辅助MCVD工艺对功率提升和效率优化的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石英光纤已从通信传输介质演变为高功率激光平台，但2 μm波段增益光纤在热负载、效率与高浓度掺杂间的矛盾制约功率 scaling，需要从材料与波导层面系统梳理解决路径。

Method: 综述性分析：讨论吸收/发射截面的温度依赖性及其对增益谱、ASE与高功率运行的影响；评述结构化纤芯Tm光纤以兼顾高局部Tm3+浓度（二对一交叉弛豫）与低平均掺杂、低纵向热负载及无基座波导；总结纳米颗粒辅助MCVD制备的Ho掺杂石英光纤中Al/Ho比与绝对铝含量对效率、荧光寿命和团簇的调控。

Result: Al/Ho比被确认为决定激光效率的关键组分参数，高绝对氧化铝含量可支持长荧光寿命并允许高Ho3+浓度而不产生过度团簇；由此实现纤芯泵浦Ho光纤激光器斜率效率达84%、输出功率接近100 W。

Conclusion: 通过协同调控光谱特性、玻璃组分与横向掺杂分布，可拓展石英光纤激光器在1.7–2.2 μm波段的性能边界。

Abstract: Silica optical fibres, originally developed as a low-loss transmission medium for telecommunications, have evolved into a versatile platform for compact and efficient high-power lasers. This review discusses recent progress in thulium- and holmium-doped active fibres emitting within the 2 μm spectral band, with an emphasis on material and waveguide approaches that address power scaling through thermal-load mitigation and enhanced laser efficiency. In particular, the temperature dependence of absorption and emission cross-sections are reviewed, and their implications for spectral gain, amplified spontaneous emission and high-power laser operation are discussed. Structured-core thulium fibres are then considered as a route to combining the high local Tm3+ concentration required for efficient two for- one cross-relaxation with a reduced average dopant concentration, lower longitudinal heat load, and a pedestal-free waveguide design. Finally, recent results on holmium-doped silica fibres prepared by nanoparticle-assisted modified chemical vapour deposition are summarised. Crucially, the Al/Ho ratio is identified as a key compositional parameter governing laser efficiency, while a high absolute alumina content supports a long fluorescence lifetime and enables high Ho3+ concentrations without excessive clustering. These advances have enabled core-pumped holmium fibre lasers with slope efficiencies reaching 84% and output powers approaching 100W. Collectively, these development illustrate how control of spectroscopy, composition, and transverse dopant distribution can extend the performance of silica fibre lasers across the 1.7-2.2 μm spectral region.

</details>


### [173] [Monolayer MoSe2 as an Attenuator of Spintronic Terahertz Emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16958)
*Hatice Nur Koyun,Alexej Pashkin,Ruslan Salikhov,Artur Erbe,Jürgen Faßbender,Manfred Helm,Stephan Winnerl,Jürgen Lindner*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 在Py/Au和Py/Pt自旋电子THz发射体上覆盖单层MoSe2后，发射THz振幅一致衰减约4倍，且与发射体本征强度无关；作者将其归因于TMD中自由载流子和三子的THz吸收。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁/二维材料界面的超快自旋-电荷转换对THz自旋电子器件（超快存储器、传感器、紧凑THz源）至关重要，但FM/TMD体系中观测到的自旋电子THz发射（STE）信号远弱于磁输运所报道的显著自旋轨道矩，二者存在矛盾，需要厘清其物理起源。

Method: 采用时域THz光谱研究覆盖单层TMD（MoSe2）的Py/Au异质结构的自旋电子THz发射，并与Py/Pt发射体对照，比较有/无MoSe2覆盖区域的THz振幅，同时结合裸发射体强度差异分析衰减机制。

Result: 单层MoSe2覆盖使Py/Au与Py/Pt的THz发射振幅均显著降低，衰减倍数一致约为4倍，尽管裸Py/Pt的THz辐射强度高出近一个数量级，说明该抑制与发射体强度无关。

Conclusion: THz衰减源于MoSe2中自由载流子与三子（由金属层电荷转移或激光脉冲光激发产生）对THz的吸收，而非自旋-电荷转换效率本身降低；这解释了FM/TMD异质结构中弱STE信号与磁输运报道的大自旋轨道矩之间的矛盾，并提示在二维TMD异质结的超快自旋-电荷转换测量中必须考虑光致THz衰减效应。

Abstract: Understanding ultrafast spin-to-charge conversion at ferromagnetic/2D interfaces is critical for developing THz-frequency spintronic devices such as ultrafast memories, sensors, and compact THz sources. We investigate this by studying spintronic THz emission (STE) from Py/Au heterostructures capped with transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) monolayer MoSe2 using time-domain THz spectroscopy. We observe a pronounced reduction in emitted THz amplitude when a monolayer MoSe2 flake covers Py/Au or Py/Pt emitters compared with uncovered regions. A consistent, approximately fourfold attenuation occurs for both systems, despite an order-of-magnitude larger THz radiation from the bare Py/Pt emitter. These results indicate that the suppression is independent of emitter strength. We attribute the attenuation to THz absorption by free charge carriers and trions that are either present due to charge transfer from the metallic layer or photoexcited by the laser pulse, which can significantly diminish the detectable STE signal. This finding provides an explanation for the discrepancy between weak STE signals from FM/TMD heterostructures and magnetotransport reports of sizable spin-orbit torques in similar systems, and highlights the need to account for photoinduced THz attenuation when interpreting ultrafast spin-to-charge conversion measurements in 2D TMD heterostructures.

</details>


### [174] [UV and deep-UV ultrafast harmonic generation enhanced by resonant gold nano-pillar metasurface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17037)
*Shroddha Mukhopadhyay,Yuxuan Chen,Hooman Barati Sedeh,Natalia M. Litchinister,Michael Scalora,Jose Trull,Maria Antonietta Vincenti,Crina Cojocaru*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 二维周期金纳米柱超表面上可调谐等离激元共振增强二次与三次谐波产生，相对平面金镜转换效率分别提升约两个和三个数量级，理论与实验吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现从近红外到紫外及深紫外波段的高效超快光频率转换，并避免依赖复杂几何或混合结构带来的制备难题。

Method: 设计周期排列的方形金纳米柱超表面，在近红外区支持角度可调谐等离激元共振；结合实验观测与全矢量数值模拟，对线性与非线性光-物质相互作用进行全波理论建模。

Result: 共振条件下纳米柱棱角处强电磁场局域显著增强非线性作用；相比平面金镜，二次谐波与三次谐波转换效率分别提升两个和三个数量级；理论预测与实验测量高度一致。

Conclusion: 简单方形金柱周期结构即可通过结构设计定制共振波长，实现增强的二次/三次谐波产生；深化了对金属表面介导非线性光学过程的理解，并展示了金属纳米结构作为紫外及深紫外超快光源的潜力。

Abstract: We report experimental observations and full-vector numerical simulations of second and third-harmonic generation from a two-dimensional periodic gold nano-pillar metasurface, for efficient ultrafast light conversion from near-infrared to the UV and deep-UV spectral ranges. The metasurface is designed to support an angle-tunable plasmonic resonance in the near-infrared spectral range, with its resonance wavelength shifting as a function of the pump angle of incidence. Under resonant conditions, strong electromagnetic-field confinement near the nano-pillar corners enhances the nonlinear light-matter interactions. Compared with a flat gold mirror, the nanostructured metal exhibits enhanced nonlinear frequency conversion, with conversion efficiencies of second and third-harmonic generation increased by two and three orders of magnitude, respectively. Full-wave theoretical modeling of linear and nonlinear light-matter interaction in nanoscale structures validates the experimental results. We demonstrate that periodically arranged square gold pillars can be engineered to achieve enhanced second and third-harmonic generation at wavelengths that can be tailored through the structural design, without requiring complex geometries or hybrid structures that pose fabrication challenges. The close agreement between theoretical predictions and experimental measurements further advances our understanding of surface-mediated nonlinear optical processes in metals and highlights the potential of metallic nanostructures as ultrafast light sources in the UV and deep-UV.

</details>


### [175] [Pseudovortex-assisted generation of first-order vector modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17043)
*L. Marques Fagundes,P. Schneider Lacerda,T. A. Petry Kock,N. Rubiano da Silva,P. H. Souto Ribeiro,R. Medeiros de Araújo*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 用无拓扑电荷的"伪涡旋"光束替代高斯输入，克服涡旋波片产生一阶矢量模的保真度上限，实验达0.921。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡旋波片是产生径向/角向偏振等一阶矢量模的实用手段，但入射高斯模与矢量模所需的环形（doughnut）光强分布不匹配，会激发寄生高阶模，从而限制生成模式的保真度。

Method: 理论结合实验：将输入高斯光束替换为"伪涡旋光束"（环形光强但拓扑电荷为零的模），用空间光调制器（SLM）产生该输入模；采用几何相位补偿的三角形腔进行模式分析；并研究保真度随输入束腰的变化。

Result: 矢量模保真度达0.921 ± 0.013，明显优于高斯输入的0.815 ± 0.003；理论上给出保真度上界8π/27 ≈ 0.931，与实验结果吻合良好。

Conclusion: 以伪涡旋光束作为输入可突破高斯输入下的保真度限制，为高保真矢量模的产生提供了一种可靠方法。

Abstract: A practical method of generating first-order vector modes like radially and azimuthally polarized modes is using the so-called vortex waveplates. Shining these plates with Gaussian beams produces parasitic higher-order modes besides the target vector beam. The reason is that there is a mismatch between the incident Gaussian mode and the doughnut-shaped profile required for the vector beam. This imposes a limit on the fidelity of the generated mode with respect to the target vector beam. In this work we demonstrate theoretically and experimentally that this limit can be overcome if the input Gaussian beam is replaced with a pseudovortex beam, which is a doughnut-shaped mode without topological charge. We use a spatial light modulator to generate this input mode and a geometric-phase-compensated triangular cavity for mode analysis. We demonstrate vector mode fidelity as high as $0.921 \pm 0.013$, which is considerably better than $0.815 \pm 0.003$ obtained with Gaussian input. We also investigate the fidelity as a function of the input beam waist, leading to a theoretical upper bound for the fidelity of $8π/27 \approx 0.931$, in close agreement with the experimental results. This approach presents a reliable method of generating high-fidelity vector modes.

</details>
